### [1] [Fully Symbolic Analysis of Loop Locality: Using Imaginary Reuse to Infer Real Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10196)
*Yifan Zhu,Yekai Pan,Chen Ding,Yanghui Wu*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 提出基于符号多项式的局部性理论及编译器支持，可推导缓存性能的二次和倒数表达式，比经验缩放规则更通用精确


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有经验缩放规则在预测缓存性能时不够通用和精确，需要一种更系统、符号化的理论来准确分析和预测程序局部性

Method: 开发完全符号化的局部性理论，将局部性表示为多项式；编译器分析支持仿射循环嵌套，推导缓存性能的二次和倒数表达式

Result: 在41个科学计算内核和张量操作基准测试中，编译器平均41秒推导局部性多项式；预测缓存缺失数小于1毫秒；数据移动预测准确率达99.6%

Conclusion: 提出的符号化局部性理论比经验缩放规则更通用精确，能高效准确地预测缓存性能，为编译器优化提供理论基础

Abstract: This paper presents a new theory of locality and its compiler support. The theory is fully symbolic and derives locality as polynomials, and the compiler analysis supports affine loop nests. They derive cache-performance scaling in quadratic and reciprocal expressions and are more general and precise than empirical scaling rules.
  Evaluated on a benchmark suite of 41 scientific kernels and tensor operations, the compiler requires an average of 41 seconds to derive the locality polynomials. After derivation, predicting the cache miss count for any given input size and cache configuration takes less than a millisecond. Across all tests--with and without loop fusion--the accuracy in the data movement prediction is 99.6\%, compared to simulated set-associative L1 data cache.

</details>

### [2] [Early-Stage Cancer Biomarker Detection via Intravascular Nanomachines: Modeling and Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10709)
*Abdollah Rezagholi,Sergi Abadal,Filip Lemic,Eduard Alarcon,Ethungshan Shitiri*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于体内纳米通信的早期癌症检测方法，通过计算模拟评估了血管环境中纳米机器检测生物标志物的性能，特别考虑了非均匀血流、尺寸依赖性粒子迁移和红细胞相互作用等现实血管特征的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期癌症检测对改善患者预后至关重要。现有建模方法往往忽略关键血管特征，如非均匀血流结构、尺寸依赖性粒子迁移性以及红细胞相互作用驱动的粒子边缘化，这些因素会影响纳米机器检测生物标志物的效率。

Method: 采用计算模拟方法模拟血管环境，评估不同生理条件下生物标志物检测性能。研究将非均匀血流结构、尺寸依赖性粒子迁移和红细胞相互作用驱动的粒子边缘化等因素纳入模拟框架，量化这些因素对检测效率的单独和综合影响。

Result: 在均匀血流假设下获得基线检测性能后，引入现实血管传输机制会逐步降低所有血管类型和纳米机器尺寸的检测概率。在所有考虑的血管中，毛细血管在所有纳米机器尺寸下始终获得最高的检测概率。

Conclusion: 研究证明了考虑现实血管特征对准确评估纳米机器检测性能的重要性。毛细血管环境为纳米机器检测生物标志物提供了最有利的条件，这为优化基于纳米通信的早期癌症检测方法提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Early detection of cancer is essential for timely diagnosis and improved patient outcomes. Among emerging technologies, intra-body nanoscale communication offers an innovative solution to identify molecular cues within the human bloodstream. This study investigates a minimally invasive approach for early-stage cancer biomarker detection using nanomachines introduced into the bloodstream. To assess the feasibility of this approach, computational simulations are used to emulate the vascular environment and evaluate biomarker detection performance under different physiological conditions. Current modeling approaches often fail to capture essential vascular characteristics, including non-uniform flow structures, size-dependent particle mobility, and particle margination driven by red blood cell interactions. To address these limitations, our study incorporates these factors into the simulation framework and quantifies their individual and combined effects on biomarker detection efficiency. Baseline detection performance is first obtained under uniform flow assumptions, after which introducing realistic vascular transport mechanisms progressively reduces detection probability for all vessel types and nanomachine sizes. Among the considered vessels, capillary consistently achieves the highest detection probability across all nanomachine sizes.

</details>


### [3] [Reference Architecture of a Quantum-Centric Supercomputer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10970)
*Seetharami Seelam,Jerry M. Chow,Antonio Córcoles,Sarah Sheldon,Tushar Mittal,Abhinav Kandala,Sean Dague,Ian Hincks,Hiroshi Horii,Blake Johnson,Michael Le,Hani Jamjoom,Jay M. Gambetta*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文提出了量子中心超级计算（QCSC）系统的参考架构和路线图，旨在整合量子处理器（QPU）、图形处理器（GPU）和中央处理器（CPU），通过三个发展阶段解决当前量子计算与经典高性能计算系统分离的问题，加速量子计算在应用研究中的采用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子计算机和经典高性能计算（HPC）系统各自独立运行，用户需要手动编排工作负载、协调作业调度以及在系统间传输数据，这一繁琐过程严重阻碍了生产力和算法探索效率。这促使需要构建灵活且高性能的量子中心超级计算系统，以加速量子计算在解决关键计算问题中的应用。

Method: 提出量子中心超级计算（QCSC）系统的参考架构，通过三个发展阶段实现：1）量子系统作为现有HPC综合体中的专用计算卸载引擎；2）通过高级中间件耦合的异构量子与经典HPC系统，实现混合量子-经典算法的无缝执行；3）为混合计算工作流程完全协同设计的异构量子-HPC系统。

Result: 提出了一个系统化的QCSC发展路线图，包括详细的架构设计和三个阶段的发展路径，为整合量子与经典计算资源提供了清晰的框架，旨在解决当前系统分离带来的挑战。

Conclusion: 量子中心超级计算系统通过整合量子与经典计算资源，并经过三个阶段的协同设计发展，将加速量子计算在化学、材料等应用领域的采用，解决当前系统分离带来的效率瓶颈，推动量子算法探索和应用研究的发展。

Abstract: Quantum computers have demonstrated utility in simulating quantum systems beyond brute-force classical approaches. As the community builds on these demonstrations to explore using quantum computing for applied research, algorithms and workflows have emerged that require leveraging both quantum computers and classical high-performance computing (HPC) systems to scale applications, especially in chemistry and materials, beyond what either system can simulate alone. Today, these disparate systems operate in isolation, forcing users to manually orchestrate workloads, coordinate job scheduling, and transfer data between systems -- a cumbersome process that hinders productivity and severely limits rapid algorithmic exploration. These challenges motivate the need for flexible and high-performance Quantum-Centric Supercomputing (QCSC) systems that integrate Quantum Processing Units (QPUs), Graphics Processing Units (GPUs), and Central Processing Units (CPUs) to accelerate discovery of such algorithms across applications. These systems will be co-designed across quantum and classical HPC infrastructure, middleware, and application layers to accelerate the adoption of quantum computing for solving critical computational problems. We envision QCSC evolution through three distinct phases: (1) quantum systems as specialized compute offload engines within existing HPC complexes; (2) heterogeneous quantum and classical HPC systems coupled through advanced middleware, enabling seamless execution of hybrid quantum-classical algorithms; and (3) fully co-designed heterogeneous quantum-HPC systems for hybrid computational workflows. This article presents a reference architecture and roadmap for these QCSC systems.

</details>


### [4] [Report for NSF Workshop on Algorithm-Hardware Co-design for Medical Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10976)
*Peipei Zhou,Zheng Dong,Insup Lee,Aidong Zhang,Robert Dick,Majid Sarrafzadeh,Xiaodong Wu,Weisong Shi,Zhuoping Yang,Jingtong Hu,Yiyu Shi*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: NSF研讨会报告总结了算法-硬件协同设计在医疗应用中的挑战与路线图，涵盖远程医疗、可穿戴设备、家庭ICU和医学成像四大领域，呼吁投资标准化基础设施并建立验证生态系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前医疗技术发展面临算法与硬件分离的挑战，需要跨学科协作来推动下一代医疗技术的创新。研讨会旨在解决医疗计算中算法-硬件协同设计的基础性问题，为医疗应用开发战略路线图。

Method: 通过NSF组织的跨学科研讨会，汇集研究人员、临床医生和行业领袖，围绕四个主题领域进行讨论：远程医疗与手术操作、可穿戴与植入式医疗、家庭ICU与老年护理、医学传感与成像重建。

Result: 研讨会提出了医疗技术发展的根本性转变建议：投资标准化数据与计算基础设施，开发临床工作流感知系统与人机协作框架，建立基于客观连续测量的可扩展验证生态系统，构建安全可靠平台以降低转化风险。

Conclusion: 报告呼吁从根本上改变下一代医疗技术的构思、设计、验证和实施方式，强调算法-硬件协同设计对医疗应用的重要性，并提出了具体的投资和发展建议以推动该领域进步。

Abstract: This report summarizes the discussions and recommendations from the NSF Workshop on Algorithm-Hardware Co-design for Medical Applications, held on September 26-27, 2024, in Pittsburgh, PA. The workshop assembled an interdisciplinary cohort of researchers, clinicians, and industry leaders to examine foundational challenges and develop a strategic roadmap for algorithm-hardware co-design in medical computing. The workshop focuses on four thematic areas: (1) teleoperations, telehealth, and surgical operations; (2) wearable and implantable medicine, including implantable living pharmacies; (3) home ICU, hospital systems, and elderly care; and (4) medical sensing, imaging, and reconstruction. This report calls for a fundamental shift in how next-generation medical technologies are conceived, designed, validated, and translated into practice. The report recommends that NSF sustain investment in shared standardized data infrastructures and compute infrastructures, develop clinic workflow-aware systems and human-AI collaboration frameworks, promote scalable validation ecosystems grounded in objective, continuous measures, and physics-informed, and enable safe, accountable, and resilient platforms, including virtual-physical healthcare ecosystems, to de-risk translational pathways. The workshop information can be found on the website: https://sites.google.com/view/nsfworkshop.

</details>

### [5] [Where Do Flow Semantics Reside? A Protocol-Native Tabular Pretraining Paradigm for Encrypted Traffic Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10051)
*Sizhe Huang,Shujie Yang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 论文提出FlowSem-MAE，一种基于协议原生范式的表格掩码自编码器，通过Flow Semantic Units解决传统字节序列掩码建模在加密流量分类中的语义不匹配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督掩码建模方法将流量扁平化为字节序列，破坏了协议定义的语义结构，导致三个具体问题：1) 字段不可预测性（如随机字段被错误作为重建目标）；2) 嵌入混淆（语义不同字段被压缩到统一嵌入空间）；3) 元数据丢失（时间分析必需的时间戳信息被丢弃）。这些导致预训练后仍需大量标注数据。

Method: 提出协议原生范式，将协议定义的字段语义作为架构先验，将任务重新表述为与数据内在表格模态对齐。具体实现FlowSem-MAE：基于Flow Semantic Units的表格掩码自编码器，包含可预测性引导过滤（专注于可学习的FSU）、FSU特定嵌入（保持字段边界）、双轴注意力（捕获包内和时序模式）。

Result: FlowSem-MAE在多个数据集上显著优于现有最先进方法。仅使用一半标注数据时，其性能已超过大多数现有方法使用全部数据训练的结果。

Conclusion: 协议原生范式通过尊重协议定义的语义结构，解决了传统序列掩码建模在加密流量分类中的根本问题。FlowSem-MAE展示了将流量视为表格而非序列的有效性，为自监督加密流量分析提供了新方向。

Abstract: Self-supervised masked modeling shows promise for encrypted traffic classification by masking and reconstructing raw bytes. Yet recent work reveals these methods fail to reduce reliance on labeled data despite costly pretraining: under frozen encoder evaluation, accuracy drops from greater than 0.9 to less than 0.47. We argue the root cause is inductive bias mismatch: flattening traffic into byte sequences destroys protocol-defined semantics. We identify three specific issues: 1) field unpredictability, random fields like ip.id are unlearnable yet treated as reconstruction targets; 2) embedding confusion, semantically distinct fields collapse into a unified embedding space; 3) metadata loss, capture-time metadata essential for temporal analysis is discarded. To address this, we propose a protocol-native paradigm that treats protocol-defined field semantics as architectural priors, reformulating the task to align with the data's intrinsic tabular modality rather than incrementally adapting sequence-based architectures. Instantiating this paradigm, we introduce FlowSem-MAE, a tabular masked autoencoder built on Flow Semantic Units (FSUs). It features predictability-guided filtering that focuses on learnable FSUs, FSU-specific embeddings to preserve field boundaries, and dual-axis attention to capture intra-packet and temporal patterns. FlowSem-MAE significantly outperforms state-of-the-art across datasets. With only half labeled data, it outperforms most existing methods trained on full data.

</details>


### [6] [Expressive Boundedness of Authoritative DNS Response Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10897)
*Chris Bertinato*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 该论文形式化分析了权威DNS响应选择机制，证明其语义受DNS协议约束限制，具有有限表达能力和代数结构，为跨系统比较提供了统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 权威DNS响应选择（常称为流量引导）在实践中广泛部署但缺乏独立于具体配置语言或实现的形式化语义定义。现有描述多为非正式，需要基于DNS协议约束建立统一的理论框架。

Method: 将权威DNS响应选择形式化为DNS可容许函数类，证明所有此类函数都允许有限范式：基于可观测上下文的条件限制后从有限候选集中选择。分析DNS语义诱导的内在代数结构，支持组合性、表达能力损失和语义坍缩的原则性推理。

Result: 证明了权威DNS响应选择占据由DNS协议约束直接确定的有限语义域，包括响应有限性、RRset原子性、终止性、可缓存性和限于解析器可见输入等要求。建立了统一框架，将具体权威系统、配置模型和序列化编码建模为该域的语义限制。

Conclusion: 该框架支持基于协议语义（而非实现细节）对异构权威DNS系统进行精确推理，包括等价性、可表示性和近似性分析。形式化方法揭示了DNS响应选择的内在限制和代数特性，为系统设计和分析提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Authoritative Domain Name System (DNS) response selection defines query-time response selection based on resolver-visible context and per-answer metadata, yielding different observable outcomes for the same query under different conditions. Although such behavior is widely deployed and often described informally as traffic steering, its semantics have not been formalized independently of particular configuration languages or implementations.
  This paper shows that authoritative DNS response selection inhabits a bounded semantic domain determined directly by DNS protocol constraints. Requirements such as finiteness of responses, RRset atomicity, termination, cacheability, and restriction to resolver-visible inputs jointly limit the expressive power of any query-time selection mechanism. We formalize authoritative response selection as a class of DNS-admissible functions and prove that every such function admits a finite normal form consisting of conditional restriction over observable context followed by selection among a finite candidate set.
  We further show that this bounded semantic domain carries intrinsic algebraic structure induced by DNS semantics, enabling principled reasoning about composition, expressiveness loss, and semantic collapse. Concrete authoritative systems, configuration models, and serialized encodings are modeled uniformly as semantic restrictions of this domain. This framework supports precise reasoning about equivalence, representability, and approximation across heterogeneous authoritative DNS systems, grounded directly in protocol semantics rather than implementation detail.

</details>


### [7] [Measurement-Driven O-RAN Diagnostics with Tail Latency and Scheduler Indicators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11023)
*Theofanis P. Raptis,Weronika Maria Bachan,Roberto Verdone*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 该研究通过真实测量分析O-RAN实例的跨层性能诊断，结合应用层延迟和无线层行为，重点关注尾部延迟特性及其与调度器、链路适配指标的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献多关注吞吐量、仿真设置或单层KPI，缺乏对真实O-RAN环境中跨层性能诊断的测量驱动研究，特别是尾部延迟与无线层行为的关联分析。

Method: 在真实测量活动中，使用两种代表性UE配置（商用智能手机和基于调制解调器的设备），在多个链路距离（2、6、11米）下进行测量，包括静态条件和受控动态遮挡场景。采用尾部聚焦延迟表征方法（如95百分位数和超限概率），并将其与调度器和链路适配指标（如块错误行为、调制/编码选择和信号质量）相关联。

Result: 研究结果显示：(i) UE依赖的差异主要体现在延迟尾部；(ii) 尾部延迟随距离和有效载荷的系统性缩放；(iii) 即使端到端延迟看似稳定时，无线层动态仍可被检测到。这些发现证明了跨层证据的必要性。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了用于可解释O-RAN诊断的测量驱动方法，并提出了轻量级、基于窗口的"退化标志"，结合尾部延迟和无线指标来支持实际监控和故障排除，区别于现有文献的常见方法。

Abstract: We investigate cross-layer performance diagnostics for an O-RAN instance by jointly analyzing application-level latency and radio-layer behavior from a real measurement campaign. Measurements were conducted at multiple link distances (2, 6 and 11 meters) using two representative UE configurations (a commercial smartphone and a modem-based device), under both static conditions and a controlled dynamic obstruction scenario. Rather than relying on averages, the study adopts tail-focused latency characterization (e.g., 95th percentile and exceedance probabilities) and connects it to scheduler- and link-adaptation indicators (e.g., block error behavior, modulation/coding selection and signal quality). The results reveal (i) UE-dependent differences that primarily manifest in the latency tail, (ii) systematic scaling of tail latency with distance and payload and (iii) cases where radio-layer dynamics are detectable even when end-to-end latency appears stable, motivating the need for cross-layer evidence. Distinct from much of the existing literature (often centered on throughput, simulated setups, or single-layer KPIs) this work contributes a measurement-driven methodology for interpretable O-RAN diagnostics and proposes lightweight, window-based "degradation flags" that combine tail latency and radio indicators to support practical monitoring and troubleshooting.

</details>

### [8] [One Model, Many Skills: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning for Multitask Code Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09978)
*Amal Akli,Maxime Cordy,Mike Papadakis,Yves Le Traon*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文首次系统评估了参数高效微调在多任务代码分析中的应用，发现共享PEFT模块可媲美全参数微调，在性能与效率间取得良好平衡，同时揭示了任务分组对多任务学习效果的关键影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然大语言模型在代码生成任务上表现出色，但在其他代码分析任务上的有效性尚不明确。同时，多任务学习能统一不同目标，但全参数微调计算成本过高。参数高效微调可降低成本，但其在多任务学习中的潜力尚未被系统探索。

Method: 首次对代码分析的多任务PEFT进行全面评估，比较多种方法、任务和模型架构。通过任务配对实验分析影响结果的关键因素，并将高效多任务PEFT与开源通用LLM的直接提示进行基准测试。

Result: 共享PEFT模块可匹配甚至超越全参数多任务微调；多任务PEFT在保持接近单任务微调准确率的同时，将可训练参数减少为任务数的倒数，计算成本降低高达85%。任务稳定性、模型架构、任务互补性、不对称性和数据集质量是决定协同微调成功的关键因素。

Conclusion: PEFT的优势可扩展到多任务场景，多任务PEFT在代码分析任务上提供了优越的性能-效率权衡。尽管开源通用LLM在代码生成上表现强劲，但在分析任务上表现不佳，即使是1B参数模型配合多任务PEFT也能显著超越它们。

Abstract: Large language models have recently surpassed specialized systems on code generation, yet their effectiveness on other code-analysis tasks remains less clear. At the same time, multi-task learning offers a way to unify diverse objectives within a single model, but fully fine-tuning LLMs across tasks is computationally prohibitive. Parameter-efficient fine-tuning mitigates this cost by updating only a small fraction of weights. Although PEFT has proven effective in single-task settings, its potential for multi-task learning has not yet been systematically explored. We present the first comprehensive evaluation of multi-task PEFT for code analysis, comparing several methods across diverse tasks and model architectures. Our experiments show that a single PEFT module shared across tasks can match, and in some cases surpass, full multi-task fine-tuning, confirming that the benefits of PEFT extend beyond isolated tasks. When comparing single-task and multi-task setups, we find that multi-task PEFT achieves a favorable performance-efficiency trade-off: it delivers accuracy close to single-task fine-tuning while reducing storage requirements, cutting the number of trainable parameters by a factor of the task count, and lowering computation costs by as much as 85%. At the same time, multi-task gains remain sensitive to task grouping. Through task-pairing experiments, we identify key factors shaping outcomes: task stability, model architecture, task complementarity, asymmetry, and dataset quality determine the success of co-fine-tuning. Finally, we benchmark efficient multi-task PEFT against direct prompting of open-source general-purpose LLMs, including DeepSeek, Qwen, Mistral, CodeLlama, and StarCoder. Despite their strong performance in code generation, these models underperform on analysis tasks, where even a 1B-parameter model with multi-task PEFT achieves significantly better results.

</details>


### [9] [MALTA: Maintenance-Aware Technical Lag, Estimation to Address Software Abandonment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10265)
*Shane K. Panter,Nasir U. Eisty*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: MALTA框架通过结合开发活动、维护者响应性和仓库元数据来识别被传统版本滞后指标忽略的已废弃软件包，显著提高了对技术滞后风险的评估准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源生态系统依赖持续的软件包维护。当维护减缓或停止时，技术滞后（安装版本与最新版本之间的差距）会累积安全性和可持续性风险。现有版本滞后指标难以区分活跃维护和已废弃的软件包，导致系统性低估风险。

Method: 提出维护感知滞后和技术废弃（MALTA）评分框架，包含三个指标：开发活动评分（DAS）、维护者响应性评分（MRS）和仓库元数据可行性评分（RMVS）。在11,047个Debian软件包（链接到上游GitHub仓库）的数据集上进行评估，涵盖170万次提交和420万次拉取请求。

Result: MALTA在分类活跃维护与衰退维护时达到AUC=0.783。最重要的是，62.2%仅通过版本滞后被分类为"低风险"的软件包，在加入MALTA信号后被重新分类为"高风险"。这些不一致的软件包平均距离上次提交已有2019天，其中9.8%的仓库已归档。

Conclusion: 版本滞后指标系统性遗漏已废弃软件包，这是影响分发生态系统中大多数依赖项的盲点。MALTA通过区分可解决滞后与上游废弃导致的终端滞后，识别出对版本滞后不可见的大量不一致群体。

Abstract: Context: Open-source ecosystems rely on sustained package maintenance. When maintenance slows or stops, Technical Lag (TL), the gap between installed and latest dependency versions accumulates, creating security and sustainability risks. However, some existing TL metrics, such as Version Lag, struggle to distinguish between actively maintained and abandoned packages, leading to a systematic underestimation of risk. Objective: We investigate the relationship between Version Lag and software abandonment by (i) identifying which repository-level signals reliably distinguish sustained maintenance from long-term decline, (ii) quantifying how Version Lag magnitude and persistence differ across maintenance states, and (iii) evaluating how maintenance-aware metrics change the identification of high-risk dependencies. Method: We introduce Maintenance-Aware Lag and Technical Abandonment (MALTA), a scoring framework comprising three metrics: Development Activity Score (DAS), Maintainer Responsiveness Score (MRS), and Repository Metadata Viability Score (RMVS). We evaluate MALTA on a dataset of 11,047 Debian packages linked to upstream GitHub repositories, encompassing 1.7 million commits and 4.2 million pull requests. Results: MALTA achieves AUC = 0.783 for classifying active versus declining maintenance. Most significantly, 62.2% of packages classified as "Low Risk" by Version Lag alone are reclassified as "High Risk" when MALTA signals are incorporated. These discordant packages average 2019 days since their last commit, with 9.8% having archived repositories. Conclusions: Version Lag metrics systematically miss abandoned packages, a blind spot affecting the majority of dependencies in distribution ecosystems. MALTA identifies a substantial discordant population invisible to Version Lag by distinguishing resolvable lag from terminal lag caused by upstream abandonment.

</details>


### [10] [From Verification to Herding: Exploiting Software's Sparsity of Influence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10478)
*Tim Menzies,Kishan Kumar Ganguly*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出从传统软件验证转向"herding"方法，利用"影响稀疏性"原理，通过EZR算法仅需少量样本即可实现高效系统引导


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统软件验证成本高昂，占用超过一半项目工作量，且无法处理现代复杂系统。需要一种更高效的方法来替代传统的验证和建模方法。

Method: 提出"herding"概念，将测试视为无模型搜索任务，引导系统朝向目标。利用"影响稀疏性"原理（大型软件状态空间通常仅由少数变量主导），引入EZR（高效零知识排序器）随机学习器，直接寻找这些控制器变量。

Result: 在数十个任务中，EZR仅用32个样本就达到了峰值结果的90%，用轻量采样替代了重型求解器。

Conclusion: 通过从验证建模转向herding方法，利用影响稀疏性和EZR算法，可以显著降低软件验证成本，提高复杂系统测试效率。

Abstract: Software verification is now costly, taking over half the project effort while failing on modern complex systems. We hence propose a shift from verification and modeling to herding: treating testing as a model-free search task that steers systems toward target goals. This exploits the "Sparsity of Influence" -the fact that, often, large software state spaces are ruled by just a few variables, We introduce EZR (Efficient Zero-knowledge Ranker), a stochastic learner that finds these controllers directly. Across dozens of tasks, EZR achieved 90% of peak results with only 32 samples, replacing heavy solvers with light sampling.

</details>


### [11] [ESG Reporting Lifecycle Management with Large Language Models and AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10646)
*Thong Hoang,Mykhailo Klymenko,Xiwei Xu,Shidong Pan,Yi Ding,Xushuo Tang,Zhengyi Yang,Jieke Shi,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出一个基于多智能体系统的ESG生命周期框架，将ESG从静态报告转变为动态、可问责、自适应的可持续发展治理系统


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前ESG报告面临非结构化数据格式、术语不一致和复杂要求等挑战，现有ESG生命周期缺乏自动化、适应性和持续反馈机制，需要新的解决方案

Method: 引入智能体化的ESG生命周期框架，系统整合识别、测量、报告、参与和改进五个阶段，通过多个AI智能体提取ESG信息、验证ESG绩效并基于组织成果更新报告

Result: 定义了支持报告验证、多报告比较、报告生成和知识库维护四大ESG任务的技术要求和质量属性，提出了单模型、单智能体和多智能体三种架构方法

Conclusion: 该框架将ESG从静态报告流程转变为动态、可问责、自适应的可持续发展治理系统，原型代码和数据已开源供同行评审

Abstract: Environmental, Social, and Governance (ESG) standards have been increasingly adopted by organizations to demonstrate accountability towards ethical, social, and sustainability goals. However, generating ESG reports that align with these standards remains challenging due to unstructured data formats, inconsistent terminology, and complex requirements. Existing ESG lifecycles provide guidance for structuring ESG reports but lack the automation, adaptability, and continuous feedback mechanisms needed to address these challenges. To bridge this gap, we introduce an agentic ESG lifecycle framework that systematically integrates the ESG stages of identification, measurement, reporting, engagement, and improvement. In this framework, multiple AI agents extract ESG information, verify ESG performance, and update ESG reports based on organisational outcomes. By embedding agentic components within the ESG lifecycle, the proposed framework transforms ESG from a static reporting process into a dynamic, accountable, and adaptive system for sustainability governance. We further define the technical requirements and quality attributes needed to support four main ESG tasks, such as report validation, multi-report comparison, report generation, and knowledge-base maintenance, and propose three architectural approaches, namely single-model, single-agent, and multi-agent, for addressing these tasks. The source code and data for the prototype of these approaches are available at https://gitlab.com/for_peer_review-group/esg_assistant.

</details>


### [12] [From Education to Evidence: A Collaborative Practice Research Platform for AI-Integrated Agile Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10679)
*Tobias Geger,Andreas Rausch,Ina Schiering,Frauke Stenzel,Stefan Wittek*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出基于项目的AI集成敏捷教育平台作为协作研究环境，解决敏捷开发研究时效性和可转移性问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 敏捷软件开发发展迅速，研究难以保持时效性和可转移性，特别是生成式AI和智能体工具的快速采用加剧了这一问题。现有研究存在理论和时间差距，导致结果往往缺乏明确的重用条件或对实际决策来说为时已晚。

Method: 引入基于项目的AI集成敏捷教育平台作为协作研究环境，位于受控研究和真实产业之间。平台通过冲刺节奏、质量门和真实利益相关者参与实现快速探究。提出了指定迭代结构、重复事件和AI辅助工程制品质量门的框架。

Result: 来自多个学期的早期结果显示：项目管道、队列增长和利益相关者参与方面，平台具有高效生成实践相关证据并保持可重用上下文的潜力。

Conclusion: 该平台为解决敏捷开发研究的时效性和可转移性问题提供了有效途径，未来将增强治理和证据捕获能力。

Abstract: Agile software development evolves so rapidly that research struggles to remain timely and transferable - an issue heightened by the swift adoption of generative AI and agentic tools. Earlier discussions highlight theory and time gaps, leading to results that often lack clear reuse conditions or arrive too late for practical decisions. This paper introduces a project-based, AI-integrated agile education platform as a collaborative research environment, positioned between controlled studies and real-world industry. The platform enables rapid inquiry through sprint rhythms, quality gates, and genuine stakeholder involvement. We present a framework specifying iteration structures, recurring events, and quality gates for AI-assisted engineering artifacts. Early results from several semesters - covering project pipeline, cohort growth, and stakeholder participation - show the platform's potential to generate practice-relevant evidence efficiently and with reusable context. Finally, we outline future steps to enhance governance and evidence capture.

</details>


### [13] [Packaging Jupyter notebooks as installable desktop apps using LabConstrictor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10704)
*Iván Hidalgo-Cenalmor,Marcela Xiomara Rivera Pineda,Bruno M. Saraiva,Ricardo Henriques,Guillaume Jacquemet*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LabConstrictor通过GitHub自动化流水线将Jupyter笔记本打包成可一键安装的桌面应用，解决生命科学开源软件部署难题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生命科学研究严重依赖开源学术软件，但许多工具因安装要求复杂、开发者资源有限等实际障碍而未被充分利用。Jupyter笔记本虽然流行，但存在环境可复现性问题，且分享后他人难以成功运行。

Method: LabConstrictor采用基于GitHub的CI/CD风格自动化流水线，无需DevOps专业知识即可检查环境并将笔记本打包成可一键安装的桌面应用程序。安装后用户可访问统一启动页面，包含文档、笔记本链接和版本检查功能。

Result: 通过简化开源软件的分发、安装和共享，LabConstrictor使计算方法的获取更快，促进了跨实验室的常规重用。隐藏代码单元格、结合运行控件和小部件提供了类似应用的体验。

Conclusion: LabConstrictor填补了学术软件部署的空白，通过自动化打包流程降低了使用门槛，使研究人员能够更便捷地访问和使用开源计算工具。

Abstract: Life sciences research depends heavily on open-source academic software, yet many tools remain underused due to practical barriers. These include installation requirements that hinder adoption and limited developer resources for software distribution and long-term maintenance. Jupyter notebooks are popular because they combine code, documentation, and results into a single executable document, enabling quick method development. However, notebooks are often fragile due to reproducibility issues in coding environments, and sharing them, especially for local execution, does not ensure others can run them successfully. LabConstrictor closes this deployment gap by bringing CI/CD-style automation to academic developers without needing DevOps expertise. Its GitHub-based pipeline checks environments and packages notebooks into one-click installable desktop applications. After installation, users access a unified start page with documentation, links to the packaged notebooks, and version checks. Code cells can be hidden by default, and run-cell controls combined with widgets provide an app-like experience. By simplifying the distribution, installation, and sharing of open-source software, LabConstrictor allows faster access to new computational methods and promotes routine reuse across labs.

</details>


### [14] [Exploring Indicators of Developers' Sentiment Perceptions in Student Software Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10864)
*Martin Obaidi,Marc Herrmann,Jendrik Martensen,Jil Klünder,Kurt Schneider*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究开发者情绪特质、状态、项目阶段等因素如何影响软件项目中文本消息的情感感知，发现情感感知在个体内仅中等稳定，受情绪特质影响较大，但整体信号较弱，强调情感分析输出需谨慎解读。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件项目中沟通至关重要，积极或消极的陈述会影响团队协作和情绪传染。开发者如何感知书面消息的情感（积极、消极或中性）可能受多种因素影响，但缺乏实证研究。本研究旨在探究情绪特质与状态、生活状况、项目阶段和群体动态等因素如何影响软件开发中文本消息的情感感知。

Method: 采用四轮调查研究的实验设计，涉及81名参与团队软件项目的学生。每轮调查中，参与者报告情绪特质、状态、生活状况等项目因素，并对30条去上下文的陈述进行情感标注（积极、消极或中性），同时提供标注理由和不确定性的元数据。使用广义估计方程（GEE）进行重复测量建模分析。

Result: 主要发现：(1) 个体内部的情感感知仅中等稳定，标签变化集中在易产生歧义的陈述上；(2) 相关性信号较小，经多重检验校正后不显著；(3) 在陈述级重复测量模型中，较高的情绪特质和反应性与更积极（更少中性）的标注相关，而消极标注的预测因子较弱（如任务冲突）；(4) 未发现明显的系统性项目阶段效应。总体而言，情感感知在个体内存在差异，且强烈依赖于具体陈述。

Conclusion: 尽管研究在学术环境中进行，但观察到的变异性和歧义效应表明，在解释情感分析输出时需要谨慎。研究结果支持未来开展更多情境化、项目内沟通的研究，以更好地理解软件开发中情感感知的动态机制。

Abstract: Communication is a crucial social factor in the success of software projects, as positively or negatively perceived statements can influence how recipients feel and affect team collaboration through emotional contagion. Whether a developer perceives a written message as positive, negative, or neutral is likely shaped by multiple factors.
  In this paper, we investigate how mood traits and states, life circumstances, project phases, and group dynamics relate to the perception of text-based messages in software development. We conducted a four-round survey study with 81 students in team-based software projects. Across rounds, participants reported these factors and labeled 30 decontextualized statements for sentiment, including meta-data on labeling rationale and uncertainty.
  Our results show: (1) Sentiment perception is only moderately stable within individuals, and label changes concentrate on ambiguity-prone statements; (2) Correlation-level signals are small and do not survive global multiple-testing correction; (3) In statement-level repeated-measures models (GEE), higher mood trait and reactivity are associated with more positive (and less neutral) labeling, while predictors of negative labeling are weaker and at most trend-level (e.g., task conflict); (4) We find no clear evidence of systematic project-phase effects. Overall, sentiment perception varies within persons and is strongly statement-dependent.
  Although our study was conducted in an academic setting, the observed variability and ambiguity effects suggest caution when interpreting sentiment analysis outputs and motivate future work with contextualized, in-project communication.

</details>


### [15] [Artificial Intelligence as a Catalyst for Innovation in Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10994)
*Carlos Alberto Fernández-y-Fernández,Jorge R. Aguilar-Cisneros*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文探讨AI如何作为催化剂增强软件工程中的敏捷性，通过文献综述和实证调查分析AI工具在敏捷开发中的感知、采用和影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代软件需求的快速演变和复杂性要求高度灵活的开发方法。尽管敏捷框架已成为行业标准，但团队在管理不断变化的需求和保持产品质量方面仍面临持续挑战。本文旨在探索AI如何作为增强敏捷性和促进创新的催化剂。

Method: 结合现有文献的全面综述和实证研究，通过针对软件工程专业人士的调查问卷，评估AI驱动工具的感知、采用和影响。

Result: 关键发现表明，AI集成（特别是通过机器学习和自然语言处理）促进了从需求管理到代码生成和测试等繁琐任务的自动化。AI不仅优化了当前的敏捷实践，还引入了维持软件开发质量、速度和创新所需的新能力。

Conclusion: AI作为强大的催化剂，能够增强软件工程中的敏捷性和创新性，为未来软件开发的可持续质量、速度和创新提供了关键能力。

Abstract: The rapid evolution and inherent complexity of modern software requirements demand highly flexible and responsive development methodologies. While Agile frameworks have become the industry standard for prioritizing iteration, collaboration, and adaptability, software development teams continue to face persistent challenges in managing constantly evolving requirements and maintaining product quality under tight deadlines. This article explores the intersection of Artificial Intelligence (AI) and Software Engineering (SE), to analyze how AI serves as a powerful catalyst for enhancing agility and fostering innovation. The research combines a comprehensive review of existing literature with an empirical study, utilizing a survey directed at Software Engineering professionals to assess the perception, adoption, and impact of AI-driven tools. Key findings reveal that the integration of AI (specifically through Machine Learning (ML) and Natural Language Processing (NLP) )facilitates the automation of tedious tasks, from requirement management to code generation and testing . This paper demonstrates that AI not only optimizes current Agile practices but also introduces new capabilities essential for sustaining quality, speed, and innovation in the future landscape of software development.

</details>

### [16] [Categorical Calculus and Algebra for Multi-Model Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10081)
*Jiaheng Lu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 该论文为范畴数据库查询提供了理论基础，通过扩展关系演算和关系代数提出了两种形式化查询语言，证明了它们的等价性，并提出了查询优化规则和复杂度分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模型数据库能够同时存储、管理和查询关系型、层次型和图数据等多种模型的数据，但缺乏统一的理论基础。该论文旨在为范畴数据库查询建立形式化的理论基础。

Method: 通过扩展关系演算和关系代数，提出了两种形式化查询语言：范畴演算和范畴代数。证明了这两种语言的等价性，并提出了一系列范畴代数的转换规则以支持查询优化。

Result: 建立了范畴演算和范畴代数之间的等价性，提出了查询优化规则，并分析了所提查询语言的表达能力和计算复杂度。

Conclusion: 该论文为多模型数据库中的范畴数据库查询提供了坚实的理论基础，包括形式化查询语言、等价性证明、优化规则和复杂度分析，为多模型数据库查询优化和实现提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Multi-model databases are designed to store, manage, and query data in various models, such as relational, hierarchical, and graph data, simultaneously.  In this paper, we provide a theoretical basis for querying categorical databases. We propose two formal query languages: categorical calculus and categorical algebra, by extending relational calculus and relational algebra respectively. We demonstrate the equivalence between these two languages of queries. We propose a series of transformation rules of categorical algebra to facilitate query optimization. Finally, we analyze the expressive power and computation complexity for the proposed query languages.

</details>


### [17] [K-Join: Combining Vertex Covers for Parallel Joins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10177)
*Simon Frisk,Austen Fan,Paraschos Koutris*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的并行连接算法，通过精心选择HyperCube份额（作为多个顶点覆盖的线性组合），实现了基于新超图理论度量"约化拟顶点覆盖"的最优负载特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模并行计算模型中，连接处理性能优化是一个重要研究方向，目标是实现机器间最小数据传输。然而，确定任意连接查询的最佳并行算法仍是一个开放问题。本文旨在推进这一方向的研究。

Method: 提出了一种基于数据分区和HyperCube原语的新算法。创新点在于通过多个顶点覆盖的线性组合来精心选择HyperCube份额，从而定义了一个新的超图理论度量"约化拟顶点覆盖"来表征算法性能。

Result: 算法负载特性为n/p^{1/κ}，其中κ是约化拟顶点覆盖。该度量匹配或改进了所有现有算法，并与描述单轮算法最坏情况最优负载的边拟填充表现出强相似性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的算法在并行连接处理方面取得了重要进展，通过引入新的超图理论度量，为确定最佳并行连接算法提供了新的理论框架和实用方法。

Abstract: Significant research effort has been devoted to improving the performance of join processing in the massively parallel computation model, where the goal is to evaluate a query with the minimum possible data transfer between machines. However, it is still an open question to determine the best possible parallel algorithm for any join query. In this paper, we present an algorithm that takes a step forward in this endeavour. Our new algorithm is simple and builds on two existing ideas: data partitioning and the HyperCube primitive. The novelty in our approach comes from a careful choice of the HyperCube shares, which is done as a linear combination of multiple vertex covers. The resulting load with input size $n$ and $p$ processors is characterized as $n/p^{1/κ}$, where $κ$ is a new hypergraph theoretic measure we call the reduced quasi vertex-cover. The new measure matches or improves on all state-of-the-art algorithms and exhibits strong similarities to the edge quasi-packing that describes the worst-case optimal load in one-round algorithms.

</details>


### [18] [HiFIVE: High-Fidelity Vector-Tile Reduction for Interactive Map Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10270)
*Tarlan Bahadori,Ahmed Eldawy*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: HiFIVE是一个用于可扩展、高保真客户端地理空间可视化的数据管理框架，通过两阶段解决方案减少瓦片大小同时保持视觉保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模空间数据可视化工具多依赖服务器端渲染，但用户更偏好客户端渲染以获得更好的可视化体验和快速样式调整。需要解决客户端渲染中瓦片大小与视觉保真度之间的权衡问题。

Method: 提出可视化感知的瓦片缩减问题，证明其NP难性。引入两阶段解决方案：分类和稀疏化，基于信息论和空间准则选择性地修剪记录、属性和值。

Result: 实验表明在TB级规模下，HiFIVE能显著减少瓦片大小，同时保持视觉保真度和交互性能。

Conclusion: HiFIVE框架成功解决了大规模地理空间数据客户端可视化中的瓦片大小与视觉保真度权衡问题，实现了可扩展的高保真可视化。

Abstract: Interactive visualization is a common tool for exploring large open-data repositories, where users quickly explore datasets across diverse domains. When it comes to large-scale spatial data, many existing tools rely on server-side rendering to produce small images that can be viewed at the client-side. However, most users prefer client-side rendering that allows quick styling of the data for better visualization experience. This paper presents HiFIVE, a data-management framework for scalable, high-fidelity client-side geospatial visualization. We formalize the visualization-aware tile reduction problem, which captures the trade-off between tile-size and visualization distortion, and prove its NP-hardness. HiFIVE introduces a two-stage solution combining triage and sparsification to selectively prune records, attributes, and values based on information-theoretic and spatial criteria. Experiments demonstrate substantial tile-size reductions while preserving visual fidelity and interactive performance at terabyte scale.

</details>


### [19] [A Hypergraph-Based Framework for Exploratory Business Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10625)
*Yunkai Lou,Shunyang Li,Longbin Lai,Jianke Yu,Wenyuan Yu,Ying Zhang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: ExBI系统通过超图数据模型和采样算法解决探索式BI的瓶颈，相比传统系统实现显著加速并保持高精度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索式BI需要迭代、多轮探索，但传统BI系统存在严重限制：过度依赖专家知识、计算成本高、静态模式、缺乏可重用性

Method: 提出ExBI系统，引入超图数据模型及Source、Join、View操作符，支持动态模式演进和物化视图重用；采用具有可证明估计保证的采样算法解决计算瓶颈

Result: 在LDBC数据集上，ExBI相比Neo4j平均加速16.21倍（最高146.25倍），相比MySQL平均加速46.67倍（最高230.53倍），COUNT查询平均误差率仅0.27%

Conclusion: ExBI通过超图数据模型和采样算法有效解决了探索式BI的关键瓶颈，实现了高效准确的大规模探索式BI工作流

Abstract: Business Intelligence (BI) analysis is evolving towards Exploratory BI, an iterative, multi-round exploration paradigm where analysts progressively refine their understanding. However, traditional BI systems impose critical limits for Exploratory BI: heavy reliance on expert knowledge, high computational costs, static schemas, and lack of reusability. We present ExBI, a novel system that introduces the hypergraph data model with operators, including Source, Join, and View, to enable dynamic schema evolution and materialized view reuse. Using sampling-based algorithms with provable estimation guarantees, ExBI addresses the computational bottlenecks, while maintaining analytical accuracy. Experiments on LDBC datasets demonstrate that ExBI achieves significant speedups over existing systems: on average 16.21x (up to 146.25x) compared to Neo4j and 46.67x (up to 230.53x) compared to MySQL, while maintaining high accuracy with an average error rate of only 0.27% for COUNT, enabling efficient and accurate large-scale exploratory BI workflows.

</details>


### [20] [EvoSchema: Towards Text-to-SQL Robustness Against Schema Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10697)
*Tianshu Zhang,Kun Qian,Siddhartha Sahai,Yuan Tian,Shaddy Garg,Huan Sun,Yunyao Li*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: EvoSchema是一个用于评估文本到SQL系统在数据库模式演化下鲁棒性的综合基准，包含10种扰动类型，揭示了表级扰动比列级扰动影响更大，并展示了在多样化模式上训练的模型具有更好的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到SQL模型在静态模式上训练，但现实中的数据库模式会不断演化，导致模型性能下降。现有研究要么主要关注NLQ、DB和SQL之间的语法或语义映射的简单改写，要么缺乏全面可控的方式来研究模式演化下的模型鲁棒性问题，这在面对现实世界中日益复杂丰富的数据库模式变化时显得不足。

Method: 提出了EvoSchema基准，引入了一个新颖的模式演化分类法，包含10种扰动类型，涵盖列级和表级修改，系统性地模拟数据库模式的动态特性。通过该基准对不同开源和闭源LLM进行了深入评估。

Result: 评估显示表级扰动对模型性能的影响显著大于列级变化。在EvoSchema的多样化模式设计上训练的模型能够迫使模型区分相同问题的模式差异，避免学习虚假模式，与在未扰动数据上训练的模型相比平均表现出显著的鲁棒性提升。

Conclusion: EvoSchema为模型行为提供了有价值的见解，并为设计能够在动态现实环境中蓬勃发展的系统指明了前进方向。该基准激发了更具弹性的文本到SQL系统的开发，无论是在模型训练还是数据库设计方面。

Abstract: Neural text-to-SQL models, which translate natural language questions (NLQs) into SQL queries given a database schema, have achieved remarkable performance. However, database schemas frequently evolve to meet new requirements. Such schema evolution often leads to performance degradation for models trained on static schemas. Existing work either mainly focuses on simply paraphrasing some syntactic or semantic mappings among NLQ, DB and SQL, or lacks a comprehensive and controllable way to investigate the model robustness issue under the schema evolution, which is insufficient when facing the increasingly complex and rich database schema changes in reality, especially in the LLM era. To address the challenges posed by schema evolution, we present EvoSchema, a comprehensive benchmark designed to assess and enhance the robustness of text-to-SQL systems under real-world schema changes. EvoSchema introduces a novel schema evolution taxonomy, encompassing ten perturbation types across columnlevel and table-level modifications, systematically simulating the dynamic nature of database schemas. Through EvoSchema, we conduct an in-depth evaluation spanning different open source and closed-source LLMs, revealing that table-level perturbations have a significantly greater impact on model performance compared to column-level changes. Furthermore, EvoSchema inspires the development of more resilient text-to-SQL systems, in terms of both model training and database design. The models trained on EvoSchema's diverse schema designs can force the model to distinguish the schema difference for the same questions to avoid learning spurious patterns, which demonstrate remarkable robustness compared to those trained on unperturbed data on average. This benchmark offers valuable insights into model behavior and a path forward for designing systems capable of thriving in dynamic, real-world environments.

</details>


### [21] [Pneuma-Seeker: A Relational Reification Mechanism to Align AI Agents with Human Work over Relational Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10747)
*Muhammad Imam Luthfi Balaka,John Hillesland,Kemal Badur,Raul Castro Fernandez*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: Pneuma-Seeker是一个基于关系具体化思想的系统，通过将用户信息需求表示为关系模式，迭代精炼并构建可执行程序来解决数据问题，相比现有方法具有更高准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据工作者常无法精确表达信息需求，而现有LLM在处理不明确意图时表现脆弱，容易产生幻觉字段、假设连接路径或生成无根据答案，需要更可靠的解决方案。

Method: 采用关系具体化方法，将用户信息需求表示为关系模式，使用LLM驱动的智能体架构，包含指挥式规划和宏观微观上下文管理，在异构关系语料库上迭代精炼模式并构建可执行程序。

Result: 在多个领域与最先进的学术和工业基线相比，Pneuma-Seeker展示了更高的答案准确性；在实际组织部署中验证了可信性和可检查性是LLM中介数据系统的关键需求。

Conclusion: 关系具体化是解决LLM在数据问题中脆弱性的有效方法，Pneuma-Seeker通过共享关系模式实现用户与系统协作，在准确性和可信任性方面优于现有方法。

Abstract: When faced with data problems, many data workers cannot articulate their information need precisely enough for software to help. Although LLMs interpret natural-language requests, they behave brittly when intent is under-specified, e.g., hallucinating fields, assuming join paths, or producing ungrounded answers.
  We present Pneuma-Seeker, a system built around a central idea: relational reification. Pneuma-Seeker represents a user's evolving information need as a relational schema: a concrete, analysis-ready data model shared between user and system. Rather than answering prompts directly, Pneuma-Seeker iteratively refines this schema, then discovers and prepares relevant sources to construct a relation and executable program that compute the answer. Pneuma-Seeker employs an LLM-powered agentic architecture with conductor-style planning and macro- and micro-level context management to operate effectively over heterogeneous relational corpora.
  We evaluate Pneuma-Seeker across multiple domains against state-of-the-art academic and industrial baselines, demonstrating higher answer accuracy. Deployment in a real organization highlights trust and inspectability as essential requirements for LLM-mediated data systems.

</details>


### [22] [Beyond Standard Datacubes: Extracting Features from Irregular and Branching Earth System Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10809)
*Mathilde Leuridan,James Hawkes,Tiago Quintino,Martin Schultz*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出基于压缩树结构的数据超立方体表示法，用于高效处理地球科学中复杂、稀疏、分支的数据空间，并集成特征提取系统


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球科学数据集在体积和结构复杂性上快速增长，传统密集正交数据立方体模型难以高效表示不规则、稀疏或分支数据空间，需要新的数据抽象方法

Method: 基于压缩树结构设计通用数据超立方体表示，分析其捕获稀疏性和条件关系的能力；在Polytope框架内实现集成特征提取系统，直接在树基数据超立方体上操作

Result: 压缩树数据超立方体作为快速缓存式索引在大规模后端数据存储上表现有效；集成系统支持精确的子字段数据提取和灵活的用户驱动访问模式，性能优于传统访问模型

Conclusion: 该工作弥合了表达性数据超立方体模型与高效数据访问方法之间的差距，为大规模异构地球科学数据集提供了可扩展、以用户为中心的访问基础框架

Abstract: Earth science datasets are growing rapidly in both volume and structural complexity. They increasingly contain richly labelled data with heterogeneous metadata and complex internal constraints that impose dependencies between variables and dimensions. Datacubes have become a common abstraction for organising such datasets, but traditional dense and orthogonal datacube models struggle to represent irregular, sparse or branching data spaces efficiently. In this paper, we introduce a generalised data hypercube representation based on compressed tree structures, which enables an accurate and compact description of complex data spaces. We describe the design of this representation and analyse its ability to capture sparsity and conditional relationships while remaining efficient to traverse. Using a concrete implementation, we study the performance characteristics of compressed tree data hypercubes and demonstrate their effectiveness as fast, cache-like indices over large backend data stores. Building on this representation, we present an integrated feature extraction system that operates directly on tree-based data hypercubes within the Polytope framework. By embedding data access strategies into the data hypercube abstraction itself, the system enables precise, sub-field data extraction and supports flexible, user-driven access patterns. We evaluate the performance of the integrated system and show how it enables new ways of interacting with complex datasets that are difficult to support using traditional access models. This work bridges the gap between expressive data hypercube models and efficient data access methods. In particular, it provides a unified framework that combines tree-based data representations with feature extraction capabilities. The proposed approach therefore offers a foundation for scalable and user-centric access to large heterogeneous Earth science datasets.

</details>


### [23] [Poisson Sampling over Acyclic Joins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10982)
*Liese Bekkers,Frank Neven,Lorrens Pantelis,Stijn Vansummeren*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出针对无环连接查询的泊松采样问题，开发实例最优算法，通过随机访问索引实现高效采样，在列存储中验证性能优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统泊松采样需要对每个连接元组进行伯努利试验，效率低下。需要开发针对无环连接的高效采样算法，既能支持泊松采样，又能作为通用连接处理的基础。

Method: 提出两阶段算法：1) 构建随机访问索引，支持不物化完整连接结果的情况下随机访问第i个连接元组；2) 利用该索引进行采样。在列存储中实现并优化工程权衡。

Result: 算法时间复杂度为O(N + k log N)，其中N为输入数据库大小，k为采样大小。实验表明显著优于重复伯努利试验算法，且随机访问索引本身也能高效实现Yannakakis无环连接算法。

Conclusion: 为查询引擎设计提供了统一基础，既能高效处理经典无环连接，又能实现泊松采样，相比传统算法无性能损失，具有实用价值。

Abstract: We introduce the problem of Poisson sampling over joins: compute a sample of the result of a join query by conceptually performing a Bernoulli trial for each join tuple, using a non-uniform and tuple-specific probability. We propose an algorithm for Poisson sampling over acyclic joins that is nearly instance-optimal, running in time O(N + k \log N) where N is the size of the input database, and k is the size of the resulting sample. Our algorithm hinges on two building blocks: (1) The construction of a random-access index that allows, given a number i, to randomly access the i-th join tuple without fully materializing the (possibly large) join result; (2) The probing of this index to construct the result sample. We study the engineering trade-offs required to make both components practical, focusing on their implementation in column stores, and identify best-performing alternatives for both. Our experiments on real-world data demonstrate that this pair of alternatives significantly outperforms the repeated-Bernoulli-trial algorithm for Poisson sampling while also demonstrating that the random-access index by itself can be used to competively implement Yannakakis' acyclic join processing algorithm when no sampling is required. This shows that, as far a query engine design is concerned, it is possible to adopt a uniform basis for both classical acyclic join processing and Poisson sampling, both without regret compared to classical join and sampling algorithms.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [24] [KernelSkill: A Multi-Agent Framework for GPU Kernel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10085)
*Qitong Sun,Jun Han,Tianlin Li,Zhe Tang,Sheng Chen,Fei Yang,Aishan Liu,Xianglong Liu,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KernelSkill是一个基于多智能体的GPU内核优化框架，通过双级内存架构协调专家技能，替代传统LLM中的隐式启发式方法，在KernelBench基准测试中实现了100%成功率和显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的GPU内核优化方法依赖模型内部隐式学习的启发式策略，导致试错效率低且优化过程缺乏可解释性。需要将隐式启发式替换为知识驱动、任务轨迹感知的专家优化技能。

Method: 提出KernelSkill多智能体框架，采用双级内存架构：长期记忆存储可重用的专家技能，短期记忆防止重复回溯。通过协调具有不同专业知识的智能体来实现知识驱动的优化。

Result: 在KernelBench Level 1-3测试中，KernelSkill实现了100%的成功率，相比Torch Eager分别获得5.44x、2.82x和1.92x的平均加速比，超越了现有基线方法。

Conclusion: KernelSkill通过将隐式启发式替换为显式的专家技能，实现了更高效、可解释的GPU内核优化，为AI系统性能提升提供了新方法。

Abstract: Improving GPU kernel efficiency is crucial for advancing AI systems. Recent work has explored leveraging large language models (LLMs) for GPU kernel generation and optimization. However, existing LLM-based kernel optimization pipelines typically rely on opaque, implicitly learned heuristics within the LLMs to determine optimization strategies. This leads to inefficient trial-and-error and weakly interpretable optimizations. Our key insight is to replace implicit heuristics with expert optimization skills that are knowledge-driven and aware of task trajectories. Specifically, we present KernelSkill, a multi-agent framework with a dual-level memory architecture. KernelSkill operates by coordinating agents with long-term memory of reusable expert skills and short-term memory to prevent repetitive backtracking. On KernelBench Levels 1-3, KernelSkill achieves a 100% success rate and average speedups of 5.44x, 2.82x, and 1.92x over Torch Eager on Levels 1, 2, and 3, respectively, outperforming prior baselines. Code is available at https://github.com/0satan0/KernelMem/.

</details>


### [25] [World Model for Battery Degradation Prediction Under Non-Stationary Aging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10527)
*Kai Chin Lim,Khay Wai See*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将锂离子电池退化预测建模为世界模型问题，通过编码循环数据到潜在状态并学习动态转移来预测未来80个循环的SOH轨迹，相比直接回归方法误差减半，并探索了电化学知识约束对预测的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动方法虽然能通过直接回归产生轨迹输出，但缺乏将退化动态向前传播的时间机制。需要开发能够编码原始时间序列数据并学习动态转移的模型，以更准确地预测电池长期退化轨迹。

Method: 将电池退化预测构建为世界模型问题：1) 编码每个循环的原始电压、电流和温度时间序列到潜在状态；2) 通过学习的动态转移向前传播潜在状态；3) 产生跨越80个循环的未来轨迹；4) 在训练损失中融入单粒子模型(SPM)约束以研究电化学知识是否改善学习动态；5) 在包含138个电池的Severson LiFePO4数据集上评估三种配置。

Result: 1) 迭代展开方法相比相同编码器的直接回归，轨迹预测误差减半；2) SPM约束在退化拐点处改善预测，该处电阻与SOH关系最为适用；3) SPM约束未改变总体精度，但针对特定退化阶段有针对性改进。

Conclusion: 世界模型框架通过编码原始时间序列和学习动态转移，显著提高了电池退化轨迹预测的准确性。电化学知识约束在特定退化阶段（如拐点）能提供额外改进，表明结合数据驱动方法与领域知识是电池退化预测的有效途径。

Abstract: Degradation prognosis for lithium-ion cells requires forecasting the state-of-health (SOH) trajectory over future cycles. Existing data-driven approaches can produce trajectory outputs through direct regression, but lack a mechanism to propagate degradation dynamics forward in time. This paper formulates battery degradation prognosis as a world model problem, encoding raw voltage, current, and temperature time-series from each cycle into a latent state and propagating it forward via a learned dynamics transition to produce a future trajectory spanning 80 cycles. To investigate whether electrochemical knowledge improves the learned dynamics, a Single Particle Model (SPM) constraint is incorporated into the training loss. Three configurations are evaluated on the Severson LiFePO4 (LFP) dataset of 138 cells. Iterative rollout halves the trajectory forecast error compared to direct regression from the same encoder. The SPM constraint improves prediction at the degradation knee where the resistance to SOH relationship is most applicable, without changing aggregate accuracy.

</details>


### [26] [MoE-SpAc: Efficient MoE Inference Based on Speculative Activation Utility in Heterogeneous Edge Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09983)
*Shuhuai Li,Jianghao Lin,Dongdong Ge,Yinyu Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MoE-SpAc：利用推测解码作为内存管理前瞻传感器，通过动态负载均衡和异步执行优化MoE模型在边缘设备上的推理性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型在边缘设备上面临严重内存限制，现有卸载策略因自回归专家激活的动态性和低信息特性而受限于I/O瓶颈

Method: 提出MoE-SpAc框架，包含：1) 推测效用估计器跟踪专家需求；2) 异构工作负载均衡器通过在线整数优化动态划分计算；3) 异步执行引擎在统一效用空间协调预取和驱逐

Result: 在七个基准测试中，MoE-SpAc相比最先进的SD基线提升42% TPS，相比所有标准基线平均加速4.04倍

Conclusion: 将推测解码重新定位为内存管理的前瞻传感器，结合效用估计、动态负载均衡和异步执行，能有效解决MoE模型在边缘设备上的内存约束问题

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models enable scalable performance but face severe memory constraints on edge devices. Existing offloading strategies struggle with I/O bottlenecks due to the dynamic, low-information nature of autoregressive expert activation. In this paper, we propose to repurpose Speculative Decoding (SD) not merely as a compute accelerator, but as an informative lookahead sensor for memory management, supported by our theoretical and empirical analyses. Hence, we introduce MoE-SpAc, an MoE inference framework that integrates a Speculative Utility Estimator to track expert demand, a Heterogeneous Workload Balancer to dynamically partition computation via online integer optimization, and an Asynchronous Execution Engine to unify the prefetching and eviction in the same utility space. Extensive experiments on seven benchmarks demonstrate that MoE-SpAc achieves a 42% improvement in TPS over the SOTA SD-based baseline, and an average 4.04x speedup over all standard baselines. Code is available at https://github.com/lshAlgorithm/MoE-SpAc .

</details>


### [27] [Muscle Synergy Priors Enhance Biomechanical Fidelity in Predictive Musculoskeletal Locomotion Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10474)
*Ilseung Park,Eunsik Choi,Jangwhan Ahn,Jooeun Ahn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于生理学约束的强化学习框架，通过肌肉协同作用约束控制，在有限实验数据下实现高保真度的人类运动预测仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类运动源于高维神经肌肉控制，使得预测性肌肉骨骼仿真具有挑战性。需要开发能够利用有限实验数据、同时保持生理学合理性的仿真方法。

Method: 从少量地面行走试验的逆向肌肉骨骼分析中提取低维肌肉协同基，将其作为动作空间，用于训练肌肉驱动的三维模型，涵盖不同速度、坡度和不平坦地形。

Result: 控制器在0.7-1.8 m/s速度范围和±6°坡度下生成稳定步态，重现关节角度、关节力矩和地面反作用力的条件依赖性调制。相比无约束控制器，协同约束控制减少了非生理性膝关节运动学，保持膝关节力矩在实验范围内。

Conclusion: 将神经生理学结构嵌入强化学习可以提高预测性人类运动仿真的生物力学保真度和泛化能力，特别是在实验数据有限的情况下。

Abstract: Human locomotion emerges from high-dimensional neuromuscular control, making predictive musculoskeletal simulation challenging. We present a physiology-informed reinforcement-learning framework that constrains control using muscle synergies. We extracted a low-dimensional synergy basis from inverse musculoskeletal analyses of a small set of overground walking trials and used it as the action space for a muscle-driven three-dimensional model trained across variable speeds, slopes and uneven terrain. The resulting controller generated stable gait from 0.7-1.8 m/s and on $\pm$ 6$^{\circ}$ grades and reproduced condition-dependent modulation of joint angles, joint moments and ground reaction forces. Compared with an unconstrained controller, synergy-constrained control reduced non-physiological knee kinematics and kept knee moment profiles within the experimental envelope. Across conditions, simulated vertical ground reaction forces correlated strongly with human measurements, and muscle-activation timing largely fell within inter-subject variability. These results show that embedding neurophysiological structure into reinforcement learning can improve biomechanical fidelity and generalization in predictive human locomotion simulation with limited experimental data.

</details>


### [28] [Personalized Group Relative Policy Optimization for Heterogenous Preference Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10009)
*Jialu Wang,Heinrich Peters,Asad A. Butt,Navid Hashemi,Alireza Hashemi,Pouya M. Ghari,Joseph Hoover,James Rae,Morteza Dehghani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: P-GRPO：一种个性化对齐框架，通过解耦优势估计与即时批次统计，针对不同用户偏好进行归一化，解决了标准GRPO在个性化设置中混淆不同用户奖励分布的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLMs）通常通过RLHF等方法优化单一全局目标，难以与多样化个人偏好对齐。标准GRPO基于组的归一化假设所有样本可互换，在个性化设置中混淆不同用户奖励分布，系统性地偏向主导偏好而抑制少数信号。

Method: 提出个性化GRPO（P-GRPO）框架，通过将优势估计与即时批次统计解耦来解决上述问题。P-GRPO不是基于当前生成组进行归一化，而是针对偏好组特定的奖励历史进行优势归一化，从而保留学习不同偏好所需的对比信号。

Result: 在多样化任务上评估P-GRPO，发现它比标准GRPO实现更快的收敛和更高的奖励，增强了恢复和对齐异质偏好信号的能力。

Conclusion: 在优化层面考虑奖励异质性对于构建能够忠实对齐多样化人类偏好而不牺牲通用能力的模型至关重要。P-GRPO通过解耦优势估计与即时批次统计，有效解决了标准GRPO在个性化对齐中的局限性。

Abstract: Despite their sophisticated general-purpose capabilities, Large Language Models (LLMs) often fail to align with diverse individual preferences because standard post-training methods, like Reinforcement Learning with Human Feedback (RLHF), optimize for a single, global objective. While Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is a widely adopted on-policy reinforcement learning framework, its group-based normalization implicitly assumes that all samples are exchangeable, inheriting this limitation in personalized settings. This assumption conflates distinct user reward distributions and systematically biases learning toward dominant preferences while suppressing minority signals. To address this, we introduce Personalized GRPO (P-GRPO), a novel alignment framework that decouples advantage estimation from immediate batch statistics. By normalizing advantages against preference-group-specific reward histories rather than the concurrent generation group, P-GRPO preserves the contrastive signal necessary for learning distinct preferences. We evaluate P-GRPO across diverse tasks and find that it consistently achieves faster convergence and higher rewards than standard GRPO, thereby enhancing its ability to recover and align with heterogeneous preference signals. Our results demonstrate that accounting for reward heterogeneity at the optimization level is essential for building models that faithfully align with diverse human preferences without sacrificing general capabilities.

</details>


### [29] [LWM-Temporal: Sparse Spatio-Temporal Attention for Wireless Channel Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10024)
*Sadjad Alikhani,Akshay Malhotra,Shahab Hamidi-Rad,Ahmed Alkhateeb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LWM-Temporal是一种针对无线信道时空特性的基础模型，通过稀疏时空注意力机制和物理信息掩码课程学习，在角度-延迟-时间域中学习可迁移的通用信道嵌入，在信道预测任务中表现出色


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线信道具有时空特性，传统方法难以有效捕捉移动性引起的信道演化。需要一种任务无关的基础模型来学习可重用的通用信道嵌入，以支持各种下游任务

Method: 在角度-延迟-时间域操作，引入稀疏时空注意力机制，将交互限制在物理上合理的邻域内，降低注意力复杂度。采用物理信息掩码课程进行自监督预训练，模拟真实遮挡、导频稀疏和测量损伤

Result: 在多种移动场景下的信道预测实验中，相比强基线模型取得一致改进，特别是在长时域和有限微调数据情况下表现优异，证明了几何感知架构和几何一致预训练的重要性

Conclusion: LWM-Temporal通过几何感知的稀疏时空注意力和物理信息预训练，成功学习了可迁移的时空无线表示，为无线信道建模提供了有效的任务无关基础模型

Abstract: LWM-Temporal is a new member of the Large Wireless Models (LWM) family that targets the spatiotemporal nature of wireless channels. Designed as a task-agnostic foundation model, LWM-Temporal learns universal channel embeddings that capture mobility-induced evolution and are reusable across various downstream tasks. To achieve this objective, LWM-Temporal operates in the angle-delay-time domain and introduces Sparse Spatio-Temporal Attention (SSTA), a propagation-aligned attention mechanism that restricts interactions to physically plausible neighborhoods, reducing attention complexity by an order of magnitude while preserving geometry-consistent dependencies. LWM-Temporal is pretrained in a self-supervised manner using a physics-informed masking curriculum that emulates realistic occlusions, pilot sparsity, and measurement impairments. Experimental results on channel prediction across multiple mobility regimes show consistent improvements over strong baselines, particularly under long horizons and limited fine-tuning data, highlighting the importance of geometry-aware architectures and geometry-consistent pretraining for learning transferable spatiotemporal wireless representations.

</details>


### [30] [Effective Dataset Distillation for Spatio-Temporal Forecasting with Bi-dimensional Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10410)
*Taehyung Kwon,Yeonje Choi,Yeongho Kim,Kijung Shin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: STemDist：首个专门用于时空时间序列预测的数据集蒸馏方法，通过平衡压缩时空维度来减少训练时间和内存消耗，在五个真实数据集上实现了6倍加速、8倍内存节省和12%预测误差降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时空时间序列预测中，随着数据集规模和模型复杂度增长，深度学习模型训练变得时间和资源密集。现有数据集蒸馏方法仅压缩单一维度，不适合同时包含时空维度的时空数据集，需要专门方法同时压缩时空维度。

Method: 提出STemDist方法：1）平衡压缩时空维度以减少训练时间和内存；2）在聚类级别而非单个位置级别进行蒸馏以降低蒸馏成本；3）结合基于子集的细粒度蒸馏技术提升预测性能。

Result: 在五个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，与通用和时间序列数据集蒸馏方法相比，STemDist蒸馏的数据集使模型训练：1）更快（最高6倍）；2）更内存高效（最高8倍）；3）更有效（预测误差降低最高12%）。

Conclusion: STemDist是首个专门针对时空时间序列预测的数据集蒸馏方法，通过平衡压缩时空维度、聚类级蒸馏和细粒度增强，显著提升了训练效率、内存效率和预测性能。

Abstract: Spatio-temporal time series are widely used in real-world applications, including traffic prediction and weather forecasting. They are sequences of observations over extensive periods and multiple locations, naturally represented as multidimensional data. Forecasting is a central task in spatio-temporal analysis, and numerous deep learning methods have been developed to address it. However, as dataset sizes and model complexities continue to grow in practice, training deep learning models has become increasingly time- and resource-intensive. A promising solution to this challenge is dataset distillation, which synthesizes compact datasets that can effectively replace the original data for model training. Although successful in various domains, including time series analysis, existing dataset distillation methods compress only one dimension, making them less suitable for spatio-temporal datasets, where both spatial and temporal dimensions jointly contribute to the large data volume. To address this limitation, we propose STemDist, the first dataset distillation method specialized for spatio-temporal time series forecasting. A key idea of our solution is to compress both temporal and spatial dimensions in a balanced manner, reducing training time and memory. We further reduce the distillation cost by performing distillation at the cluster level rather than the individual location level, and we complement this coarse-grained approach with a subset-based granular distillation technique that enhances forecasting performance. On five real-world datasets, we show empirically that, compared to both general and time-series dataset distillation methods, datasets distilled by our STemDist method enable model training (1) faster (up to 6X) (2) more memory-efficient (up to 8X), and (3) more effective (with up to 12% lower prediction error).

</details>


### [31] [Revisiting Sharpness-Aware Minimization: A More Faithful and Effective Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10048)
*Jianlong Chen,Zhiming Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: XSAM提出了一种显式锐度感知最小化方法，通过显式估计局部邻域内最大值方向来改进SAM，解决了SAM中梯度近似不准确的问题，在单步和多步设置下都表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然SAM通过最小化参数邻域内的最大训练损失来提升泛化能力，但其实践中采用梯度上升后使用上升点梯度更新当前参数的做法缺乏直观理解。作者发现这种做法的优势在于上升点梯度能更好地近似局部邻域内最大值的方向，但存在两个问题：单步上升点梯度近似不准确，且随着上升步数增加近似质量会下降。

Method: 提出了XSAM方法：1) 通过显式估计训练过程中局部邻域内最大值的方向来解决第一个问题；2) 构建一个能有效利用多步上升点梯度信息的搜索空间来解决第二个问题。XSAM采用统一公式适用于单步和多步设置，计算开销可忽略。

Result: 大量实验表明XSAM在性能上持续优于现有方法，证明了其有效性。

Conclusion: XSAM为SAM提供了更直观的理论解释，并通过显式估计最大值方向和改进梯度信息利用，解决了SAM中梯度近似不准确的问题，实现了更好的优化效果。

Abstract: Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM) enhances generalization by minimizing the maximum training loss within a predefined neighborhood around the parameters. However, its practical implementation approximates this as gradient ascent(s) followed by applying the gradient at the ascent point to update the current parameters. This practice can be justified as approximately optimizing the objective by neglecting the (full) derivative of the ascent point with respect to the current parameters. Nevertheless, a direct and intuitive understanding of why using the gradient at the ascent point to update the current parameters works superiorly is still lacking. Our work bridges this gap by proposing a novel and intuitive interpretation. We show that the gradient at the single-step ascent point, \uline{when applied to the current parameters}, provides a better approximation of the direction from the current parameters toward the maximum within the local neighborhood than the local gradient. This improved approximation thereby enables a more direct escape from the maximum within the local neighborhood. Nevertheless, our analysis further reveals two issues. First, the approximation by the gradient at the single-step ascent point is often inaccurate. Second, the approximation quality may degrade as the number of ascent steps increases. To address these limitations, we propose in this paper eXplicit Sharpness-Aware Minimization (XSAM). It tackles the first by explicitly estimating the direction of the maximum during training, while addressing the second by crafting a search space that effectively leverages the gradient information at the multi-step ascent point. XSAM features a unified formulation that applies to both single-step and multi-step settings and only incurs negligible computational overhead. Extensive experiments demonstrate the consistent superiority of XSAM against existing counterparts.

</details>


### [32] [InFusionLayer: a CFA-based ensemble tool to generate new classifiers for learning and modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10049)
*Eric Roginek,Jingyan Xu,D. Frank. Hsu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: InFusionLayer是一个基于组合融合分析（CFA）的机器学习架构，通过整合秩-分特征函数和认知多样性来优化无监督和监督学习的多分类问题，为PyTorch、TensorFlow和Scikit-learn提供了通用Python工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管组合融合分析（CFA）提供了结合多个评分系统的方法和实践，包括秩-分特征函数和认知多样性等技术，但目前缺乏将这些技术整合的通用Python工具，限制了这些先进集成学习方法的实际应用。

Method: 开发了InFusionLayer架构，该系统融合级别的机器学习架构使用中等规模的基础模型集合，整合了秩-分特征函数和认知多样性特征，支持PyTorch、TensorFlow和Scikit-learn工作流，优化无监督和监督学习的多分类问题。

Result: 在多个计算机视觉数据集上验证了InFusionLayer的性能，结果表明整合秩-分特征函数和认知多样性特征具有实际优势，为更复杂的集成学习应用铺平了道路。

Conclusion: InFusionLayer填补了组合融合分析缺乏通用Python工具的空白，通过开源代码促进了该技术的持续发展和社区可访问性，推动了集成学习在机器学习中的更广泛应用。

Abstract: Ensemble learning is a well established body of methods for machine learning to enhance predictive performance by combining multiple algorithms/models. Combinatorial Fusion Analysis (CFA) has provided method and practice for combining multiple scoring systems, using rank-score characteristic (RSC) function and cognitive diversity (CD), including ensemble method and model fusion. However, there is no general-purpose Python tool available that incorporate these techniques. In this paper we introduce \texttt{InFusionLayer}, a machine learning architecture inspired by CFA at the system fusion level that uses a moderate set of base models to optimize unsupervised and supervised learning multiclassification problems. We demonstrate \texttt{InFusionLayer}'s ease of use for PyTorch, TensorFlow, and Scikit-learn workflows by validating its performance on various computer vision datasets. Our results highlight the practical advantages of incorporating distinctive features of RSC function and CD, paving the way for more sophisticated ensemble learning applications in machine learning. We open-sourced our code to encourage continuing development and community accessibility to leverage CFA on github: https://github.com/ewroginek/Infusion

</details>


### [33] [Cluster-Aware Attention-Based Deep Reinforcement Learning for Pickup and Delivery Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10053)
*Wentao Wang,Lifeng Han,Guangyu Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CAADRL是一个用于解决取送货问题的深度强化学习框架，通过集群感知编码和分层解码显式利用PDP实例的多尺度结构，在保持竞争力的同时显著降低推理时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DRL方法要么将所有节点建模在平面图上依赖隐式学习来强制约束，要么通过推理时的协作搜索实现强性能但代价是显著的延迟。需要一种既能利用PDP实例的聚类结构又能保持高效推理的方法。

Method: 提出CAADRL框架：1) 编码器基于Transformer，结合全局自注意力和对仓库、取货、送货节点的集群内注意力；2) 引入具有可学习门控的动态双解码器，平衡集群内路由和集群间转移；3) 使用POMO风格策略梯度方案进行端到端训练，每个实例使用多个对称rollout。

Result: 在合成的聚类和均匀PDP基准测试中，CAADRL在聚类实例上匹配或优于强SOTA基线，在均匀实例上保持高度竞争力，尤其随着问题规模增大。关键优势是推理时间显著低于神经协作搜索基线。

Conclusion: 显式建模集群结构为神经PDP求解器提供了有效且高效的归纳偏置，CAADRL在保持性能竞争力的同时实现了更低的推理延迟。

Abstract: The Pickup and Delivery Problem (PDP) is a fundamental and challenging variant of the Vehicle Routing Problem, characterized by tightly coupled pickup--delivery pairs, precedence constraints, and spatial layouts that often exhibit clustering. Existing deep reinforcement learning (DRL) approaches either model all nodes on a flat graph, relying on implicit learning to enforce constraints, or achieve strong performance through inference-time collaborative search at the cost of substantial latency. In this paper, we propose \emph{CAADRL} (Cluster-Aware Attention-based Deep Reinforcement Learning), a DRL framework that explicitly exploits the multi-scale structure of PDP instances via cluster-aware encoding and hierarchical decoding. The encoder builds on a Transformer and combines global self-attention with intra-cluster attention over depot, pickup, and delivery nodes, producing embeddings that are both globally informative and locally role-aware. Based on these embeddings, we introduce a Dynamic Dual-Decoder with a learnable gate that balances intra-cluster routing and inter-cluster transitions at each step. The policy is trained end-to-end with a POMO-style policy gradient scheme using multiple symmetric rollouts per instance. Experiments on synthetic clustered and uniform PDP benchmarks show that CAADRL matches or improves upon strong state-of-the-art baselines on clustered instances and remains highly competitive on uniform instances, particularly as problem size increases. Crucially, our method achieves these results with substantially lower inference time than neural collaborative-search baselines, suggesting that explicitly modeling cluster structure provides an effective and efficient inductive bias for neural PDP solvers.

</details>


### [34] [HTMuon: Improving Muon via Heavy-Tailed Spectral Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10067)
*Tianyu Pang,Yujie Fang,Zihang Liu,Shenyang Deng,Lei Hsiung,Shuhua Yu,Yaoqing Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HTMuon改进Muon优化器，通过保留参数间依赖关系的同时产生重尾更新和权重谱，在LLM预训练和图像分类任务上优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon的正交化更新规则抑制了重尾权重谱的出现，并过度强调噪声主导方向的训练。受重尾自正则化理论启发，需要改进Muon以产生更重尾的更新和权重谱。

Method: 提出HTMuon方法，在保留Muon捕捉参数间依赖关系能力的同时，产生更重尾的更新并诱导更重尾的权重谱。该方法可作为插件应用于现有Muon变体。

Result: 在LLM预训练和图像分类实验中，HTMuon持续优于最先进的基线方法。例如，在C4数据集上的LLaMA预训练中，HTMuon相比Muon将困惑度降低了0.98。

Conclusion: HTMuon在理论和实验上都表现出优越性，对应Schatten-q范数约束下的最速下降，并在平滑非凸设置下提供了收敛性分析。

Abstract: Muon has recently shown promising results in LLM training. In this work, we study how to further improve Muon. We argue that Muon's orthogonalized update rule suppresses the emergence of heavy-tailed weight spectra and over-emphasizes the training along noise-dominated directions. Motivated by the Heavy-Tailed Self-Regularization (HT-SR) theory, we propose HTMuon. HTMuon preserves Muon's ability to capture parameter interdependencies while producing heavier-tailed updates and inducing heavier-tailed weight spectra. Experiments on LLM pretraining and image classification show that HTMuon consistently improves performance over state-of-the-art baselines and can also serve as a plug-in on top of existing Muon variants. For example, on LLaMA pretraining on the C4 dataset, HTMuon reduces perplexity by up to $0.98$ compared to Muon. We further theoretically show that HTMuon corresponds to steepest descent under the Schatten-$q$ norm constraint and provide convergence analysis in smooth non-convex settings. The implementation of HTMuon is available at https://github.com/TDCSZ327/HTmuon.

</details>


### [35] [Improving Search Agent with One Line of Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10069)
*Jian Li,Dongsheng Chen,Zhenhua Xu,Yizhang Jin,Jiafu Wu,Chengjie Wang,Xiaotong Yuan,Yabiao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAPO通过条件令牌级KL约束解决TARL中的重要性采样分布漂移问题，仅需一行代码修改即可稳定训练，在多个QA基准上实现显著性能提升


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具驱动的智能体强化学习(TARL)在训练搜索智能体进行多轮信息寻求过程中存在训练不稳定性问题，具体表现为重要性采样分布漂移(ISDD)，这会导致梯度更新失效和不可逆的训练失败

Method: 提出搜索智能体策略优化(SAPO)，采用条件令牌级KL约束来稳定训练。与传统硬截断不同，SAPO选择性地惩罚当前策略与旧策略之间的KL散度，仅对概率较低且策略偏移过度的正令牌应用惩罚，从而防止分布漂移同时保持梯度流动

Result: 在七个QA基准测试中，SAPO相比Search-R1实现了+10.6%的绝对改进（+31.5%相对改进），在不同模型规模（1.5B、14B）和模型家族（Qwen、LLaMA）上都获得了稳定增益

Conclusion: SAPO通过简单有效的条件KL约束机制解决了TARL中的训练不稳定性问题，仅需一行代码修改即可部署，为工具驱动的智能体强化学习提供了稳定可靠的训练框架

Abstract: Tool-based Agentic Reinforcement Learning (TARL) has emerged as a promising paradigm for training search agents to interact with external tools for a multi-turn information-seeking process autonomously. However, we identify a critical training instability that leads to catastrophic model collapse: Importance Sampling Distribution Drift(ISDD). In Group Relative Policy Optimization(GRPO), a widely adopted TARL algorithm, ISDD manifests as a precipitous decline in the importance sampling ratios, which nullifies gradient updates and triggers irreversible training failure. To address this, we propose \textbf{S}earch \textbf{A}gent \textbf{P}olicy \textbf{O}ptimization (\textbf{SAPO}), which stabilizes training via a conditional token-level KL constraint. Unlike hard clipping, which ignores distributional divergence, SAPO selectively penalizes the KL divergence between the current and old policies. Crucially, this penalty is applied only to positive tokens with low probabilities where the policy has shifted excessively, thereby preventing distribution drift while preserving gradient flow. Remarkably, SAPO requires only one-line code modification to standard GRPO, ensuring immediate deployability. Extensive experiments across seven QA benchmarks demonstrate that SAPO achieves \textbf{+10.6\% absolute improvement} (+31.5\% relative) over Search-R1, yielding consistent gains across varying model scales (1.5B, 14B) and families (Qwen, LLaMA).

</details>


### [36] [Dissecting Chronos: Sparse Autoencoders Reveal Causal Feature Hierarchies in Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10071)
*Anurag Mishra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首次将稀疏自编码器应用于时间序列基础模型Chronos-T5-Large，通过特征消融实验揭示模型内部表示结构，发现因果关键特征集中在中层编码器而非语义最丰富的最终层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列基础模型在关键领域应用日益广泛，但其内部表示仍然不透明，需要开发可解释性方法来理解这些模型的工作原理和决策依据。

Method: 在Chronos-T5-Large模型的六个层上训练TopK稀疏自编码器，通过392个单特征消融实验评估特征对预测性能的影响，使用CRPS指标量化特征重要性。

Result: 所有消融特征均导致CRPS性能下降，证实特征因果相关性。发现深度依赖的层次结构：早期编码器编码低频特征，中层编码器集中因果关键的变化检测特征，最终编码器包含丰富但因果重要性较低的时态概念分类。最关键特征位于中层编码器（最大单特征ΔCRPS=38.61）。

Conclusion: 机械可解释性方法可有效应用于时间序列基础模型，Chronos-T5主要依赖突变动态检测而非周期性模式识别，最关键特征位于中层编码器而非语义最丰富的最终层。

Abstract: Time series foundation models (TSFMs) are increasingly deployed in high-stakes domains, yet their internal representations remain opaque. We present the first application of sparse autoencoders (SAEs) to a TSFM, training TopK SAEs on activations of Chronos-T5-Large (710M parameters) across six layers. Through 392 single-feature ablation experiments, we establish that every ablated feature produces a positive CRPS degradation, confirming causal relevance. Our analysis reveals a depth-dependent hierarchy: early encoder layers encode low-level frequency features, the mid-encoder concentrates causally critical change-detection features, and the final encoder compresses a rich but less causally important taxonomy of temporal concepts. The most critical features reside in the mid-encoder (max single-feature Delta CRPS = 38.61), not in the semantically richest final encoder layer, where progressive ablation paradoxically improves forecast quality. These findings demonstrate that mechanistic interpretability transfers effectively to TSFMs and that Chronos-T5 relies on abrupt-dynamics detection rather than periodic pattern recognition.

</details>


### [37] [Digging Deeper: Learning Multi-Level Concept Hierarchies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10084)
*Oscar Hill,Mateo Espinosa Zarlenga,Mateja Jamnik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MLCS方法从顶层监督中发现多层次概念层次结构，以及Deep-HiCEMs架构来表示这些层次结构并支持多抽象级别的干预


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于概念的模型虽然承诺可解释性，但通常依赖详尽的标注，并将概念视为扁平且独立的。最近的HiCEMs和概念分割方法虽然能建模概念关系，但仅限于浅层层次结构

Method: 提出Multi-Level Concept Splitting (MLCS)从仅顶层监督中发现多层次概念层次结构，以及Deep-HiCEMs架构来表示这些发现的层次结构，支持测试时多抽象级别的概念干预

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，MLCS能发现训练时不存在的人类可解释概念，Deep-HiCEMs在保持高准确率的同时支持测试时概念干预，并能提升任务性能

Conclusion: MLCS和Deep-HiCEMs克服了现有方法仅限于浅层层次结构的限制，能够从顶层监督中发现多层次概念层次结构，并支持有效的多级别概念干预，提高了基于概念模型的可解释性和实用性

Abstract: Although concept-based models promise interpretability by explaining predictions with human-understandable concepts, they typically rely on exhaustive annotations and treat concepts as flat and independent. To circumvent this, recent work has introduced Hierarchical Concept Embedding Models (HiCEMs) to explicitly model concept relationships, and Concept Splitting to discover sub-concepts using only coarse annotations. However, both HiCEMs and Concept Splitting are restricted to shallow hierarchies. We overcome this limitation with Multi-Level Concept Splitting (MLCS), which discovers multi-level concept hierarchies from only top-level supervision, and Deep-HiCEMs, an architecture that represents these discovered hierarchies and enables interventions at multiple levels of abstraction. Experiments across multiple datasets show that MLCS discovers human-interpretable concepts absent during training and that Deep-HiCEMs maintain high accuracy while supporting test-time concept interventions that can improve task performance.

</details>


### [38] [ES-dLLM: Efficient Inference for Diffusion Large Language Models by Early-Skipping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10088)
*Zijian Zhu,Fei Ren,Zhanhong Tan,Kaisheng Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ES-dLLM：一种无需训练、基于令牌重要性估计的扩散大语言模型推理加速框架，通过跳过早期层中不重要的令牌来减少计算量，实现5.6-16.8倍加速


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型(dLLMs)相比自回归模型具有捕获双向上下文和并行生成的潜力，但其推理过程计算成本高昂，因为每次迭代都需要处理完整的输入上下文。研究发现中间表示在连续迭代中变化细微，这为加速提供了机会。

Method: 提出ES-dLLM框架：1) 分析dLLM生成动态，发现键、值和隐藏状态在连续迭代中变化细微；2) 基于中间张量变化和先前迭代的置信度分数计算令牌重要性；3) 在早期层根据重要性估计跳过不重要的令牌，减少计算量。

Result: 在LLaDA-8B和Dream-7B模型上，ES-dLLM在NVIDIA H200 GPU上分别达到226.57和308.51 TPS的吞吐量，相比原始实现实现5.6-16.8倍加速，相比最先进的缓存方法提升1.85倍，同时保持生成质量。

Conclusion: ES-dLLM通过利用dLLM中间表示的稳定性，基于令牌重要性跳过早期层计算，实现了显著的推理加速，为扩散大语言模型的高效部署提供了有效的训练免费解决方案。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) are emerging as a promising alternative to autoregressive models (ARMs) due to their ability to capture bidirectional context and the potential for parallel generation. Despite the advantages, dLLM inference remains computationally expensive as the full input context is processed at every iteration. In this work, we analyze the generation dynamics of dLLMs and find that intermediate representations, including key, value, and hidden states, change only subtly across successive iterations. Leveraging this insight, we propose \textbf{ES-dLLM}, a training-free inference acceleration framework for dLLM that reduces computation by skipping tokens in early layers based on the estimated importance. Token importance is computed with intermediate tensor variation and confidence scores of previous iterations. Experiments on LLaDA-8B and Dream-7B demonstrate that ES-dLLM achieves throughput of up to 226.57 and 308.51 tokens per second (TPS), respectively, on an NVIDIA H200 GPU, delivering 5.6$\times$ to 16.8$\times$ speedup over the vanilla implementation and up to 1.85$\times$ over the state-of-the-art caching method, while preserving generation quality.

</details>
