TL;DR: 提出基于随机平滑的轨迹预测模型防御机制，能有效对抗对抗攻击而不影响正常场景精度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶轨迹预测模型对对抗攻击高度脆弱，现有防御措施有限，需要开发有效的对抗攻击防御机制

Method: 基于随机平滑的防御机制，通过一系列实验测试不同的随机平滑策略，评估其对模型鲁棒性的提升效果

Result: 该方法能一致提升多个基础轨迹预测模型在各种数据集上的预测鲁棒性，且不影响非对抗场景下的准确性

Conclusion: 随机平滑为轨迹预测中的对抗攻击缓解提供了一种简单且计算成本低的技术

Abstract: Accurate and robust trajectory prediction is essential for safe and efficient autonomous driving, yet recent work has shown that even state-of-the-art prediction models are highly vulnerable to inputs being mildly perturbed by adversarial attacks. Although model vulnerabilities to such attacks have been studied, work on effective countermeasures remains limited. In this work, we develop and evaluate a new defense mechanism for trajectory prediction models based on randomized smoothing -- an approach previously applied successfully in other domains. We evaluate its ability to improve model robustness through a series of experiments that test different strategies of randomized smoothing. We show that our approach can consistently improve prediction robustness of multiple base trajectory prediction models in various datasets without compromising accuracy in non-adversarial settings. Our results demonstrate that randomized smoothing offers a simple and computationally inexpensive technique for mitigating adversarial attacks in trajectory prediction.

</details>


### [79] [$V_{0.5}$: Generalist Value Model as a Prior for Sparse RL Rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10848)
*Yi-Kai Zhang,Yueqing Sun,Hongyan Hao,Qi Gu,Xunliang Cai,De-Chuan Zhan,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出V_{0.5}方法，通过自适应融合预训练价值模型的基线预测与稀疏rollouts的经验均值，构建低方差、计算高效的鲁棒优势基线，用于强化学习中的可验证奖励场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在可验证奖励的强化学习中，构建鲁棒的优势基线对策略梯度至关重要。现有通用价值模型（如V_0）通过上下文编码模型能力实现预训练价值估计，但存在系统性偏差或幻觉问题，而稀疏采样则导致高方差。需要平衡计算效率与低方差的基线估计方法。

Method: 提出V_{0.5}方法：1）自适应融合预训练价值模型的基线预测（作为先验）与稀疏rollouts的经验均值；2）引入实时统计测试和动态预算分配机制，通过假设检验评估先验可靠性，动态分配额外rollout预算；3）最小化基线估计器的均方误差，确保策略梯度稳定。

Result: 在六个数学推理基准测试上的广泛评估表明，V_{0.5}显著优于GRPO和DAPO，实现更快的收敛速度和超过10%的性能提升。即使在组大小为4的极端稀疏条件下，也能保证稳定的策略梯度。

Conclusion: V_{0.5}通过自适应融合价值模型先验与经验均值，结合实时统计测试和动态预算分配，构建了计算高效且低方差的鲁棒优势基线，在可验证奖励的强化学习中实现了显著的性能改进和稳定收敛。

Abstract: In Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), constructing a robust advantage baseline is critical for policy gradients, effectively guiding the policy model to reinforce desired behaviors. Recent research has introduced Generalist Value Models (such as $V_0$), which achieve pre-trained value estimation by explicitly encoding model capabilities in-context, eliminating the need to synchronously update the value model alongside the policy model. In this paper, we propose $V_{0.5}$, which adaptively fuses the baseline predicted by such value model (acting as a prior) with the empirical mean derived from sparse rollouts. This constructs a robust baseline that balances computational efficiency with extremely low variance. Specifically, we introduce a real-time statistical testing and dynamic budget allocation. This balances the high variance caused by sparse sampling against the systematic bias (or hallucinations) inherent in the value model's prior. By constructing a hypothesis test to evaluate the prior's reliability in real-time, the system dynamically allocates additional rollout budget on demand. This mechanism minimizes the baseline estimator's Mean Squared Error (MSE), guaranteeing stable policy gradients, even under extreme sparsity with a group size of 4. Extensive evaluations across six mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that $V_{0.5}$ significantly outperforms GRPO and DAPO, achieving faster convergence and over some 10% performance improvement.

</details>


### [80] [6ABOS: An Open-Source Atmospheric Correction Framework for the EnMAP Hyperspectral Mission Based on 6S](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10856)
*Gabriel Caballero Cañas,Bárbara Alvado Arranz,Xavier Sòria-Perpinyà,Antonio Ruiz-Verdú,Jesús Delegido,José Moreno*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 6ABOS是一个基于6S辐射传输模型的开源Python框架，用于自动化EnMAP高光谱影像的大气校正，特别针对水体反射率反演，通过集成Google Earth Engine API实现动态大气参数获取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EnMAP任务为光学复杂环境监测开辟了新领域，但水体表面反射率的准确反演仍然是一个重大挑战，因为水体出射信号通常只占总辐射的一小部分，容易被大气散射和表面反射效应掩盖。

Method: 开发了6ABOS框架，基于6S辐射传输模型实现物理反演方案，考虑瑞利散射、气溶胶相互作用和气体吸收。框架集成了自动EnMAP元数据解析和通过Google Earth Engine API的动态大气参数检索。

Result: 在两个地中海内陆水库（贫营养的Benagéber和超富营养的Bellús）进行验证，结果显示现场测量与EnMAP反演的水体出射反射率具有高度光谱相似性，光谱角度制图值在两个研究地点均保持较低水平（SAM < 10°）。

Conclusion: 6ABOS通过conda-forge分发，为科学界提供了一个可扩展、透明且可重复的开源科学工具，用于推进云计算时代的高光谱水生研究。

Abstract: The Environmental Mapping and Analysis Program (EnMAP) mission has opened new frontiers in the monitoring of optically complex environments. However, the accurate retrieval of surface reflectance over water bodies remains a significant challenge, as the water-leaving signal typically accounts for only a small fraction of the total radiance, being easily obscured by atmospheric scattering and surface reflection effects. This paper introduces 6ABOS (6S-based Atmospheric Background Offset Subtraction), a novel open-source Python framework designed to automate the atmospheric correction (AC) of EnMAP hyperspectral imagery. By leveraging the Second Simulation of the Satellite Signal in the Solar Spectrum (6S) radiative transfer model, 6ABOS implements a physically-based inversion scheme that accounts for Rayleigh scattering, aerosol interactions, and gaseous absorption. The framework integrates automated EnMAP metadata parsing with dynamic atmospheric parameter retrieval via the Google Earth Engine (GEE) Application Programming Interface (API). Validation was conducted over two Mediterranean inland water reservoirs with contrasting trophic states: the oligotrophic Benag{'e}ber and the hypertrophic Bell{'u}s. Results demonstrate a high degree of spectral similarity between in situ measurements and EnMAP-derived water-leaving reflectances. The Spectral Angle Mapper (SAM) values remained consistently low (SAM $<$ 10$^\circ$) across both study sites. 6ABOS is distributed via conda-forge, providing the scientific community with a scalable, transparent, and reproducible open-science tool for advancing hyperspectral aquatic research in the cloud-computing era.

</details>


### [81] [SNPgen: Phenotype-Supervised Genotype Representation and Synthetic Data Generation via Latent Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10873)
*Andrea Lampis,Michela Carlotta Massi,Nicola Pirastu,Francesca Ieva,Matteo Matteucci,Emanuele Di Angelantonio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SNPgen是一个两阶段条件潜在扩散框架，用于生成表型监督的合成基因型数据，通过GWAS指导的变异选择、变分自编码器压缩和条件潜在扩散模型，在保持隐私的同时匹配真实数据的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多基因风险评分等基因组分析需要大量个体层面的基因型数据，但严格的数据访问限制阻碍了数据共享。现有的合成基因型生成方法大多是无条件的，无法与表型对齐，或者依赖于无监督压缩，导致统计保真度与下游任务效用之间存在差距。

Method: SNPgen采用两阶段条件潜在扩散框架：1) GWAS指导的变异选择（1,024-2,048个性状相关SNP）；2) 使用变分自编码器进行基因型压缩；3) 通过分类器自由指导，基于二元疾病标签的条件潜在扩散模型。

Result: 在458,724名UK Biobank个体和四种复杂疾病（冠心病、乳腺癌、1型和2型糖尿病）上的评估显示：使用合成数据训练的模型在"合成训练-真实测试"协议中匹配了真实数据的预测性能，接近使用2-6倍更多变异的全基因组PRS方法。隐私分析确认零相同匹配、接近随机的成员推断（AUC≈0.50）、保留连锁不平衡结构以及与源数据的高等位基因频率相关性（r≥0.95）。

Conclusion: SNPgen提供了一种隐私保护的合成基因型生成方法，能够在保持数据隐私的同时，保持统计保真度和下游任务效用，为基因组数据共享提供了可行的替代方案。

Abstract: Polygenic risk scores and other genomic analyses require large individual-level genotype datasets, yet strict data access restrictions impede sharing. Synthetic genotype generation offers a privacy-preserving alternative, but most existing methods operate unconditionally, producing samples without phenotype alignment, or rely on unsupervised compression, creating a gap between statistical fidelity and downstream task utility. We present SNPgen, a two-stage conditional latent diffusion framework for generating phenotype-supervised synthetic genotypes. SNPgen combines GWAS-guided variant selection (1,024-2,048 trait-associated SNPs) with a variational autoencoder for genotype compression and a latent diffusion model conditioned on binary disease labels via classifier-free guidance. Evaluated on 458,724 UK Biobank individuals across four complex diseases (coronary artery disease, breast cancer, type 1 and type 2 diabetes), models trained on synthetic data matched real-data predictive performance in a train-on-synthetic, test-on-real protocol, approaching genome-wide PRS methods that use $2$-$6\times$ more variants. Privacy analysis confirmed zero identical matches, near-random membership inference (AUC $\approx 0.50$), preserved linkage disequilibrium structure, and high allele frequency correlation ($r \geq 0.95$) with source data. A controlled simulation with known causal effects verified faithful recovery of the imposed genetic association structure.

</details>


### [82] [LAtte: Hyperbolic Lorentz Attention for Cross-Subject EEG Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10881)
*Johannes Burchert,Ahmad Bdeir,Tom Hanika,Lars Schmidt-Thieme,Niels Landwehr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LAtte：一种结合Lorentz注意力模块和InceptionTime编码器的新型EEG分类框架，专注于跨被试训练，通过共享基线信号和个体化适配器实现鲁棒且可泛化的分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG分类在医疗诊断和脑机接口等应用中至关重要，但由于信号信噪比低、被试间变异性高，现有方法面临挑战。传统方法主要关注单被试性能，缺乏跨被试的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Method: 提出LAtte框架：1）使用Lorentz注意力模块和InceptionTime编码器；2）通过预训练任务学习所有被试共享的基线信号，捕捉共同模式；3）采用新颖的Lorentz低秩适配器学习被试特定的嵌入，建模个体差异；4）支持跨被试训练，可微调用于个体被试或泛化到未见被试。

Result: 在三个成熟的EEG数据集上评估LAtte，相比当前最先进方法取得了显著性能提升，证明了其在跨被试EEG分类中的有效性。

Conclusion: LAtte通过整合共享基线学习和个体化适配器，成功解决了EEG分类中的信噪比低和个体差异问题，为跨被试鲁棒EEG分类提供了有效解决方案，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Electroencephalogram (EEG) classification is critical for applications ranging from medical diagnostics to brain-computer interfaces, yet it remains challenging due to the inherently low signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) and high inter-subject variability. To address these issues, we propose LAtte, a novel framework that integrates a Lorentz Attention Module with an InceptionTime-based encoder to enable robust and generalizable EEG classification. Unlike prior work, which evaluates primarily on single-subject performance, LAtte focuses on cross-subject training. First, we learn a shared baseline signal across all subjects using pretraining tasks to capture common underlying patterns. Then, we utilize novel Lorentz low-rank adapters to learn subject-specific embeddings that model individual differences. This allows us to learn a shared model that performs robustly across subjects, and can be subsequently finetuned for individual subjects or used to generalize to unseen subjects. We evaluate LAtte on three well-established EEG datasets, achieving a substantial improvement in performance over current state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [83] [Continuous Diffusion Transformers for Designing Synthetic Regulatory Elements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10885)
*Jonathan Liu,Kia Ghods*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出参数高效的扩散Transformer（DiT），用于生成200bp细胞类型特异性调控DNA序列，相比DNA-Diffusion的U-Net骨干，在更少训练周期内达到更低验证损失，并减少记忆化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进现有DNA序列生成方法，解决U-Net架构在DNA-Diffusion中训练效率低、记忆化严重的问题，开发更高效、泛化性更好的调控DNA序列生成模型。

Method: 使用Transformer去噪器替换U-Net骨干，配备2D CNN输入编码器；采用DDPO微调，以Enformer作为奖励模型；通过BLAT评估记忆化程度，并与DRAKES进行交叉验证。

Result: 在13个epoch内达到U-Net最佳验证损失（减少60倍训练），最终收敛损失降低39%；记忆化从5.3%降至1.7%；DDPO微调后预测调控活性提升38倍；交叉验证确认改进反映真实调控信号而非过拟合。

Conclusion: 参数高效的DiT架构显著提升DNA序列生成的训练效率和泛化能力，CNN编码器对性能至关重要，DDPO微调能大幅增强生成序列的调控活性，为合成生物学提供有效工具。

Abstract: We present a parameter-efficient Diffusion Transformer (DiT) for generating 200bp cell-type-specific regulatory DNA sequences. By replacing the U-Net backbone of DNA-Diffusion with a transformer denoiser equipped with a 2D CNN input encoder, our model matches the U-Net's best validation loss in 13 epochs (60$\times$ fewer) and converges 39% lower, while reducing memorization from 5.3% to 1.7% of generated sequences aligning to training data via BLAT. Ablations show the CNN encoder is essential: without it, validation loss increases 70% regardless of positional embedding choice. We further apply DDPO finetuning using Enformer as a reward model, achieving a 38$\times$ improvement in predicted regulatory activity. Cross-validation against DRAKES on an independent prediction task confirms that improvements reflect genuine regulatory signal rather than reward model overfitting.

</details>


### [84] [Dynamics-Predictive Sampling for Active RL Finetuning of Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10887)
*Yixiu Mao,Yun Qu,Qi Wang,Heming Zou,Xiangyang Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DPS是一种通过预测学习动态来高效选择训练提示的方法，显著减少RL微调中的冗余rollout计算开销


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线提示选择方法虽然能加速RL微调，但需要大量LLM rollout来筛选信息样本，计算开销巨大，甚至可能超过微调过程本身

Method: 提出动态预测采样(DPS)，将每个提示在RL微调中的解决进度建模为动态系统，使用隐马尔可夫模型表征状态转移，基于历史rollout奖励信号进行在线贝叶斯推理来估计演化状态分布，从而无需rollout密集型过滤即可高效选择提示

Result: 在数学、规划和视觉几何等多种推理任务上的实验表明，DPS显著减少了冗余rollout，加速了训练过程，并实现了更优的推理性能

Conclusion: DPS通过预测学习动态来高效选择训练提示，解决了现有在线提示选择方法计算开销大的问题，为RL微调提供了一种更高效的训练数据选择策略

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) finetuning has become a key technique for enhancing the reasoning abilities of large language models (LLMs). However, its effectiveness critically depends on the selection of training data. Recent advances underscore the importance of online prompt selection methods, which typically concentrate training on partially solved or moderately challenging examples under the current policy, thereby yielding more effective model updates. While significantly accelerating RL finetuning in terms of training steps, they also incur substantial computational overhead by requiring extensive LLM rollouts over large candidate batches to identify informative samples, an expense that can outweigh the finetuning process itself. To address this challenge, this work proposes Dynamics-Predictive Sampling (DPS), which online predicts and selects informative prompts by inferring their learning dynamics prior to costly rollouts. Specifically, we introduce a new perspective by modeling each prompt's solving progress during RL finetuning as a dynamical system, where the extent of solving is represented as the state and the transition is characterized by a hidden Markov model. Using historical rollout reward signals, we perform online Bayesian inference to estimate evolving state distributions, and the inference outcome provides a predictive prior for efficient prompt selection without rollout-intensive filtering. Empirical results across diverse reasoning tasks, including mathematics, planning, and visual geometry, demonstrate that DPS substantially reduces redundant rollouts, accelerates the training process, and achieves superior reasoning performance.

</details>


### [85] [Ergodicity in reinforcement learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10895)
*Dominik Baumann,Erfaun Noorani,Arsenii Mustafin,Xinyi Sheng,Bert Verbruggen,Arne Vanhoyweghen,Vincent Ginis,Thomas B. Schön*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文讨论非遍历奖励过程对强化学习的影响，指出当奖励过程非遍历时，基于期望值的优化目标无法反映单个智能体在部署时的长期表现，并提出了相应解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在强化学习中，通常使用奖励总和的期望值作为优化目标。然而，当奖励过程是非遍历的时，基于无限多个轨迹的期望值无法反映单个无限长轨迹的平均表现。如果关注智能体在部署时的个体表现，期望值就不是一个好的优化目标。

Method: 通过指导性示例讨论非遍历奖励过程对强化学习智能体的影响，将遍历奖励过程的概念与更广泛使用的遍历马尔可夫链概念联系起来，并提出了针对非遍历奖励动态下优化单个轨迹长期表现的现有解决方案。

Result: 论文阐明了非遍历奖励过程对强化学习目标设定的重要影响，建立了遍历奖励过程与遍历马尔可夫链之间的联系，并提供了针对非遍历奖励动态下优化个体轨迹长期性能的解决方案框架。

Conclusion: 当奖励过程非遍历时，传统的基于期望值的强化学习优化目标存在局限性，需要采用专门的方法来优化单个智能体在部署时的长期表现。论文为理解和解决这一问题提供了概念框架和现有解决方案的概述。

Abstract: In reinforcement learning, we typically aim to optimize the expected value of the sum of rewards an agent collects over a trajectory. However, if the process generating these rewards is non-ergodic, the expected value, i.e., the average over infinitely many trajectories with a given policy, is uninformative for the average over a single, but infinitely long trajectory. Thus, if we care about how the individual agent performs during deployment, the expected value is not a good optimization objective. In this paper, we discuss the impact of non-ergodic reward processes on reinforcement learning agents through an instructive example, relate the notion of ergodic reward processes to more widely used notions of ergodic Markov chains, and present existing solutions that optimize long-term performance of individual trajectories under non-ergodic reward dynamics.

</details>


### [86] [LookaheadKV: Fast and Accurate KV Cache Eviction by Glimpsing into the Future without Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10899)
*Jinwoo Ahn,Ingyu Seong,Akhil Kedia,Junhan Kim,Hyemi Jang,Kangwook Lee,Yongkweon Jeon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LookaheadKV：一种轻量级KV缓存淘汰框架，无需显式草稿生成即可利用未来响应信息，显著降低长上下文推理开销


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer大语言模型依赖KV缓存提高自回归推理效率，但缓存大小随输入序列长度线性增长，成为长上下文任务的瓶颈。现有基于重要性评分的淘汰方法需要昂贵的草稿生成来"窥视未来"，引入显著的预填充开销，限制了实际部署的实用性。

Method: 提出LookaheadKV框架，通过参数高效模块增强Transformer层，训练这些模块来准确预测真实重要性分数，无需显式草稿生成。设计确保运行时开销可忽略，同时达到比昂贵近似方法更高的准确性。

Result: 在多种模型上的长上下文理解基准测试表明，该方法不仅在各种长上下文理解任务中优于现有基线，还将淘汰成本降低高达14.5倍，显著缩短首token生成时间。

Conclusion: LookaheadKV通过参数高效模块准确预测KV重要性，实现了无需显式草稿生成的轻量级KV缓存淘汰，在保持高准确性的同时大幅降低计算开销，为长上下文任务提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Transformer-based large language models (LLMs) rely on key-value (KV) caching to avoid redundant computation during autoregressive inference. While this mechanism greatly improves efficiency, the cache size grows linearly with the input sequence length, quickly becoming a bottleneck for long-context tasks. Existing solutions mitigate this problem by evicting prompt KV that are deemed unimportant, guided by estimated importance scores. Notably, a recent line of work proposes to improve eviction quality by "glimpsing into the future", in which a draft generator produces a surrogate future response approximating the target model's true response, and this surrogate is subsequently used to estimate the importance of cached KV more accurately. However, these approaches rely on computationally expensive draft generation, which introduces substantial prefilling overhead and limits their practicality in real-world deployment. To address this challenge, we propose LookaheadKV, a lightweight eviction framework that leverages the strength of surrogate future response without requiring explicit draft generation. LookaheadKV augments transformer layers with parameter-efficient modules trained to predict true importance scores with high accuracy. Our design ensures negligible runtime overhead comparable to existing inexpensive heuristics, while achieving accuracy superior to more costly approximation methods. Extensive experiments on long-context understanding benchmarks, across a wide range of models, demonstrate that our method not only outperforms recent competitive baselines in various long-context understanding tasks, but also reduces the eviction cost by up to 14.5x, leading to significantly faster time-to-first-token. Our code is available at https://github.com/SamsungLabs/LookaheadKV.

</details>


### [87] [NCAA Bracket Prediction Using Machine Learning and Combinatorial Fusion Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10916)
*Yuanhong Wu,Isaiah Smith,Tushar Marwah,Michael Schroeter,Mohamed Rahouti,D. Frank Hsu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出使用组合融合分析（CFA）结合多个排名系统来预测体育比赛结果，通过排名-得分特征函数和认知多样性提高预测准确率，在2024年数据集上达到74.60%的准确率，优于单个最佳排名系统（73.02%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型在体育预测中取得了显著成功，但通常将体育预测视为分类任务。本文旨在通过新的视角分析体育数据，更准确地预测比赛结果，特别是探索如何通过组合多个排名系统来提高预测精度。

Method: 采用组合融合分析（CFA）这一新范式，通过排名-得分特征（RSC）函数和认知多样性（CD）来组合多个评分系统。具体方法包括：1）利用排名生成球队排名；2）使用CFA将多个公共排名系统进行融合；3）基于排名组合进行预测。

Result: 基于排名组合的球队排名预测准确率达到74.60%，这比十个流行公共排名系统中最佳的系统（73.02%）更高，证明了CFA在提高体育预测精度方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 组合融合分析（CFA）通过整合多个排名系统的优势，能够有效提高体育比赛结果预测的准确性，为体育数据分析提供了新的有效方法。

Abstract: Machine learning models have demonstrated remarkable success in sports prediction in the past years, often treating sports prediction as a classification task within the field. This paper introduces new perspectives for analyzing sports data to predict outcomes more accurately. We leverage rankings to generate team rankings for the 2024 dataset using Combinatorial Fusion Analysis (CFA), a new paradigm for combining multiple scoring systems through the rank-score characteristic (RSC) function and cognitive diversity (CD). Our result based on rank combination with respect to team ranking has an accuracy rate of $74.60\%$, which is higher than the best of the ten popular public ranking systems ($73.02\%$). This exhibits the efficacy of CFA in enhancing the precision of sports prediction through different lens.

</details>


### [88] [ECoLAD: Deployment-Oriented Evaluation for Automotive Time-Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10926)
*Kadir-Kaan Özer,René Ebeling,Markus Enzweiler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ECoLAD：面向部署的异常检测评估协议，通过计算缩减阶梯和CPU线程限制来评估时间序列异常检测器在车载环境下的实际可行性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异常检测器通常在无约束的工作站硬件上比较，但车载监控需要可预测的延迟和有限CPU并行性下的稳定行为。仅基于准确性的排行榜可能误导哪些方法在实际部署约束下仍然可行。

Method: 提出ECoLAD（异常检测效率计算阶梯），使用单调计算缩减阶梯对异构检测器家族应用机械确定的整数缩放规则和显式CPU线程限制，记录所有配置变更。通过扫描目标评分速率来表征吞吐量约束行为。

Result: 在受限的汽车遥测数据上，轻量级经典检测器在整个吞吐量扫描范围内都能维持覆盖率和检测提升度高于随机基线。几种深度方法在失去准确性之前就失去了可行性。

Conclusion: ECoLAD提供了部署导向的评估框架，揭示了在车载监控等资源受限环境中，计算效率与准确性之间的权衡关系，强调了实际部署可行性评估的重要性。

Abstract: Time-series anomaly detectors are commonly compared on workstation-class hardware under unconstrained execution. In-vehicle monitoring, however, requires predictable latency and stable behavior under limited CPU parallelism. Accuracy-only leaderboards can therefore misrepresent which methods remain feasible under deployment-relevant constraints.
  We present ECoLAD (Efficiency Compute Ladder for Anomaly Detection), a deployment-oriented evaluation protocol instantiated as an empirical study on proprietary automotive telemetry (anomaly rate ${\approx}$0.022) and complementary public benchmarks. ECoLAD applies a monotone compute-reduction ladder across heterogeneous detector families using mechanically determined, integer-only scaling rules and explicit CPU thread caps, while logging every applied configuration change. Throughput-constrained behavior is characterized by sweeping target scoring rates and reporting (i) coverage (the fraction of entities meeting the target) and (ii) the best AUC-PR achievable among measured ladder configurations satisfying the target. On constrained automotive telemetry, lightweight classical detectors sustain both coverage and detection lift above the random baseline across the full throughput sweep. Several deep methods lose feasibility before they lose accuracy.

</details>


### [89] [Quantifying Membership Disclosure Risk for Tabular Synthetic Data Using Kernel Density Estimators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10937)
*Rajdeep Pathak,Sayantee Jana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于核密度估计的实用方法，用于量化表格合成数据中的成员推断攻击风险，通过建模合成数据与训练记录之间的最近邻距离分布来实现概率成员推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据作为隐私保护的数据共享替代方案日益流行，但其隐私保障并非绝对，仍然容易受到成员推断攻击。现有方法需要计算昂贵的影子模型，缺乏实用的风险评估框架。

Method: 提出基于核密度估计的方法，建模合成数据与训练记录之间的最近邻距离分布，实现概率成员推断。设计了两种攻击模型：假设拥有训练数据特权的"真实分布攻击"和使用无真实标签辅助数据的"现实攻击"。

Result: 在四个真实世界数据集和六个合成数据生成器上的实证评估表明，该方法比现有基线方法获得更高的F1分数和更精确的风险特征，且无需计算昂贵的影子模型。

Conclusion: 该方法为量化合成数据中的成员披露风险提供了实用框架和度量标准，使数据保管人能够在发布合成数据前进行生成后风险评估。

Abstract: The use of synthetic data has become increasingly popular as a privacy-preserving alternative to sharing real datasets, especially in sensitive domains such as healthcare, finance, and demography. However, the privacy assurances of synthetic data are not absolute, and remain susceptible to membership inference attacks (MIAs), where adversaries aim to determine whether a specific individual was present in the dataset used to train the generator. In this work, we propose a practical and effective method to quantify membership disclosure risk in tabular synthetic datasets using kernel density estimators (KDEs). Our KDE-based approach models the distribution of nearest-neighbour distances between synthetic data and the training records, allowing probabilistic inference of membership and enabling robust evaluation via ROC curves. We propose two attack models: a 'True Distribution Attack', which assumes privileged access to training data, and a more realistic, implementable 'Realistic Attack' that uses auxiliary data without true membership labels. Empirical evaluations across four real-world datasets and six synthetic data generators demonstrate that our method consistently achieves higher F1 scores and sharper risk characterization than a prior baseline approach, without requiring computationally expensive shadow models. The proposed method provides a practical framework and metric for quantifying membership disclosure risk in synthetic data, which enables data custodians to conduct a post-generation risk assessment prior to releasing their synthetic datasets for downstream use. The datasets and codes for this study are available at https://github.com/PyCoder913/MIA-KDE.

</details>


### [90] [Safe RLHF Beyond Expectation: Stochastic Dominance for Universal Spectral Risk Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10938)
*Yaswanth Chittepu,Ativ Joshi,Rajarshi Bhattacharjee,Scott Niekum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RAD框架，用一阶随机占优约束替代期望成本约束，通过最优传输框架实现风险敏感的强化学习对齐


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于期望成本约束的RLHF方法仅关注成本分布的单一统计量，无法处理分布不确定性，特别是在重尾分布或罕见灾难事件场景下。期望约束无法有效控制尾部风险和潜在分布外故障，而随机占优方法能够比较整个成本分布而非仅平均值

Method: 提出RAD框架，用一阶随机占优约束替代标量期望成本约束。通过最优传输框架比较目标策略与参考策略的成本分布，使用熵正则化和Sinkhorn迭代获得可微分且计算高效的目标函数。引入分位数加权FSD约束，证明加权FSD普遍控制一类谱风险度量

Result: 实验结果表明，RAD在无害性方面优于基线方法，同时在有帮助性方面保持竞争力，在分布外无害性评估中表现出更强的鲁棒性

Conclusion: RAD框架通过随机占优约束提供了原则性的风险敏感对齐方法，能够直接控制尾部风险，并通过分位数加权函数为调整模型风险配置提供了机制。该方法在保持有帮助性的同时提高了无害性和鲁棒性

Abstract: Safe Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) typically enforces safety through expected cost constraints, but the expectation captures only a single statistic of the cost distribution and fails to account for distributional uncertainty, particularly under heavy tails or rare catastrophic events. This limitation is problematic when robustness and risk sensitivity are critical. Stochastic dominance offers a principled alternative by comparing entire cost distributions rather than just their averages, enabling direct control over tail risks and potential out-of-distribution failures that expectation-based constraints may overlook. In this work, we propose Risk-sensitive Alignment via Dominance (RAD), a novel alignment framework that replaces scalar expected cost constraints with First-Order Stochastic Dominance (FSD) constraints. We operationalize this constraint by comparing the target policy's cost distribution to that of a reference policy within an Optimal Transport (OT) framework, using entropic regularization and Sinkhorn iterations to obtain a differentiable and computationally efficient objective for stable end-to-end optimization. Furthermore, we introduce quantile-weighted FSD constraints and show that weighted FSD universally controls a broad class of Spectral Risk Measures (SRMs), so that improvements under weighted dominance imply guaranteed improvements in the corresponding spectral risk. This provides a principled mechanism for tuning a model's risk profile via the quantile weighting function. Empirical results demonstrate that RAD improves harmlessness over baselines while remaining competitive in helpfulness, and exhibits greater robustness on out-of-distribution harmlessness evaluations.

</details>


### [91] [Ranking Reasoning LLMs under Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10960)
*Mohsen Hariri,Michael Hinczewski,Jing Ma,Vipin Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Scorio是一个用于测试时扩展场景下推理LLM排名的开源库，提供多种统计排名方法，在数学竞赛基准测试中验证了其有效性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时扩展通过每个提示采样多个输出来评估推理LLM，但在此机制下的模型排名方法研究不足，需要形式化密集基准排名并开发可靠工具

Method: 提出Scorio库，实现配对比较模型、项目反应理论模型、投票规则、图和谱方法等统计排名方法，在数学竞赛基准上验证

Result: 在4个数学竞赛基准上测试20个推理模型，大多数全试验排名与贝叶斯黄金标准高度一致（平均Kendall's τ_b = 0.93-0.95），19-34种方法恢复完全相同的排序；单试验机制下最佳方法达到τ_b ≈ 0.86

Conclusion: Scorio为高预算和低预算测试时扩展提供了可靠的排名方法，使用贪婪解码作为经验先验可减少方差但可能引入偏差，开源库有助于标准化模型评估

Abstract: Test-time scaling evaluates reasoning LLMs by sampling multiple outputs per prompt, but ranking models in this regime remains underexplored. We formalize dense benchmark ranking under test-time scaling and introduce Scorio, a library that implements statistical ranking methods such as paired-comparison models, item response theory (IRT) models, voting rules, and graph- and spectral-based methods. Across $20$ reasoning models on four Olympiad-style math benchmarks (AIME'24, AIME'25, HMMT'25, and BrUMO'25; up to $N=80$ trials), most full-trial rankings agree closely with the Bayesian gold standard $\mathrm{Bayes}_{\mathcal{U}}@80$ (mean Kendall's $τ_b = 0.93$--$0.95$), and $19$--$34$ methods recover exactly the same ordering. In the single-trial regime, the best methods reach $τ_b \approx 0.86$. Using greedy decoding as an empirical prior ($\mathrm{Bayes}_{\mathbf{R}_0}@N$) reduces variance at $N=1$ by $16$--$52\%$, but can bias rankings when greedy and stochastic sampling disagree. These results identify reliable ranking methods for both high- and low-budget test-time scaling. We release Scorio as an open-source library at https://github.com/mohsenhariri/scorio.

</details>


### [92] [Bio-Inspired Self-Supervised Learning for Wrist-worn IMU Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10961)
*Prithviraj Tarale,Kiet Chu,Abhishek Varghese,Kai-Chun Liu,Maxwell A Xu,Mohit Iyyer,Sunghoon I. Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于运动控制子运动理论的新型标记化策略，将手腕加速度计信号中的运动片段作为标记，通过掩码运动片段重建预训练Transformer编码器，在人体活动识别任务上超越了现有自监督学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴加速度计虽然实现了大规模健康监测，但标记数据稀缺限制了鲁棒人体活动表征学习。现有自监督学习方法将传感器数据视为非结构化时间序列，忽略了人体运动的生物结构，而作者认为这是有效人体活动识别的关键因素。

Method: 基于运动控制的子运动理论，提出新型标记化策略：将连续手腕运动视为基本子运动函数的叠加，定义运动片段（由有限序列子运动组成）作为标记。使用Transformer编码器通过掩码运动片段重建进行预训练，建模运动片段的时间依赖关系。

Result: 在NHANES语料库（约28k小时，11k参与者，10M窗口）上预训练后，该方法在六个主体不相交的人体活动识别基准测试中超越了强可穿戴自监督学习基线，并在数据稀缺场景中表现出更强的数据效率。

Conclusion: 通过将生物启发的运动片段作为标记进行自监督预训练，能够学习更有效的人体活动表征，克服了传统方法忽略运动生物结构的局限性，为可穿戴传感器数据分析提供了新方向。

Abstract: Wearable accelerometers have enabled large-scale health and wellness monitoring, yet learning robust human-activity representations has been constrained by the scarcity of labeled data. While self-supervised learning offers a potential remedy, existing approaches treat sensor streams as unstructured time series, overlooking the underlying biological structure of human movement, a factor we argue is critical for effective Human Activity Recognition (HAR). We introduce a novel tokenization strategy grounded in the submovement theory of motor control, which posits that continuous wrist motion is composed of superposed elementary basis functions called submovements. We define our token as the movement segment, a unit of motion composed of a finite sequence of submovements that is readily extractable from wrist accelerometer signals. By treating these segments as tokens, we pretrain a Transformer encoder via masked movement-segment reconstruction to model the temporal dependencies of movement segments, shifting the learning focus beyond local waveform morphology. Pretrained on the NHANES corpus (approximately 28k hours; approximately 11k participants; approximately 10M windows), our representations outperform strong wearable SSL baselines across six subject-disjoint HAR benchmarks. Furthermore, they demonstrate stronger data efficiency in data-scarce settings. Code and pretrained weights will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [93] [Factorized Neural Implicit DMD for Parametric Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10995)
*Siyuan Chen,Zhecheng Wang,Yixin Chen,Yue Chang,Peter Yichen Chen,Eitan Grinspun,Jonathan Panuelos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于物理编码神经场的Koopman算子谱分解方法，用于学习动力系统的参数化流，支持长期预测、参数空间插值和谱分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值求解器在处理高维状态空间和非线性动力学时计算成本高，不适合实时分析和控制。即使有物理先验（如偏微分方程），也需要一种能够支持长期推演、未见参数泛化和谱分析的数据驱动方法。

Method: 提出物理编码神经场参数化的Koopman算子谱分解方法。与物理约束神经场（拟合单个解曲面）和神经算子（在固定时间范围近似解算子）不同，该方法学习因子化的流算子，将空间模式和时间演化解耦，从而揭示底层特征值、模态和稳定性。

Result: 在多种动力学问题上展示了方法的有效性，能够准确预测复杂的时空现象，同时提供对系统动态行为的深入理解，支持稳定的长期推演和参数空间插值。

Conclusion: 该方法通过Koopman算子的谱分解提供了一种强大的框架，用于学习动力系统的参数化流，实现了长期预测、泛化和谱分析能力，为复杂物理系统的建模和控制提供了新工具。

Abstract: A data-driven, model-free approach to modeling the temporal evolution of physical systems mitigates the need for explicit knowledge of the governing equations. Even when physical priors such as partial differential equations are available, such systems often reside in high-dimensional state spaces and exhibit nonlinear dynamics, making traditional numerical solvers computationally expensive and ill-suited for real-time analysis and control. Consider the problem of learning a parametric flow of a dynamical system: with an initial field and a set of physical parameters, we aim to predict the system's evolution over time in a way that supports long-horizon rollouts, generalization to unseen parameters, and spectral analysis.
  We propose a physics-coded neural field parameterization of the Koopman operator's spectral decomposition. Unlike a physics-constrained neural field, which fits a single solution surface, and neural operators, which directly approximate the solution operator at fixed time horizons, our model learns a factorized flow operator that decouples spatial modes and temporal evolution. This structure exposes underlying eigenvalues, modes, and stability of the underlying physical process to enable stable long-term rollouts, interpolation across parameter spaces, and spectral analysis. We demonstrate the efficacy of our method on a range of dynamics problems, showcasing its ability to accurately predict complex spatiotemporal phenomena while providing insights into the system's dynamic behavior.

</details>


### [94] [Cross-Species Transfer Learning for Electrophysiology-to-Transcriptomics Mapping in Cortical GABAergic Interneurons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11000)
*Theo Schwider,Ramin Ramezani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究复制并扩展了Gouwens等人(2020)的电生理学-转录组学框架，使用公开的Allen Institute Patch-seq数据，开发了基于注意力的BiLSTM模型，实现了跨物种（小鼠到人类）的迁移学习，提升了人类神经元亚类预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立可复现的电生理学-转录组学分析框架，开发能够直接处理结构化电生理特征、避免特征工程、提供特征家族级可解释性的深度学习模型，并探索跨物种迁移学习在神经元亚类预测中的应用价值。

Method: 1) 使用Allen Institute公开的Patch-seq数据集（小鼠视觉皮层3,699个神经元，人类新皮层506个神经元）；2) 采用标准化电生理特征和稀疏PCA复现原始研究；3) 开发基于注意力的BiLSTM模型，直接处理IPFX特征家族表示；4) 实施跨物种迁移学习：在小鼠数据上预训练，在人类数据上微调；5) 使用类别平衡随机森林作为特征工程基线。

Result: 1) 成功复现了小鼠数据中主要的类别水平分离；2) 随机森林基线在小鼠数据中表现强劲，在人类数据中表现有所降低但仍具信息性；3) 注意力BiLSTM模型能够匹配特征工程基线性能；4) 跨物种迁移学习（小鼠预训练+人类微调）相比仅使用人类数据训练，显著提升了人类4类任务的宏观F1分数。

Conclusion: 该研究证实了Gouwens流程在小鼠数据中的可复现性，证明了序列模型能够达到特征工程基线的性能，并展示了从小鼠到人类的迁移学习能够为人类神经元亚类预测带来可测量的性能提升，为跨物种神经科学研究提供了有价值的分析框架。

Abstract: Single-cell electrophysiological recordings provide a powerful window into neuronal functional diversity and offer an interpretable route for linking intrinsic physiology to transcriptomic identity. Here, we replicate and extend the electrophysiology-to-transcriptomics framework introduced by Gouwens et al. (2020) using publicly available Allen Institute Patch-seq datasets from both mouse and human cortex. We focus on GABAergic inhibitory interneurons to target a subclass structure (Lamp5, Pvalb, Sst, Vip) that is comparable and conserved across species. After quality control, we analyzed 3,699 mouse visual cortex neurons and 506 human neocortical neurons from neurosurgical resections. Using standardized electrophysiological features and sparse PCA, we reproduced the major class-level separations reported in the original mouse study. For supervised prediction, a class-balanced random forest provided a strong feature-engineered baseline in mouse data and a reduced but still informative baseline in human data. We then developed an attention-based BiLSTM that operates directly on the structured IPFX feature-family representation, avoiding sPCA and providing feature-family-level interpretability via learned attention weights. Finally, we evaluated a cross-species transfer setting in which the sequence model is pretrained on mouse data and fine-tuned on human data for an aligned 4-class task, improving human macro-F1 relative to a human-only training baseline. Together, these results confirm reproducibility of the Gouwens pipeline in mouse data, demonstrate that sequence models can match feature-engineered baselines, and show that mouse-to-human transfer learning can provide measurable gains for human subclass prediction.

</details>


### [95] [Leech Lattice Vector Quantization for Efficient LLM Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11021)
*Tycho F. A. van der Ouderaa,Mart van Baalen,Paul Whatmough,Markus Nagel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Leech Lattice Vector Quantization (LLVQ) 是一种基于24维Leech晶格的向量量化方法，通过高效索引和并行化解量化内核，实现了无需显式码本存储的LLM量化，在性能上超越了现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标量量化受信息论限制，而向量量化虽能突破这些限制但需要昂贵的查找机制或显式码本存储。晶格方法通过结构化密集填充解决此问题，Leech晶格在24维具有最优球体填充和接触配置特性，是最高维具有此类最优性质的晶格。

Method: 扩展基于扩展Golay码构造的现有搜索算法：1) 支持索引，实现比特串与晶格点之间的双向转换而无需物化码本；2) 允许在Leech晶格壳层并集上进行角度搜索；3) 提出完全可并行化的解量化内核。这些组件共同构成了实用的LLVQ算法。

Result: LLVQ在LLM量化方面实现了最先进的性能，超越了近期方法如Quip#、QTIP和PVQ。结果表明高维晶格对于可扩展、理论基础的模型压缩具有重要意义。

Conclusion: Leech晶格向量量化为LLM压缩提供了实用且高性能的解决方案，通过利用高维晶格的数学特性，实现了无需显式码本存储的高效量化，展示了高维晶格在模型压缩中的重要性。

Abstract: Scalar quantization of large language models (LLMs) is fundamentally limited by information-theoretic bounds. While vector quantization (VQ) overcomes these limits by encoding blocks of parameters jointly, practical implementations must avoid the need for expensive lookup mechanisms or other explicit codebook storage. Lattice approaches address this through highly structured and dense packing. This paper explores the Leech lattice, which, with its optimal sphere packing and kissing configurations at 24 dimensions, is the highest dimensional lattice known with such optimal properties. To make the Leech lattice usable for LLM quantization, we extend an existing search algorithm based on the extended Golay code construction, to i) support indexing, enabling conversion to and from bitstrings without materializing the codebook, ii) allow angular search over union of Leech lattice shells, iii) propose fully-parallelisable dequantization kernel. Together this yields a practical algorithm, namely Leech Lattice Vector Quantization (LLVQ). LLVQ delivers state-of-the-art LLM quantization performance, outperforming recent methods such as Quip\#, QTIP, and PVQ. These results highlight the importance of high-dimensional lattices for scalable, theoretically grounded model compression.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [96] [Targeted Bit-Flip Attacks on LLM-Based Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10042)
*Jialai Wang,Ya Wen,Zhongmou Liu,Yuxiao Wu,Bingyi He,Zongpeng Li,Ee-Chien Chang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Flip-Agent：首个针对LLM智能体的定向比特翻转攻击框架，能够操纵最终输出和工具调用，揭示了LLM智能体系统的关键安全漏洞


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前针对单步推理模型（如图像分类器）的比特翻转攻击研究，未能覆盖具有多阶段流水线和外部工具的LLM智能体系统，这些系统存在新的攻击面需要探索

Method: 提出Flip-Agent框架，通过硬件故障操纵模型参数，针对LLM智能体的多阶段流程进行定向比特翻转攻击，能够同时操纵最终输出和工具调用

Result: 实验表明Flip-Agent在真实世界智能体任务上显著优于现有定向比特翻转攻击方法，成功揭示了LLM智能体系统的关键安全漏洞

Conclusion: LLM智能体系统面临严重的比特翻转攻击威胁，Flip-Agent框架展示了这类攻击的有效性，强调了需要加强LLM智能体系统的安全防护措施

Abstract: Targeted bit-flip attacks (BFAs) exploit hardware faults to manipulate model parameters, posing a significant security threat. While prior work targets single-step inference models (e.g., image classifiers), LLM-based agents with multi-stage pipelines and external tools present new attack surfaces, which remain unexplored. This work introduces Flip-Agent, the first targeted BFA framework for LLM-based agents, manipulating both final outputs and tool invocations. Our experiments show that Flip-Agent significantly outperforms existing targeted BFAs on real-world agent tasks, revealing a critical vulnerability in LLM-based agent systems.

</details>


### [97] [OAuthHub: Mitigating OAuth Data Overaccess through a Local Data Hub](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10056)
*Qiyu Li,Yuhe Tian,Haojian Jin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: OAuthHub是一个开发框架，利用用户个人设备作为OAuth数据共享的中介控制器，通过声明式数据访问和三种访问模式来减少不必要的用户数据暴露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前OAuth服务提供商（如Google、Microsoft）仅提供有限的粗粒度数据访问权限，导致第三方OAuth应用即使开发者希望最小化数据访问，也不得不获取超出必要范围的用户数据。

Method: OAuthHub框架包含三个关键创新：1）认识到大多数OAuth应用不需要持续的数据访问，只需在三个明确定义的时刻访问数据（安装时、响应用户操作时、预定间隔时）；2）提供需要明确声明预期数据访问的开发框架，通过间歇可用的个人设备支持三种常见访问模式；3）建立跨提供商的集中运行时权限模型来管理OAuth访问。

Result: 在PC和手机上使用三个真实应用进行评估，发现OAuthHub需要适度的应用代码修改，且性能开销可忽略。对18名开发者的研究表明，使用OAuthHub完成编程任务显著更快（9.1分钟 vs 18.0分钟），代码量更少（4.7行 vs 15.8行）。

Conclusion: OAuthHub通过利用个人设备作为中介控制器，提供声明式数据访问框架，有效解决了OAuth应用中过度数据访问的问题，显著提高了开发效率并减少了不必要的用户数据暴露。

Abstract: Most OAuth service providers, such as Google and Microsoft, offer only a limited range of coarse-grained data access. As a result, third-party OAuth applications often end up accessing more user data than necessary, even if their developers want to minimize data access. We present OAuthHub, a development framework that leverages users' personal devices as the intermediary controller for OAuth-based data sharing between cloud services. The key innovations of OAuthHub are: (1) the insight that discretionary data access is largely unnecessary for most OAuth apps, which typically only require access at three well-defined moments-during installation, in response to user actions, and at scheduled intervals; (2) a development framework that requires explicit declarations of intended data access and supports the three common access patterns through intermittently available personal devices; and (3) a centralized runtime permission model for managing OAuth access across providers. We evaluated OAuthHub with three real-world apps on both PCs and mobile phones and found that OAuthHub requires moderate changes to the application code and imposes insignificant performance overheads. Our study with 18 developers showed that participants completed programming tasks significantly faster (9.1 vs. 18.0 minutes) with less code (4.7 vs. 15.8 lines) using OAuthHub than conventional OAuth APIs.

</details>


### [98] [SBOMs into Agentic AIBOMs: Schema Extensions, Agentic Orchestration, and Reproducibility Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10057)
*Petar Radanliev,Carsten Maple,Omar Santos,Kayvan Atefi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出AI驱动的物料清单（AIBOMs），将静态SBOMs扩展为支持动态执行环境、运行时行为和漏洞可利用性评估的主动溯源构件


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统软件物料清单（SBOMs）只能提供静态依赖清单，无法捕获运行时行为、环境漂移或漏洞可利用性上下文，限制了软件供应链安全在动态执行条件下的可重现性和漏洞评估能力

Method: 提出基于多智能体架构的agentic AIBOM框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 基线环境重建智能体（MCP），(2) 运行时依赖和漂移监控智能体（A2A），(3) 策略感知漏洞和VEX推理智能体（AGNTCY）。这些智能体结合运行时执行证据、依赖使用和环境缓解措施，使用ISO/IEC 20153:2025 CSAF v2.0语义生成上下文可利用性断言，并对CycloneDX和SPDX进行最小化、标准对齐的模式扩展

Result: 在异构分析工作负载上的评估表明，相比现有溯源系统，该框架在运行时依赖捕获、可重现性保真度和漏洞解释稳定性方面均有改进，且计算开销较低。消融研究证实每个智能体都提供了确定性自动化无法实现的独特能力

Conclusion: AIBOMs通过自主、策略约束的推理将SBOMs扩展为主动溯源构件，能够生成结构化VEX断言而非强制执行动作，在保持互操作性的同时显著提升了软件供应链安全在动态环境中的能力

Abstract: Software supply-chain security requires provenance mechanisms that support reproducibility and vulnerability assessment under dynamic execution conditions. Conventional Software Bills of Materials (SBOMs) provide static dependency inventories but cannot capture runtime behaviour, environment drift, or exploitability context. This paper introduces agentic Artificial Intelligence Bills of Materials (AIBOMs), extending SBOMs into active provenance artefacts through autonomous, policy-constrained reasoning. We present an agentic AIBOM framework based on a multi-agent architecture comprising (i) a baseline environment reconstruction agent (MCP), (ii) a runtime dependency and drift-monitoring agent (A2A), and (iii) a policy-aware vulnerability and VEX reasoning agent (AGNTCY). These agents generate contextual exploitability assertions by combining runtime execution evidence, dependency usage, and environmental mitigations with ISO/IEC 20153:2025 Common Security Advisory Framework (CSAF) v2.0 semantics. Exploitability is expressed via structured VEX assertions rather than enforcement actions. The framework introduces minimal, standards-aligned schema extensions to CycloneDX and SPDX, capturing execution context, dependency evolution, and agent decision provenance while preserving interoperability. Evaluation across heterogeneous analytical workloads demonstrates improved runtime dependency capture, reproducibility fidelity, and stability of vulnerability interpretation compared with established provenance systems, with low computational overhead. Ablation studies confirm that each agent contributes distinct capabilities unavailable through deterministic automation.

</details>


### [99] [Building Privacy-and-Security-Focused Federated Learning Infrastructure for Global Multi-Centre Healthcare Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10063)
*Fan Zhang,Daniel Kreuter,Javier Fernandez-Marques,BloodCounts Consortium,Gregory Verghese,Bernard Butler,Nicholas Lane,Suthesh Sivapalaratnam,Joseph Taylor,Norbert C. J. de Wit,Nicholas S. Gleadall,Carola-Bibiane Schönlieb,Michael Roberts*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FLA³是一个治理感知的联邦学习平台，通过运行时策略执行实现监管义务，在保护数据隐私的同时确保跨机构医疗AI部署的可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨机构医疗研究需要多样化临床数据，但跨境数据共享受隐私法规严格限制。现有联邦学习框架多为概念验证，缺乏对治理风险（如未经授权参与、滥用、缺乏问责）的充分应对，特别是缺少可执行的认证、授权和计费机制，限制了临床实际部署。

Method: 提出FLA³平台，将XACML兼容的基于属性的访问控制、加密计费和研究所范围的联邦直接集成到联邦学习编排层，通过运行时策略执行来维护机构主权和协议遵守。平台采用可扩展访问控制标记语言实现属性基访问控制。

Result: 通过两项互补研究进行评估：1) 在四个国家（英国、荷兰、印度、冈比亚）五个BloodCounts!联盟机构部署平台基础设施，证明操作可行性；2) 使用INTERVAL研究中25个中心的54,446个样本（35,315名受试者）的全血计数数据进行模拟联邦，评估临床效用。结果显示FLA³在严格实施治理约束的同时，实现了与集中训练相当的预测性能。

Conclusion: 可执行的治理可以作为一等隐私保护控制机制，提高跨司法管辖区医疗部署中可扩展人工智能的可信度，为实际临床部署提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Collaborative healthcare research across multiple institutions increasingly requires diverse clinical datasets, but cross-border data sharing is strictly constrained by privacy regulations. Federated learning (FL) enables model training while keeping data local; however, many existing frameworks remain proof-of-concept and do not adequately address governance risks such as unauthorised participation, misuse, and lack of accountability. In particular, enforceable mechanisms for authentication, authorisation, and accounting (AAA) are often missing, limiting real-world clinical deployment. This paper presents FLA$^3$ (Federated Learning with Authentication, Authorisation, and Accounting), a governance-aware federated learning platform that operationalises regulatory obligations through runtime policy enforcement. FLA$^3$ integrates eXtensible Access Control Markup Language (XACML) compliant attribute-based access control (ABAC), cryptographic accounting, and study-scoped federation directly into the federated learning orchestration layer to enforce institutional sovereignty and protocol adherence. We evaluate FLA$^3$ through two complementary studies. First, we demonstrate operational feasibility by deploying the platform infrastructure across five BloodCounts! Consortium institutions in four countries: United Kingdom, Netherlands, India, and The Gambia. Second, we assess clinical utility using simulated federation of full blood count (FBC) data from 54,446 samples from 35,315 subjects across 25 centres in the INTERVAL study. Results show that FLA$^3$ achieves predictive performance comparable to centralised training while strictly enforcing governance constraints. These results show that enforceable governance can function as a first-class privacy-preserving control, improving trustworthiness for scalable artificial intelligence (AI) in cross-jurisdictional healthcare deployments.

</details>


### [100] [ADVERSA: Measuring Multi-Turn Guardrail Degradation and Judge Reliability in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10068)
*Harry Owiredu-Ashley*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ADVERSA是一个自动化红队框架，通过连续多轮对抗交互评估LLM安全防护的动态退化过程，而非传统的单轮二元评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全评估主要关注单次提示攻击的二元结果，无法捕捉在持续对抗交互下安全防护的动态退化过程，需要更精细的评估框架。

Method: 使用微调的70B攻击模型（ADVERSA-Red）消除攻击方安全拒绝问题，采用结构化5点评分标准评估受害者响应，通过三法官共识架构测量法官可靠性，在三个前沿受害者模型上进行15次最多10轮的对抗对话实验。

Result: 观察到26.7%的越狱率，平均越狱轮数为1.25轮，表明成功越狱集中在早期轮次而非通过持续压力积累；记录了法官间一致性率、自我评分倾向、攻击模型漂移和攻击拒绝等关键发现。

Conclusion: ADVERSA框架提供了更精细的LLM安全防护动态评估方法，揭示了攻击模型漂移和攻击拒绝等先前被低估的混淆因素，为持续对抗交互下的安全评估建立了新标准。

Abstract: Most adversarial evaluations of large language model (LLM) safety assess single prompts and report binary pass/fail outcomes, which fails to capture how safety properties evolve under sustained adversarial interaction. We present ADVERSA, an automated red-teaming framework that measures guardrail degradation dynamics as continuous per-round compliance trajectories rather than discrete jailbreak events. ADVERSA uses a fine-tuned 70B attacker model (ADVERSA-Red, Llama-3.1-70B-Instruct with QLoRA) that eliminates the attacker-side safety refusals that render off-the-shelf models unreliable as attackers, scoring victim responses on a structured 5-point rubric that treats partial compliance as a distinct measurable state.
  We report a controlled experiment across three frontier victim models (Claude Opus 4.6, Gemini 3.1 Pro, GPT-5.2) using a triple-judge consensus architecture in which judge reliability is measured as a first-class research outcome rather than assumed. Across 15 conversations of up to 10 adversarial rounds, we observe a 26.7% jailbreak rate with an average jailbreak round of 1.25, suggesting that in this evaluation setting, successful jailbreaks were concentrated in early rounds rather than accumulating through sustained pressure. We document inter-judge agreement rates, self-judge scoring tendencies, attacker drift as a failure mode in fine-tuned attackers deployed out of their training distribution, and attacker refusals as a previously-underreported confound in victim resistance measurement. All limitations are stated explicitly. Attack prompts are withheld per responsible disclosure policy; all other experimental artifacts are released.

</details>


### [101] [Why LLMs Fail: A Failure Analysis and Partial Success Measurement for Automated Security Patch Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10072)
*Amir Al-Maamari*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: LLM生成的安全补丁在Java漏洞修复中效果有限，仅24.8%完全正确，51.4%同时存在安全和功能问题，主要失败原因是语义误解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大型语言模型在自动程序修复中显示出潜力，但其在安全漏洞修复方面的有效性尚未得到充分评估。本研究旨在系统分析LLM生成的安全补丁在实际Java漏洞修复中的表现。

Method: 使用Vul4J基准测试中的64个Java漏洞，生成319个LLM安全补丁。采用三轴评估方法：编译测试、基于PoV测试的安全验证、基于测试套件的功能验证。提出安全修复分数(SRS)量化修复效果。

Result: 仅24.8%的补丁完全正确，51.4%同时存在安全和功能问题。LLM在保持功能方面表现较好(平均SRS 0.832)，但在安全修复方面表现较差(平均SRS 0.251)。漏洞类型对修复难度有显著影响，修复率从输入验证的0%到无限循环的45%不等。

Conclusion: LLM生成的安全补丁存在严重的语义误解问题，导致修复策略错误。虽然能保持代码功能，但在安全修复方面表现不佳。不同漏洞类型的修复难度差异显著，表明LLM安全补丁在部署前需要严格的验证。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) show promise for Automated Program Repair (APR), yet their effectiveness on security vulnerabilities remains poorly characterized. This study analyzes 319 LLM-generated security patchesacross 64 Java vulnerabilities from the Vul4J benchmark. Using tri-axis evaluation (compilation, security via PoV tests, functionality via test suites), the analysis reveals that only 24.8% of patches achieve full correctness, while 51.4% fail both security and functionality. The dominant failure mode is semantic misunderstanding: LLMs produce syntactically valid code but apply incorrect repair strategies. The proposed Security Repair Score (SRS) quantifies this gap, showing LLMs preserve functionality (mean 0.832) but struggle with security (mean 0.251). Vulnerability type strongly predicts difficulty, with fix rates ranging from 0% (input validation) to 45% (infinite loop). These findings demonstrate that LLM security patches require rigorous validation before deployment.

</details>


### [102] [TASER: Task-Aware Spectral Energy Refine for Backdoor Suppression in UAV Swarms Decentralized Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10075)
*Sizhe Huang,Shujie Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TASER：首个利用频谱集中性而非复杂异常检测的高效后门防御框架，通过结构性地破坏后门任务来缓解隐蔽攻击


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机去中心化联邦学习中，现有基于异常检测的防御方法对精心设计的后门攻击仍然脆弱，且缺乏全局协调和有限资源使得异常检测不切实际。梯度频谱分析提供了有前景的替代方案，但先前工作主要利用低频系数进行成对比较，忽略了后门梯度的内在频谱特性。

Method: 提出任务感知频谱能量精炼（TASER）——一个去中心化防御框架。通过分析现有隐蔽攻击，发现攻击者越努力模仿良性行为，频谱集中性越明显。TASER通过保留主任务相关的频率系数并丢弃其他系数来抑制后门任务，从而结构性地破坏后门任务。

Result: TASER能够有效抵御绕过异常检测防御的隐蔽后门攻击，攻击成功率低于20%，准确率损失小于5%。

Conclusion: TASER是首个利用频谱集中性而非复杂异常检测的高效后门防御框架，为无人机去中心化联邦学习中的后门攻击防御提供了实用且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: As backdoor attacks in UAV-based decentralized federated learning (DFL) grow increasingly stealthy and sophisticated, existing defenses have likewise escalated in complexity. Yet these defenses, which rely heavily on outlier detection, remain vulnerable to carefully crafted backdoors. In UAV-DFL, the lack of global coordination and limited resources further render outlier-based defenses impractical. Against this backdrop, gradient spectral analysis offers a promising alternative. While prior work primarily leverages low-frequency coefficients for pairwise comparisons, it neglects to analyze the intrinsic spectral characteristics of backdoor gradients. Through empirical analysis of existing stealthy attacks, we reveal a key insight: the more effort attackers invest in mimicking benign behaviors, the more distinct the spectral concentration becomes. Motivated by this, we propose Task-Aware Spectral Energy Refine (TASER) -- a decentralized defense framework. To our knowledge, this is the first efficient backdoor defense that utilizes spectral concentration instead of complex outlier detection, enabling mitigation of stealthy attacks by structurally disrupting the backdoor task. To suppress the backdoor task, TASER preserves main-task-relevant frequency coefficients and discards others. We provide theoretical guarantees and demonstrate through experiments that TASER remains effective against stealthy backdoor attacks that bypass outlier-based defenses, achieving attack success rate below 20% and accuracy loss under 5%.

</details>


### [103] [Amnesia: Adversarial Semantic Layer Specific Activation Steering in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10080)
*Ali Raza,Gurang Gupta,Nikolay Matyunin,Jibesh Patra*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Amnesia是一种轻量级的激活空间对抗攻击，通过操纵Transformer内部状态来绕过开源大语言模型的安全机制，无需微调即可生成有害内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有RLHF等技术对齐LLM输出与人类价值观，但现有安全措施是否足以防止有害内容生成仍不确定。需要评估开源LLM安全机制的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出Amnesia攻击方法，在激活空间进行对抗攻击，操纵Transformer内部状态以绕过安全机制。该方法无需微调或额外训练，直接作用于模型内部表示。

Result: 实验表明Amnesia能有效绕过最先进开源LLM的安全防护，在基准数据集上诱导多种反社会行为，成功生成有害内容。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了开源LLM安全机制的脆弱性，强调需要更鲁棒的安全措施，并突显持续研究防止LLM滥用的重要性。

Abstract: Warning: This article includes red-teaming experiments, which contain examples of compromised LLM responses that may be offensive or upsetting.
  Large Language Models (LLMs) have the potential to create harmful content, such as generating sophisticated phishing emails and assisting in writing code of harmful computer viruses. Thus, it is crucial to ensure their safe and responsible response generation. To reduce the risk of generating harmful or irresponsible content, researchers have developed techniques such as reinforcement learning with human feedback to align LLM's outputs with human values and preferences. However, it is still undetermined whether such measures are sufficient to prevent LLMs from generating interesting responses. In this study, we propose Amnesia, a lightweight activation-space adversarial attack that manipulates internal transformer states to bypass existing safety mechanisms in open-weight LLMs. Through experimental analysis on state-of-the-art, open-weight LLMs, we demonstrate that our attack effectively circumvents existing safeguards, enabling the generation of harmful content without the need for any fine-tuning or additional training. Our experiments on benchmark datasets show that the proposed attack can induce various antisocial behaviors in LLMs. These findings highlight the urgent need for more robust security measures in open-weight LLMs and underscore the importance of continued research to prevent their potential misuse.

</details>


### [104] [Multi-Stream Perturbation Attack: Breaking Safety Alignment of Thinking LLMs Through Concurrent Task Interference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10091)
*Fan Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出多流扰动攻击方法，通过交织多个任务流来干扰LLMs的思维模式，从而绕过安全机制并导致思维过程崩溃或重复输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs思维模式的广泛应用增强了复杂任务处理能力，但引入了新的安全风险。当受到越狱攻击时，逐步推理过程可能导致模型生成更详细的有害内容。作者观察到思维模式在处理交织的多个任务时表现出独特漏洞。

Method: 提出多流扰动攻击，通过在单个提示中交织多个任务流来生成叠加干扰。设计了三种扰动策略：多流交织（通过并发任务交织干扰思维过程）、反转扰动（字符反转）和形状变换（格式约束）。

Result: 在JailbreakBench、AdvBench和HarmBench数据集上，该方法在包括Qwen3系列、DeepSeek、Qwen3-Max和Gemini 2.5 Flash等主流模型上实现了超过大多数方法的攻击成功率。实验显示思维崩溃率和响应重复率分别达到17%和60%。

Conclusion: 多流扰动攻击不仅能绕过LLMs的安全机制，还能导致思维过程崩溃或重复输出，揭示了思维模式在处理交织任务时的安全漏洞。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of thinking mode in large language models (LLMs) has significantly enhanced complex task processing capabilities while introducing new security risks. When subjected to jailbreak attacks, the step-by-step reasoning process may cause models to generate more detailed harmful content. We observe that thinking mode exhibits unique vulnerabilities when processing interleaved multiple tasks. Based on this observation, we propose multi-stream perturbation attack, which generates superimposed interference by interweaving multiple task streams within a single prompt. We design three perturbation strategies: multi-stream interleaving, inversion perturbation, and shape transformation, which disrupt the thinking process through concurrent task interleaving, character reversal, and format constraints respectively. On JailbreakBench, AdvBench, and HarmBench datasets, our method achieves attack success rates exceeding most methods across mainstream models including Qwen3 series, DeepSeek, Qwen3-Max, and Gemini 2.5 Flash. Experiments show thinking collapse rates and response repetition rates reach up to 17% and 60% respectively, indicating multi-stream perturbation not only bypasses safety mechanisms but also causes thinking process collapse or repetitive outputs.

</details>


### [105] [Detecting Privilege Escalation with Temporal Braid Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10094)
*Christophe Parisel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 使用Burau Lyapunov指数作为代数探针，在云权限图的时间演化中识别两种风险机制边界


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要在云权限图的强连通分量时间演化中，区分两种不同的风险机制（分散式和集中式），以便自动化分类和管理风险权限流

Method: 使用Burau Lyapunov指数作为代数探针，证明阿贝尔统计量无法确定该指数，利用非交换优势来区分风险机制

Result: 非交换优势虽小但可操作，能够有效区分分散式和集中式两种风险机制，实现自动化分类和风险权限流治理

Conclusion: Burau Lyapunov指数可作为有效的代数工具来识别云权限图中的风险机制边界，支持自动化风险分类和治理

Abstract: Within the Strongly Connected Components (SCCs) formed during the temporal evolution of a Cloud permission graph, we use the Burau Lyapunov exponent LE as an algebraic probe to locate the boundary between two risks regimes. We prove that no Abelian statistic (edge counts, net privilege flow, gate-firing rates) can determine LE. The non-commutation advantage is small, but actionable: we show how to leverage it to discriminate the two outstanding risk regimes, that we call dispersed and focused, for automating classification and governing remediation of risky Cloud permission flows.

</details>


### [106] [ACE Runtime - A ZKP-Native Blockchain Runtime with Sub-Second Cryptographic Finality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10242)
*Jian Sheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ACE Runtime是一种基于身份授权分离的ZKP原生执行层，通过将每笔交易的签名验证替换为轻量级HMAC认证，并在异步证明阶段生成聚合零知识最终性证书，实现O(1)的区块验证成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高性能区块链在关键路径上为每笔交易验证一个签名，导致O(N)验证成本、高硬件压力以及困难的抗量子迁移问题。

Method: 采用身份授权分离架构，构建"认证-执行-证明"流水线：热路径中使用轻量级HMAC认证替代签名检查，异步阶段生成聚合零知识最终性证书；实现双层最终性（BFT投票的软最终性和证明验证的硬最终性），并设计两阶段超时和备份证明路径确保活性。

Result: 原型显示低CPU编排开销，模型分析预测恒定区块验证成本、降低非构建者验证节点硬件需求、比每交易签名设计更好的带宽效率；形式化论证了认证不可伪造性和硬最终性不可逆性。

Conclusion: 身份授权分离是实现亚秒级密码学最终性的实用架构，为更强的抗量子组件提供了清晰路径。

Abstract: Existing high performance blockchains verify one signature per transaction on the critical path, which creates O(N) verification cost, high hardware pressure, and difficult post quantum migration. This paper presents ACE Runtime, a ZKP native execution layer built on identity authorization separation. We replace per transaction signature checks with lightweight HMAC attestations in the hot path, then generate one aggregated zero knowledge finality certificate per block in an asynchronous prove stage. The system is organized as an Attest Execute Prove pipeline with two tier finality: soft finality from BFT voting and hard finality from proof verification. Under standard cryptographic assumptions, we provide formal arguments for attestation unforgeability and hard finality irreversibility. We also define a two phase timeout and backup proving path with witness availability gossip for liveness under builder failure. Quantitative results combine analytical modeling with reference implementation measurements. The prototype shows low CPU orchestration overhead, while model driven analysis projects constant per block verification cost, lower validator hardware requirements for non builders, and better bandwidth efficiency than per transaction signature designs. These results indicate that identity authorization separation is a practical architecture for sub second cryptographic finality with a clear path toward stronger post quantum components.

</details>


### [107] [Post-Quantum Entropy as a Service for Embedded Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10274)
*Javier Blanco-Romero,Yuri Melissa Garcia-Niño,Florina Almenares Mendoza,Daniel Díaz-Sánchez,Carlos García-Rubio,Celeste Campo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 构建量子熵即服务系统，通过后量子安全通道将QRNG熵从Quantis设备传输到ESP32客户端，实现比传统加密更快的后量子密钥交换和认证


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入式设备缺乏可靠的熵源且难以承受重量级协议，需要构建轻量级的量子熵服务系统来解决熵质量问题和后量子安全需求

Method: 设计QEaaS系统，服务器端提供直接量子熵和混合熵两种路径，客户端扩展libcoap的Zephyr支持，集成wolfSSL-based DTLS 1.3，并添加BLAKE2s熵池；使用ML-KEM-512和ML-DSA-44等后量子算法

Result: ML-KEM-512完成DTLS 1.3握手平均313ms（无证书验证），比ECDHE P-256快35%；ML-KEM-512+ML-DSA-44组合平均225ms；证书验证仅增加17ms（ML-DSA-44），完全后量子配置比传统ECDHE P-256+ECDSA快63%；本地BLAKE2s池操作低于0.1ms

Conclusion: 在该平台上，后量子密钥交换和认证不仅可行，而且比传统基线更快，为嵌入式设备提供了高效的后量子安全解决方案

Abstract: Embedded cryptography stands or falls on entropy quality, yet small devices have few trustworthy sources and little tolerance for heavyweight protocols. We build a Quantum Entropy as a Service (QEaaS) system that moves QRNG-derived entropy from a Quantis device to ESP32-class clients over post-quantum-secured channels. On the server side, the design exposes two paths: direct quantum entropy through a custom OpenSSL provider and mixed entropy through the Linux system pool. On the client side, we extend libcoap's Zephyr support, integrate wolfSSL-based DTLS 1.3 into the CoAP stack, and add a BLAKE2s entropy pool that preserves the standard Zephyr extraction interface while introducing an injection API for server-provided entropy. Benchmarks on ESP32 hardware, targeting 100 iterations per configuration, show that ML-KEM-512 completes a DTLS 1.3 handshake in 313 ms on average without certificate verification, 35% faster than ECDHE P-256. Pairing ML-KEM-512 with ML-DSA-44 lowers the mean to 225 ms. Certificate verification adds roughly 194 ms for ECDSA but only 17 ms for ML-DSA-44, so the fully post-quantum configuration remains 63% faster than classical ECDHE P-256 with ECDSA even under full verification. Local BLAKE2s pool operations stay below 0.1 ms combined. On this platform, post-quantum key exchange and authentication are not only feasible; they are faster than the classical baseline.

</details>


### [108] [PRoADS: Provably Secure and Robust Audio Diffusion Steganography with latent optimization and backward Euler Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10314)
*YongPeng Yan,Yanan Li,Qiyang Xiao,Yanzhen Ren*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PRoADS是一个基于音频扩散模型的可证明安全鲁棒音频隐写框架，通过正交矩阵投影将秘密消息嵌入扩散模型的初始噪声中，采用潜在优化和后向欧拉反演降低重构误差，在64kbps MP3压缩下实现0.15%的低误码率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的隐写方法在扩散反演过程中存在重构误差，导致高误码率，特别是在音频压缩等失真条件下鲁棒性不足。需要开发能够同时保证安全性和鲁棒性的音频隐写方案。

Method: 1. 基于音频扩散模型的生成式隐写框架；2. 通过正交矩阵投影将秘密消息嵌入扩散模型的初始噪声；3. 引入潜在优化技术最小化潜在重构误差；4. 采用后向欧拉反演方法降低扩散反演误差。

Result: 在64kbps MP3压缩条件下，PRoADS实现了0.15%的极低误码率，显著优于现有方法。实验证明该框架具有强大的鲁棒性，同时保持了可证明的安全性。

Conclusion: PRoADS是一个安全、鲁棒的音频隐写框架，通过正交投影和误差优化技术有效解决了扩散模型隐写中的重构误差问题，在音频压缩等失真条件下表现出优异的性能。

Abstract: This paper proposes PRoADS, a provably secure and robust audio steganographic framework based on audio diffusion models. As a generative steganography scheme, PRoADS embeds secret messages into the initial noise of diffusion models via orthogonal matrix projection. To address the reconstruction errors in diffusion inversion that cause high bit error rates (BER), we introduce Latent Optimization and Backward Euler Inversion to minimize the latent reconstruction and diffusion inversion errors. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate that our scheme sustains a remarkably low BER of 0.15\% under 64 kbps MP3 compression, significantly outperforming existing methods and exhibiting strong robustness.

</details>


### [109] [The Orthogonal Vulnerabilities of Generative AI Watermarks: A Comparative Empirical Benchmark of Spatial and Latent Provenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10323)
*Jesse Yu,Nicholas Wei*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究通过对抗性攻击模拟引擎对空间域(RivaGAN)和潜在域(Tree-Ring)两种主流不可见水印进行系统性评估，发现它们在生成式AI编辑工具攻击下存在正交脆弱性，证明单域水印在对抗现代工具时存在根本性不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着开源生成式AI的快速普及，超逼真媒体合成能力对数字信任构成严峻挑战。当前最先进的不可见水印主要分为空间域和潜在域两种数学流形，但现有研究缺乏针对现代生成式AI编辑工具的严格比较基准测试。

Method: 研究采用自动化攻击模拟引擎，对RivaGAN(空间域)和Tree-Ring(潜在域)两种代表性范式进行实证评估。通过30个强度区间的几何和生成扰动测试，并形式化"对抗性规避区域"(AER)框架来衡量密码学退化与语义视觉保留(OpenCLIP > 70.0)的关系。统计分析采用每区间n=100样本，误差幅度±3.92%。

Result: 统计分析显示两种水印域存在相互排斥、数学正交的脆弱性：空间域水印在算法像素重写下经历严重密码学退化(Img2Img转换下AER规避率达67.47%)，而潜在域水印对几何错位表现出深刻脆弱性(静态裁剪下AER规避率达43.20%)。

Conclusion: 研究证明单域水印在对抗现代对抗性工具集时存在根本性不足，暴露了当前数字来源标准的系统性漏洞，为未来多域密码学架构奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: As open-weights generative AI rapidly proliferates, the ability to synthesize hyper-realistic media has introduced profound challenges to digital trust. Automated disinformation and AI-generated imagery have made robust digital provenance a critical cybersecurity imperative. Currently, state-of-the-art invisible watermarks operate within one of two primary mathematical manifolds: the spatial domain (post-generation pixel embedding) or the latent domain (pre-generation frequency embedding). While existing literature frequently evaluates these models against isolated, classical distortions, there is a critical lack of rigorous, comparative benchmarking against modern generative AI editing tools. In this study, we empirically evaluate two leading representative paradigms, RivaGAN (Spatial) and Tree-Ring (Latent), utilizing an automated Attack Simulation Engine across 30 intensity intervals of geometric and generative perturbations. We formalize an "Adversarial Evasion Region" (AER) framework to measure cryptographic degradation against semantic visual retention (OpenCLIP > 70.0). Our statistical analysis ($n=100$ per interval, $MOE = \pm 3.92\%$) reveals that these domains possess mutually exclusive, mathematically orthogonal vulnerabilities. Spatial watermarks experience severe cryptographic degradation under algorithmic pixel-rewriting (exhibiting a 67.47% AER evasion rate under Img2Img translation), whereas latent watermarks exhibit profound fragility against geometric misalignment (yielding a 43.20% AER evasion rate under static cropping). By proving that single-domain watermarking is fundamentally insufficient against modern adversarial toolsets, this research exposes a systemic vulnerability in current digital provenance standards and establishes the foundational exigence for future multi-domain cryptographic architectures.

</details>


### [110] [Naïve Exposure of Generative AI Capabilities Undermines Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10504)
*Sunpill Kim,Chanwoo Hwang,Minsu Kim,Jae Hong Seo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究发现，商业生成式AI系统通过聊天机器人界面暴露的强大推理和图像精修能力，从根本上破坏了现代深度伪造检测器的有效性，即使仅使用合规提示也能实现检测规避。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI系统通过用户友好的聊天机器人界面展示了强大的推理和图像精修能力，但研究者发现这些能力被天真地暴露出来，从根本上破坏了现代深度伪造检测器的有效性。当前检测方法在面对语义保持的图像精修时失效，而商业AI系统无意中暴露了真实性标准，可被直接用作精修目标。

Method: 研究采用现实且已部署的使用场景，攻击者仅使用良性、合规的提示和商业生成式AI系统。通过展示最先进的深度伪造检测方法在语义保持图像精修下的失败情况，证明生成式AI系统明确表达真实性标准并通过无限制推理无意中暴露这些标准，使其能够直接作为精修目标被重用。

Result: 精修后的图像同时实现了检测规避、保持了商业人脸识别API验证的身份，并展现出显著更高的感知质量。研究发现广泛可访问的商业聊天机器人服务比开源模型构成更大的安全风险，因为其更高的真实感、语义可控性和低门槛界面使非专业用户也能有效规避检测。

Conclusion: 当前检测框架假设的威胁模型与真实世界生成式AI的实际能力存在结构性不匹配。虽然检测基线主要受先前基准测试影响，但已部署系统在其他领域严格安全控制下仍暴露了无限制的真实性推理和精修能力，揭示了现有检测方法的根本局限性。

Abstract: Generative AI systems increasingly expose powerful reasoning and image refinement capabilities through user-facing chatbot interfaces. In this work, we show that the naïve exposure of such capabilities fundamentally undermines modern deepfake detectors. Rather than proposing a new image manipulation technique, we study a realistic and already-deployed usage scenario in which an adversary uses only benign, policy-compliant prompts and commercial generative AI systems. We demonstrate that state-of-the-art deepfake detection methods fail under semantic-preserving image refinement. Specifically, we show that generative AI systems articulate explicit authenticity criteria and inadvertently externalize them through unrestricted reasoning, enabling their direct reuse as refinement objectives. As a result, refined images simultaneously evade detection, preserve identity as verified by commercial face recognition APIs, and exhibit substantially higher perceptual quality. Importantly, we find that widely accessible commercial chatbot services pose a significantly greater security risk than open-source models, as their superior realism, semantic controllability, and low-barrier interfaces enable effective evasion by non-expert users. Our findings reveal a structural mismatch between the threat models assumed by current detection frameworks and the actual capabilities of real-world generative AI. While detection baselines are largely shaped by prior benchmarks, deployed systems expose unrestricted authenticity reasoning and refinement despite stringent safety controls in other domains.

</details>


### [111] [An Approach for Safe and Secure Software Protection Supported by Symbolic Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10608)
*Daniel Dorfmeister,Flavio Ferrarotti,Bernhard Fischer,Evelyn Haslinger,Rudolf Ramler,Markus Zimmermann*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 基于物理不可克隆函数(PUF)的工业控制软件复制保护方法，确保程序仅在目标硬件上正确执行，在其他机器上行为不同，同时保证安全属性并抵抗逆向工程


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业控制软件需要防止非法复制和盗版，传统保护方法存在被破解的风险，需要一种更安全可靠的硬件-软件绑定机制来保护知识产权

Method: 使用物理不可克隆函数(PUF)实现硬件-软件绑定，结合符号执行技术确保安全属性在不同执行环境下的保持，并设计抵抗逆向工程的保护机制

Result: 提出的方法能够确保程序仅在目标硬件上正确执行，在其他机器上表现不同，同时保证安全属性在PUF响应问题或不同执行环境下得到保持，且能抵抗逆向工程攻击

Conclusion: 基于PUF和符号执行的复制保护方法为工业控制软件提供了有效的硬件-软件绑定机制，既能保护知识产权，又能确保安全属性，具有实际应用价值

Abstract: We introduce a novel copy-protection method for industrial control software. With our method, a program executes correctly only on its target hardware and behaves differently on other machines. The hardware-software binding is based on Physically Unclonable Functions (PUFs). We use symbolic execution to guarantee the preservation of safety properties if the software is executed on a different machine, or if there is a problem with the PUF response. Moreover, we show that the protection method is also secure against reverse engineering.

</details>


### [112] [Repurposing Backdoors for Good: Ephemeral Intrinsic Proofs for Verifiable Aggregation in Cross-silo Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10692)
*Xian Qin,Xue Yang,Xiaohu Tang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量级可验证聚合架构，通过将后门注入作为内在证明来检测恶意服务器，相比密码学方法实现1000倍加速


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全聚合虽然保护了跨孤岛联邦学习中更新的机密性，但无法保证聚合完整性，恶意服务器可以静默省略或篡改更新。现有可验证聚合方案依赖重量级密码学（如零知识证明、同态加密），计算成本随模型规模增长而急剧增加。

Method: 提出轻量级架构，从外在密码学证明转向内在证明。通过重新利用后门注入技术，将验证信号直接嵌入模型参数中。利用灾难性遗忘特性，这些信号既足够鲁棒用于即时验证，又具有短暂性，自然衰减以保持最终模型效用。设计了随机化单验证器审计框架，兼容安全聚合，确保客户端匿名性并防止信号冲突，无需可信第三方。

Result: 在SVHN、CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100数据集上的实验表明，对恶意服务器具有高检测概率。特别地，在ResNet-18上相比密码学基线实现了超过1000倍的加速，能够有效扩展到大型模型。

Conclusion: 通过将后门注入重新定位为内在证明，提出了一种轻量级可验证聚合方案，解决了现有密码学方法计算开销大的问题，实现了高效的可扩展性，同时保持客户端匿名性和模型最终效用。

Abstract: While Secure Aggregation (SA) protects update confidentiality in Cross-silo Federated Learning, it fails to guarantee aggregation integrity, allowing malicious servers to silently omit or tamper with updates. Existing verifiable aggregation schemes rely on heavyweight cryptography (e.g., ZKPs, HE), incurring computational costs that scale poorly with model size. In this paper, we propose a lightweight architecture that shifts from extrinsic cryptographic proofs to \textit{Intrinsic Proofs}. We repurpose backdoor injection to embed verification signals directly into model parameters. By harnessing Catastrophic Forgetting, these signals are robust for immediate verification yet ephemeral, naturally decaying to preserve final model utility. We design a randomized, single-verifier auditing framework compatible with SA, ensuring client anonymity and preventing signal collision without trusted third parties. Experiments on SVHN, CIFAR-10, and CIFAR-100 demonstrate high detection probabilities against malicious servers. Notably, our approach achieves over $1000\times$ speedup on ResNet-18 compared to cryptographic baselines, effectively scaling to large models.

</details>


### [113] [CacheSolidarity: Preventing Prefix Caching Side Channels in Multi-tenant LLM Serving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10726)
*Panagiotis Georgios Pennas,Konstantinos Papaioannou,Marco Guarnieri,Thaleia Dimitra Doudali*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CacheSolidarity系统保护多租户LLM服务系统免受自动前缀缓存侧信道攻击，同时保持高性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动前缀缓存(APC)虽然提高LLM推理吞吐量，但引入时序侧信道攻击风险，攻击者可通过缓存命中/未命中模式推断敏感信息。现有防御方法简单禁用APC和缓存共享，牺牲了正常用户的效率

Method: CacheSolidarity系统监控跨用户缓存重用，标记可疑共享，并选择性隔离前缀，仅在必要时限制其重用，实现安全与性能的平衡

Result: 相比现有用户隔离防御方法，CacheSolidarity实现高达70%的缓存重用提升和30%的推理延迟降低，在保持安全的同时显著提升性能

Conclusion: CacheSolidarity的轻量级设计证明LLM服务安全不必以性能降低或不可承受的开销为代价，实现了安全与效率的平衡

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) rely on optimizations like Automatic Prefix Caching (APC) to accelerate inference. APC works by reusing previously computed states for the beginning part of a request (prefix), when another request starts with the same text. While APC improves throughput, it introduces timing side channels: cache hits are faster than misses, creating observable latency differences. In multi-tenant systems, attackers can exploit these differences to infer sensitive information, e.g., by incrementally reconstructing another user's request by observing hit/miss patterns. Current defenses take a sledgehammer approach: they disable APC and cache sharing, isolating users, and sacrificing efficiency for regular users. This paper presents CacheSolidarity, a system that secures multi-tenant LLM serving systems against APC side channels without sacrificing performance and efficiency. CacheSolidarity monitors cache reuse across users, flags suspicious sharing, and selectively isolates prefixes, restricting their reuse only when necessary. Evaluation shows that CacheSolidarity enables up to 70% higher cache reuse and 30% lower inference latency compared to existing defenses that isolate users. CacheSolidarity's lightweight design demonstrates how security in LLM serving does not have to come at the cost of unnecessarily reduced performance or unbearable overheads.

</details>


### [114] [MAD: Memory Allocation meets Software Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10840)
*Manuel Wiesinger,Daniel Dorfmeister,Stefan Brunthaler*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MAD（内存分配多样性）是一种通过软件多样性原理结合内存分配来防御RowHammer攻击的新方法，利用两种互补的空间多样化技术克服内存子系统熵不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DRAM错误导致的漏洞（特别是RowHammer攻击）是一个尚未解决且无法忽视的难题。现有防御方法仅针对早期RowHammer攻击的特定细节，无法应对近期攻击的泛化。目前尚不清楚现有技术能否应对这些泛化攻击，可能需要全新的方法。

Method: 提出MAD（内存分配多样性）系统，结合内存分配与软件多样性原理。采用两种新颖的互补空间多样化技术来克服内存子系统熵不足的障碍。系统具有易于实现、性能影响可忽略、硬件和软件无关的特性。

Result: 初步结果显示该方法前景良好。MAD的目标是通过尽可能延迟RowHammer攻击来阻止它们，这种延迟为各种额外响应措施（如主动重启或对正在进行的攻击进行深入分析）创造了条件。

Conclusion: 虽然仍是进行中的工作，但MAD提供了一种新的防御RowHammer攻击的方法，通过软件多样性和内存分配的结合，克服了传统软件多样性在内存子系统中面临的熵不足障碍，为应对不断演变的RowHammer攻击提供了有希望的解决方案。

Abstract: Vulnerabilities emanating from DRAM errors pose a vexing problem that remains, as of yet, unsolved and elusive but cannot be ignored. Prior defenses focused on specific details of early RowHammer attacks and fail to generalize with the generalizations of recent RowHammer attacks. Even worse, it is presently not clear that techniques from prior defenses will be able to cope with these generalizations or if an entirely new approach is required. Although still work-in-progress, we have identified a new approach that combines memory allocation with principles underlying software diversity and shows promising early results. At first glance, software diversity seems to be an unlikely contender, since it faces seemingly insurmountable obstacles, primarily the lack of sufficient entropy in memory subsystems. Our system - called MAD, short for memory allocation diversity - leverages two novel, complementary spatial diversification techniques to overcome this entropy obstacle. Entropy aside, MAD offers ease-of-implementation, negligible performance impact, and is both hardware and software agnostic. From a security perspective, MAD's goal is to deter RowHammer attacks by delaying them to the maximum extent possible. Such a delay opens the door for a variety of additional responses, e.g., proactive rebooting, or complementary in-depth analysis of ongoing attacks that would be too slow for an always-on defense.

</details>


### [115] [Separating Oblivious and Adaptive Differential Privacy under Continual Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11029)
*Mark Bun,Marco Gaboardi,Connor Wagaman*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次展示了持续观测模型中差分隐私在非自适应与自适应设置间的显式分离：非自适应设置下存在指数级时间步保持准确性的算法，而自适应设置下所有算法在常数步后即失效


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Jain等人（ICML 2023）提出的开放问题：证明持续观测模型中差分隐私在非自适应与自适应设置间存在分离。持续观测模型形式化了流算法中的隐私保护需求，其中数据随时间到达并在每个时间步输出结果。非自适应设置仅需对预先固定的数据流提供隐私保护，而自适应设置要求即使对基于算法输出自适应选择的数据流也能保持隐私。

Method: 基于Bun、Steinke和Ullman（SODA 2017）的相关向量查询问题构建分离实例。在非自适应设置中，设计了一个(ε,0)-差分隐私算法，该算法在输入维度上能保持指数级时间步的准确性。在自适应设置中，证明所有(ε,δ)-差分隐私算法在仅释放常数个时间步的输出后就会失去准确性。

Result: 首次展示了持续观测模型中差分隐私在非自适应与自适应设置间的显式分离。具体而言，非自适应设置下存在(ε,0)-DP算法能在指数级时间步内保持准确性，而自适应设置下所有(ε,δ)-DP算法在常数个时间步后即失效，这解决了Jain等人提出的开放问题。

Conclusion: 本文成功证明了持续观测模型中差分隐私在非自适应与自适应设置间存在根本性差异，这一分离结果基于相关向量查询问题构建，为理解流算法中不同隐私设置下的能力边界提供了重要理论依据。

Abstract: We resolve an open question of Jain, Raskhodnikova, Sivakumar, and Smith (ICML 2023) by exhibiting a problem separating differential privacy under continual observation in the oblivious and adaptive settings. The continual observation (a.k.a. continual release) model formalizes privacy for streaming algorithms, where data is received over time and output is released at each time step. In the oblivious setting, privacy need only hold for data streams fixed in advance; in the adaptive setting, privacy is required even for streams that can be chosen adaptively based on the streaming algorithm's output.
  We describe the first explicit separation between the oblivious and adaptive settings. The problem showing this separation is based on the correlated vector queries problem of Bun, Steinke, and Ullman (SODA 2017). Specifically, we present an $(\varepsilon,0)$-DP algorithm for the oblivious setting that remains accurate for exponentially many time steps in the dimension of the input.
