On the other hand, we show that every $(\varepsilon,δ)$-DP adaptive algorithm fails to be accurate after releasing output for only a constant number of time steps.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [116] [NasoVoce: A Nose-Mounted Low-Audibility Speech Interface for Always-Available Speech Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10324)
*Jun Rekimoto,Yu Nishimura,Bojian Yang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: NasoVoce：一种集成麦克风和振动传感器的鼻梁式接口，通过融合声学和振动信号实现抗干扰的高质量语音识别，适用于耳语和无声语音交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在词汇量、可穿戴性、静默性和噪声鲁棒性之间难以平衡。耳语和无声语音为实现始终可用的AI语音交互提供了可能，但需要解决环境噪声干扰和低音量语音捕获问题。

Method: 设计鼻梁式接口，集成麦克风和振动传感器，置于智能眼镜鼻托位置。利用鼻梁靠近口腔的优势，采集骨传导和皮肤传导的语音信号。通过融合麦克风的高质量音频信号和振动传感器的抗噪声信号，生成高质量且抗干扰的语音。

Result: 使用Whisper Large-v2、PESQ、STOI和MUSHRA评分进行评估，证实了识别率和语音质量的提升。能够可靠捕获低音量语音（如耳语），并在噪声环境中保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: NasoVoce展示了实现实用、始终可用、连续且隐蔽的AI语音对话界面的可行性，为耳语和无声语音交互提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Silent and whispered speech offer promise for always-available voice interaction with AI, yet existing methods struggle to balance vocabulary size, wearability, silence, and noise robustness. We present NasoVoce, a nose-bridge-mounted interface that integrates a microphone and a vibration sensor. Positioned at the nasal pads of smart glasses, it unobtrusively captures both acoustic and vibration signals. The nasal bridge, close to the mouth, allows access to bone- and skin-conducted speech and enables reliable capture of low-volume utterances such as whispered speech. While the microphone captures high-quality audio, it is highly sensitive to environmental noise. Conversely, the vibration sensor is robust to noise but yields lower signal quality. By fusing these complementary inputs, NasoVoce generates high-quality speech robust against interference. Evaluation with Whisper Large-v2, PESQ, STOI, and MUSHRA ratings confirms improved recognition and quality. NasoVoce demonstrates the feasibility of a practical interface for always-available, continuous, and discreet AI voice conversations.

</details>


### [117] [Reactive Writers: How Co-Writing with AI Changes How We Engage with Ideas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10374)
*Advait Bhat,Marianne Aubin Le Quéré,Mor Naaman,Maurice Jakesch*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究发现AI写作辅助工具改变了写作过程，从传统的构思-表达转变为"反应式写作"：用户优先评估AI建议而非自主构思，导致观点易受AI影响但用户却感知不到这种影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明AI写作辅助会改变人们表达的观点和后续持有的意见，但缺乏对AI协同写作过程中程序性和行为变化的实质性理解，需要探究AI写作工具产生意见塑造效应的底层机制。

Method: 采用混合方法研究：对19名参与者的AI协同写作经历进行回顾性访谈，同时对1,291个AI协同写作会话中的观点和意见参与进行定量分析追踪。

Result: 研究发现：1) 评估AI建议成为写作过程的核心活动，取代了传统的构思和语言生成过程；2) 作家常在完成自主构思前就接触AI建议，导致建议的观点成为后续发展的方向；3) 作家未察觉AI的影响，认为自己对写作有完全控制权，因为他们理论上可以编辑最终文本。

Conclusion: 研究提出了"反应式写作"概念：这是一种评估优先、建议主导的写作实践，与传统写作过程有本质区别，极易受到AI诱导的偏见和观点转变影响，尽管用户感知不到这种影响。

Abstract: Emerging experimental evidence shows that writing with AI assistance can change both the views people express in writing and the opinions they hold afterwards. Yet, we lack substantive understanding of procedural and behavioral changes in co-writing with AI that underlie the observed opinion-shaping power of AI writing tools. We conducted a mixed-methods study, combining retrospective interviews with 19 participants about their AI co-writing experience with a quantitative analysis tracing engagement with ideas and opinions in 1{,}291 AI co-writing sessions. Our analysis shows that engaging with the AI's suggestions -- reading them and deciding whether to accept them -- becomes a central activity in the writing process, taking away from more traditional processes of ideation and language generation. As writers often do not complete their own ideation before engaging with suggestions, the suggested ideas and opinions seeded directions that writers then elaborated on. At the same time, writers did not notice the AI's influence and felt in full control of their writing, as they -- in principle -- could always edit the final text. We term this shift \textit{Reactive Writing}: an evaluation-first, suggestion-led writing practice that departs substantially from conventional composing in the presence of AI assistance and is highly vulnerable to AI-induced biases and opinion shifts.

</details>


### [118] [Moving Phones, Active Peers: Exploring the Effect of Animated Phones as Facilitators in In-Person Group Discussion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10394)
*Ziqi Pan,Ziqi Liu,Jinhan Zhang,Zeyu Huang,Xiaojuan Ma*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究通过动画化智能手机作为主动的具身协调者，提升面对面小组讨论中的行为参与度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能手机在面对面小组讨论中已成为智能工作站，但其作为共同在场设备如何增强人们与他人行为参与的研究不足。需要探索如何通过动画化手机表达性移动，将其转变为主动的具身协调者，而不影响常规功能。

Method: 1. 基于Tuckman群体发展理论，在设计工作坊(n=12)中识别问题性群体工作情境，设计表达性、注意力高效的动画手机协调方案。2. 开发AnimaStand移动手机支架，根据对话动态动画化手机传递群体协调线索。3. 在陌生人小组讨论的组间设计Wizard-of-Oz研究(n=56)中评估效果，所有参与者的手机都置于AnimaStand上。

Result: 动画化手机协调重新吸引了不活跃成员，改善了群体动态、任务操作表现和成员关系。协调功能有效提升了小组讨论的整体参与度和互动质量。

Conclusion: 动画化个人设备可作为有效的具身协调者增强共处群体互动。未来可探索更自适应和普适的动画设备个人协调方案，扩展智能设备在面对面协作中的角色。

Abstract: In today's in-person group discussions, smartphones are integrated as intelligent workstations; yet given their co-presence in such face-to-face interactions, whether and how they may enhance people's behavioral engagement with others remains underexplored. This work investigates how animating personal smartphones to move expressively, without compromising regular functions, can transform them into active embodied facilitators for co-located group interaction. In the four-stranger small-group discussion setting, guided by Tuckman's group-development theory, we conducted a design workshop (n=12) to identify problematic group-work circumstances and design expressive, attention-efficient animated phone facilitations. Subsequently, we developed AnimaStand, a movement-enabled phone stand that animates phones to deliver group facilitation cues according to conversation dynamics. In a between-subjects Wizard-of-Oz study (n=56) with four-stranger group discussions, where everyone's phone was on an AnimaStand, the facilitations re-engaged inactive members, enhancing group dynamics, task operation performance, and relationships. We finally discuss prospects for more adaptive and generalizable animated device personal facilitation.

</details>


### [119] [Graphing Inline: Understanding Word-scale Graphics Use in Scientific Papers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10533)
*Siyu Lu,Yanhan Liu,Shiyu Xu,Ruishi Zou,Chen Ye*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究分析了科学论文中词级图形（word-scale graphics）的使用情况，发现这类图形在科学论文中很少使用，图标是主要表现形式，且视觉表现形式与交流功能相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学论文中图形（如图表和插图）普遍存在，但将图形与文本分离会增加理解文本-图形联系的认知负担。词级图形（视觉装饰以排版尺寸呈现）可以增强原始文本的表达性和可理解性，但科学论文是否以及如何使用词级图形进行学术交流尚不清楚。

Method: 通过语料库研究，从126,797篇科学论文中提取了909个词级图形进行分析。提出了一个框架来表征作者在科学论文中应用词级图形的位置（定位）、原因（交流功能）和方式（视觉表现形式）。

Result: 研究发现：1）词级图形在科学论文中很少使用；2）图标在视觉表现形式中占主导地位；3）视觉表现形式与交流功能相关（例如使用定量图表进行数据标注）。

Conclusion: 词级图形在科学论文中的使用有限，但具有增强学术交流的潜力。通过技术和行政创新可以促进词级图形在学术交流中的更广泛应用。

Abstract: Graphics (e.g., figures and charts) are ubiquitous in scientific papers, yet separating graphics from text increases cognitive load in understanding text-graphic connections. Research has found that word-scale graphics, or visual embellishments at typographic size, can augment original text, making it more expressive and easier to understand. However, whether, if so, how scientific papers adopt word-scale graphics for scholarly communication remains unclear. To address this gap, we conducted a corpus study reviewing 909 word-scale graphics extracted from 126,797 scientific papers. Through analysis, we propose a framework that characterizes where (positioning), why (communicative function), and how (visual representation) authors apply word-scale graphics in scientific papers. Our findings reveal that word-scale graphics are rarely used, that icons dominate visual representation, and that visual representation connects with communicative function (e.g., using quantitative graphs for data annotation). We further discuss opportunities to enhance scholarly communication with word-scale graphics through technical and administrative innovations.

</details>


### [120] [A Platform-Agnostic Multimodal Digital Human Modelling Framework: Neurophysiological Sensing in Game-Based Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10680)
*Daniel J. Buxton,Mufti Mahmud,Jordan J. Bird,Thomas Hughes-Roberts,David J. Brown*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出一个平台无关的数字人体建模框架，将传感、交互建模和推理准备分离，使用OpenBCI Galea头戴设备作为统一多模态传感层，结合SuperTux游戏环境，支持AI就绪的多模态交互研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI驱动的数字人体建模方法通常与特定平台、任务或解释流程紧密耦合，限制了可重复性、可扩展性和伦理重用。需要一种平台无关的框架来支持AI就绪的多模态交互研究。

Method: 开发平台无关的DHM框架，明确分离传感、交互建模和推理准备。集成OpenBCI Galea头戴设备作为统一多模态传感层（EEG、EMG、EOG、PPG和惯性数据），使用SuperTux创建可重复的游戏交互环境。生理信号表示为结构化、时间对齐的可观测数据，交互通过计算任务基元和带时间戳的事件标记建模。

Result: 通过作者自我仪器化进行技术验证，确认数据完整性、流连续性和同步性。讨论了数据吞吐量、延迟以及扩展到其他传感器或交互模式的扩展性。展示了框架如何支持AI驱动的DHM和HCI研究，包括无障碍交互设计和自适应系统研究。

Conclusion: 该框架为未来伦理批准的包容性DHM研究提供了以新兴技术为重点的基础设施，支持AI就绪的多模态交互研究，无需架构修改即可应用于各种研究场景。

Abstract: Digital Human Modelling (DHM) is increasingly shaped by advances in AI, wearable biosensing, and interactive digital environments, particularly in research addressing accessibility and inclusion. However, many AI-enabled DHM approaches remain tightly coupled to specific platforms, tasks, or interpretative pipelines, limiting reproducibility, scalability, and ethical reuse. This paper presents a platform-agnostic DHM framework designed to support AI-ready multimodal interaction research by explicitly separating sensing, interaction modelling, and inference readiness. The framework integrates the OpenBCI Galea headset as a unified multimodal sensing layer, providing concurrent EEG, EMG, EOG, PPG, and inertial data streams, alongside a reproducible, game-based interaction environment implemented using SuperTux. Rather than embedding AI models or behavioural inference, physiological signals are represented as structured, temporally aligned observables, enabling downstream AI methods to be applied under appropriate ethical approval. Interaction is modelled using computational task primitives and timestamped event markers, supporting consistent alignment across heterogeneous sensors and platforms. Technical verification via author self-instrumentation confirms data integrity, stream continuity, and synchronisation; no human-subjects evaluation or AI inference is reported. Scalability considerations are discussed with respect to data throughput, latency, and extension to additional sensors or interaction modalities. Illustrative use cases demonstrate how the framework can support AI-enabled DHM and HCI studies, including accessibility-oriented interaction design and adaptive systems research, without requiring architectural modifications. The proposed framework provides an emerging-technology-focused infrastructure for future ethics-approved, inclusive DHM research.

</details>


### [121] [Proceedings of CHIdeology 2026: CHI Workshop on Disentangling the fragmented politics, values and imaginaries of Human-Computer Interaction through ideologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10681)
*Felix Anand Epp,Matti Nelimarkka,Jesse Haapoja,Pedro Ferreira,Os Keyes,Shaowen Bardzell*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: CHI 2024研讨会论文集，聚焦HCI领域的政治、价值观和意识形态分析


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HCI领域存在碎片化的政治立场、价值观和意识形态，需要系统性地解构和分析这些元素，以理解它们如何影响技术设计和研究实践

Method: 通过研讨会形式汇集学者，采用意识形态分析框架，探讨HCI中的政治、价值观和想象，包括批判性理论、女权主义技术研究、后殖民主义视角等方法

Result: 汇集了多篇论文和讨论，建立了CHIdeology这一新兴研究领域，为理解HCI中的意识形态维度提供了概念工具和批判性视角

Conclusion: HCI研究需要更自觉地审视其意识形态基础，CHIdeology研讨会为这一重要对话提供了平台，有助于推动更负责任、更具反思性的技术设计实践

Abstract: This is the Proceedings of the First CHI Workshop on CHIdeology: Disentangling the fragmented politics, values, and imaginaries of Human-Computer Interaction through ideologies, held on Wednesday, 15 April, in Barcelona, Spain, at the ACM CHI Conference on Human Factors in Computing Systems.

</details>


### [122] [Believing vs. Achieving -- The Disconnect between Efficacy Beliefs and Collaborative Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10708)
*Philipp Spitzer,Joshua Holstein*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探讨人类在AI协作中的委托决策如何受到效能信念影响，发现存在系统性"AI乐观主义"偏见，情境信息对偏见消除具有不对称效应


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI日益融入工作流程，人类需要决定何时依赖AI建议。这些决策取决于一般效能信念（人类对自身能力的信心和对AI能力的认知）。虽然先前研究考察了影响AI依赖的因素，但效能信念在塑造协作中的作用仍未充分探索

Method: 通过受控实验（N=240），参与者进行重复委托决策，研究效能信念如何在不同情境信息下转化为实例层面的效能判断。实验设计允许探索效能信念作为认知锚点的作用

Result: 发现效能信念是持久的认知锚点，导致系统性"AI乐观主义"偏见。情境信息具有不对称效应：AI性能信息能选择性地消除AI乐观主义偏见，而数据或AI信息会放大效能差异对委托决策的影响。虽然效能差异影响委托行为，但对人-AI团队绩效的影响较弱

Conclusion: 研究结果挑战了以透明度为中心的方法，提出了有效协作环境的设计指南。效能信念在AI协作中扮演重要角色，需要针对性设计来优化人类与AI的协作效果

Abstract: As artificial intelligence (AI) becomes increasingly integrated into workflows, humans must decide when to rely on AI advice. These decisions depend on general efficacy beliefs, i.e., humans' confidence in their own abilities and their perceptions of AI competence. While prior work has examined factors influencing AI reliance, the role of efficacy beliefs in shaping collaboration remains underexplored. Through a controlled experiment (N=240) where participants made repeated delegation decisions, we investigate how efficacy beliefs translate into instance-wise efficacy judgments under varying contextual information. Our explorative findings reveal efficacy beliefs as persistent cognitive anchors, leading to systematic "AI optimism". Contextual information operates asymmetrically: while AI performance information selectively eliminates the AI optimism bias, data or AI information amplify how efficacy discrepancies influence delegation decisions. Although efficacy discrepancies influence delegation behavior, they show weaker effects on human-AI team performance. As these findings challenge transparency-focused approaches, we propose design guidelines for effective collaborative settings.

</details>


### [123] [AI-Generated Rubric Interfaces: K-12 Teachers' Perceptions and Practices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10773)
*Bahare Riahi,Sayali Patukale,Joy Niranjan,Yogya Koneru,Tiffany Barnes,Veronica Cateté*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探讨K-12教师对AI生成量规的认知与体验，发现AI工具能提供良好初稿但需教师监督编辑，教师有条件采纳AI量规工具


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教师很少从头创建量规，因为过程耗时且定义清晰的绩效等级具有挑战性。研究旨在探索AI支持的量规生成如何帮助教师克服这些障碍，并了解教师对此技术的接受度。

Method: 在暑期专业发展工作坊中，25名教师使用MagicSchool.ai生成量规，练习提示工程来定制标准和绩效等级。他们将生成的量规应用于样本积木编程活动，并使用聊天机器人提供基于量规的反馈。通过前后测调查、开放式讨论和退出票收集数据，采用主题分析处理定性数据。

Result: 教师认为AI生成的量规是良好的初稿，能改进结构和澄清模糊标准，但需要教师监督以避免通用或年级不匹配的语言、与教学重点不一致等问题。调查显示高感知清晰度和伦理可接受性，中等程度的作业匹配度，可用性（特别是添加、删除或修订标准的能力）是主要弱点。教师发现AI反馈存在"严格度与细节度"的权衡：更严格但更详细且可扩展。

Conclusion: 教师有条件地愿意采用AI量规工具，前提是工作流程支持轻松定制并保留教师控制权。AI工具可作为量规创建的起点，但需要教师监督和编辑以确保与教学目标和学生需求保持一致。

Abstract: This study investigates K--12 teachers' perceptions and experiences with AI-supported rubric generation during a summer professional development workshop ($n = 25$). Teachers used MagicSchool.ai to generate rubrics and practiced prompting to tailor criteria and performance levels. They then applied these rubrics to provide feedback on a sample block-based programming activity, followed by using a chatbot to deliver rubric-based feedback for the same work.
  Data were collected through pre- and post-workshop surveys, open discussions, and exit tickets. We used thematic analysis to analyze the qualitative data. Teachers reported that they rarely create rubrics from scratch because the process is time-consuming and defining clear distinctions between performance levels is challenging.
  After hands-on use, teachers described AI-generated rubrics as strong starting drafts that improved structure and clarified vague criteria. However, they emphasized the need for teacher oversight due to generic or grade-misaligned language, occasional misalignment with instructional priorities, and the need for substantial editing.
  Survey results indicated high perceived clarity and ethical acceptability, moderate alignment with assignments, and usability as the primary weakness -- particularly the ability to add, remove, or revise criteria. Open-ended responses highlighted a ``strictness-versus-detail'' trade-off: AI feedback was often perceived as harsher but more detailed and scalable. As a result, teachers expressed conditional willingness to adopt AI rubric tools when workflows support easy customization and preserve teacher control.

</details>


### [124] [World Mouse: Exploring Interactions with a Cross-Reality Cursor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10984)
*Esen K. Tütüncü,Mar Gonzalez-Franco,Khushman Patel,Eric J. Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: World Mouse：一种跨现实光标系统，将传统2D桌面鼠标重新设计用于3D场景交互，通过表面法线实现精确光标定位，利用语义分割和网格重建将物理对象作为交互表面


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展现实（XR）系统虽然能映射和理解物理世界，但与这些混合表示的交互仍然具有挑战性。当前"自然"输入方式存在局限性：触摸受限于人类可达范围和疲劳，而注视通常缺乏精细交互的精度。需要弥合这一差距

Method: 引入World Mouse跨现实光标系统，包含两个核心机制：1) 对象内交互 - 使用表面法线进行精确光标定位；2) 对象间导航 - 利用插值在空空间中进行遍历。系统利用语义分割和网格重建将物理对象作为交互表面，不同于以往的纯虚拟方法

Result: 通过一系列原型（包括对象操作和屏幕到世界的转换）展示了跨现实光标如何实现真实和虚拟环境之间的无缝交互。系统能够处理复杂的3D场景，提供精确的交互能力

Conclusion: World Mouse通过重新设计熟悉的2D桌面鼠标概念，为复杂的3D场景提供了一种有效的跨现实交互解决方案，可能实现真实和虚拟环境之间的无缝交互

Abstract: As Extended Reality (XR) systems increasingly map and understand the physical world, interacting with these blended representations remains challenging. The current push for "natural" inputs has its trade-offs: touch is limited by human reach and fatigue, while gaze often lacks the precision for fine interaction. To bridge this gap, we introduce World Mouse, a cross-reality cursor that reinterprets the familiar 2D desktop mouse for complex 3D scenes. The system is driven by two core mechanisms: within-object interaction, which uses surface normals for precise cursor placement, and between-object navigation, which leverages interpolation to traverse empty space. Unlike previous virtual-only approaches, World Mouse leverages semantic segmentation and mesh reconstruction to treat physical objects as interactive surfaces. Through a series of prototypes, including object manipulation and screen-to-world transitions, we illustrate how cross-reality cursors may enable seamless interactions across real and virtual environments.

</details>


### [125] [Task-Aware Delegation Cues for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11011)
*Xingrui Gu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出任务感知协作信号层，将离线偏好评估转化为面向用户的在线委托原语，通过任务分类、能力配置文件和协调风险提示实现透明、可审计的人-智能体协作委托


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前人-智能体协作脆弱，存在信息不对称问题：用户缺乏任务特定的可靠性线索，智能体很少展示校准的不确定性或推理过程。需要建立透明、可协商的委托机制

Method: 使用Chatbot Arena成对比较数据，通过语义聚类生成可解释的任务分类法，推导任务条件化的能力配置文件（胜率图）和协调风险提示（平局率先验），构建闭环委托协议支持共同基础验证、自适应路由、显式推理披露和隐私保护问责日志

Result: 预测探针验证任务分类具有可操作结构：聚类特征提高胜者预测准确性，在分层5折交叉验证下减少难度预测误差。框架将委托从系统默认转变为可见、可协商、可审计的协作决策

Conclusion: 该框架为自适应人-智能体协作提供了原则性设计空间，基于相互意识和共同问责，将委托重新定义为透明、可协商、可审计的协作决策过程

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly present as conversational collaborators, yet human--agent teamwork remains brittle due to information asymmetry: users lack task-specific reliability cues, and agents rarely surface calibrated uncertainty or rationale. We propose a task-aware collaboration signaling layer that turns offline preference evaluations into online, user-facing primitives for delegation. Using Chatbot Arena pairwise comparisons, we induce an interpretable task taxonomy via semantic clustering, then derive (i) Capability Profiles as task-conditioned win-rate maps and (ii) Coordination-Risk Cues as task-conditioned disagreement (tie-rate) priors. These signals drive a closed-loop delegation protocol that supports common-ground verification, adaptive routing (primary vs.\ primary+auditor), explicit rationale disclosure, and privacy-preserving accountability logs. Two predictive probes validate that task typing carries actionable structure: cluster features improve winner prediction accuracy and reduce difficulty prediction error under stratified 5-fold cross-validation. Overall, our framework reframes delegation from an opaque system default into a visible, negotiable, and auditable collaborative decision, providing a principled design space for adaptive human--agent collaboration grounded in mutual awareness and shared accountability.

</details>


### [126] [Chasing RATs: Tracing Reading for and as Creative Activity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11031)
*Sophia Liu,Shm Garanganao Almeda*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文提出"阅读活动痕迹(RATs)"概念，将阅读视为创造性活动，通过追踪阅读轨迹、关联和反思来可视化被算法压缩的创造性工作


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前创造力研究过于关注制作过程，而忽视了塑造创作的前期解释性劳动。算法推荐和AI摘要正在压缩和自动化人类解释过程，导致创造性工作变得不可见

Method: 引入阅读活动痕迹(RATs)概念，将阅读定义为包括导航、解释和策展媒体的创造性活动。通过追踪遍历轨迹、关联和反思作为可检查的工件，使创造性工作变得可见。在维基百科上创建了WikiRAT作为概念验证

Result: RATs能够可视化被算法压缩的创造性工作，为反思实践、读者建模、集体意义建构开辟了新途径，并帮助理解人类解释被自动化时丢失了什么

Conclusion: 阅读本身就是创造性活动，RATs为设计保留人类解释的智能工具提供了理论基础，强调需要保护阅读过程中的创造性劳动不被完全自动化

Abstract: Creativity research has privileged making over the interpretive labor that precedes and shapes it. We introduce Reading Activity Traces (RATs), a proposal that treats reading -- broadly defined to include navigating, interpreting, and curating media across interconnected sources -- as creative activity both for future artifacts and as a form of creation in its own right. By tracing trajectories of traversal, association, and reflection as inspectable artifacts, RATs render visible the creative work that algorithmic feeds and AI summarization increasingly compress and automate away. We illustrate this through WikiRAT, a speculative instantiation on Wikipedia, and open new ground for reflective practice, reader modeling, collective sensemaking, and understanding what is lost when human interpretation is automated -- towards designing intelligent tools that preserve it.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [OmniGuide: Universal Guidance Fields for Enhancing Generalist Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10052)
*Yunzhou Song,Long Le,Yong-Hyun Park,Jie Wang,Junyao Shi,Lingjie Liu,Jiatao Gu,Eric Eaton,Dinesh Jayaraman,Kostas Daniilidis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OMNIGUIDE是一个提升视觉-语言-动作模型在复杂任务上性能的框架，通过将多种引导源（如3D基础模型、语义推理VLM、人体姿态模型）表达为可微能量函数来指导动作采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作模型在简单任务上表现良好，但在需要复杂空间/语义理解、杂乱环境操作或精确操控的复杂任务上性能有限。需要一种灵活框架来整合多种互补的引导源以提升VLA模型在挑战性任务上的表现。

Method: 提出OMNIGUIDE框架，将各种引导源（3D基础模型、语义推理VLM、人体姿态模型等）表达为可微能量函数，这些函数在3D空间中定义任务特定的吸引子和排斥子，从而影响VLA动作的采样过程。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中的大量实验表明，OMNIGUIDE显著提升了最先进通用策略（如π₀.₅、GR00T N1.6）在成功率和安全性方面的表现。该统一框架匹配甚至超越了专门为特定引导源设计的先前方法。

Conclusion: OMNIGUIDE提供了一个灵活统一的框架，能够有效整合多种互补的引导源来增强VLA模型在复杂任务上的性能，为提升通用机器人策略的能力提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Vision-language-action(VLA) models have shown great promise as generalist policies for a large range of relatively simple tasks. However, they demonstrate limited performance on more complex tasks, such as those requiring complex spatial or semantic understanding, manipulation in clutter, or precise manipulation. We propose OMNIGUIDE, a flexible framework that improves VLA performance on such tasks by leveraging arbitrary sources of guidance, such as 3D foundation models, semantic-reasoning VLMs, and human pose models. We show how many kinds of guidance can be naturally expressed as differentiable energy functions with task-specific attractors and repellers located in 3D space, that influence the sampling of VLA actions. In this way, OMNIGUIDE enables guidance sources with complementary task-relevant strengths to improve a VLA model's performance on challenging tasks. Extensive experiments in both simulation and real-world environments, across diverse sources of guidance, demonstrate that OMNIGUIDE enhances the performance of state-of-the-art generalist policies (e.g., $π_{0.5}$, GR00T N1.6) significantly across success and safety rates. Critically, our unified framework matches or surpasses the performance of prior methods designed to incorporate specific sources of guidance into VLA policies. Project Page: $\href{https://omniguide.github.io/}{this \; url}$

</details>


### [128] [GRACE: A Unified 2D Multi-Robot Path Planning Simulator & Benchmark for Grid, Roadmap, And Continuous Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10858)
*Chuanlong Zang,Anna Mannucci,Isabelle Barz,Philipp Schillinger,Florian Lier,Wolfgang Hönig*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GRACE是一个统一的多智能体路径规划仿真+基准测试平台，通过在网格、路网和连续空间三个抽象层次上实例化相同任务，实现跨表示方法的可比较评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体路径规划工具要么在简化假设下扩展（网格、同质智能体），要么提供高保真度但缺乏可比性。需要透明、可复现的跨建模选择比较平台。

Method: 开发GRACE统一2D仿真+基准测试平台，通过显式、可复现的操作符在网格、路网和连续空间三个抽象层次上实例化相同任务，采用共同评估协议。

Result: 在公共地图和代表性规划器上的实证结果实现了共享实例集上的可比较评估，量化了表示-保真度权衡（连续空间规划器保真度高但速度慢，网格/路网规划器扩展性更好）。

Conclusion: GRACE通过整合表示、执行和评估，使跨表示研究更具可比性，为推进多机器人规划研究及其实际应用提供了工具。

Abstract: Advancing Multi-Agent Pathfinding (MAPF) and Multi-Robot Motion Planning (MRMP) requires platforms that enable transparent, reproducible comparisons across modeling choices. Existing tools either scale under simplifying assumptions (grids, homogeneous agents) or offer higher fidelity with less comparable instrumentation. We present GRACE, a unified 2D simulator+benchmark that instantiates the same task at multiple abstraction levels (grid, roadmap, continuous) via explicit, reproducible operators and a common evaluation protocol. Our empirical results on public maps and representative planners enable commensurate comparisons on a shared instance set. Furthermore, we quantify the expected representation-fidelity trade-offs (MRMP solves instances at higher fidelity but lower speed, while grid/roadmap planners scale farther). By consolidating representation, execution, and evaluation, GRACE thereby aims to make cross-representation studies more comparable and provides a means to advance multi-robot planning research and its translation to practice.

</details>


### [129] [Dynamic Modeling and Attitude Control of a Reaction-Wheel-Based Low-Gravity Bipedal Hopper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10670)
*Shriram Hari,M Venkata Sai Nikhil,R Prasanth Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于低重力天体（如月球）的欠驱动双足跳跃机器人，通过内部反作用飞轮在弹道飞行阶段调节身体姿态，显著减少空中姿态偏差并实现稳定着陆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 月球和近地小行星等低重力天体由于接触力减小和空中时间延长，对运动控制提出独特挑战。跳跃运动虽然能量效率高，但容易因不对称推力产生和地形不平导致空中姿态不稳定。

Method: 设计欠驱动双足跳跃机器人，采用内部反作用飞轮调节身体姿态；将系统建模为陀螺仪，分析躯干旋转与反作用飞轮动量之间的动态耦合；运动周期包括腿部驱动推进跳跃、主动动量交换控制器的空中姿态稳定和减震着陆三个阶段；开发降阶模型捕捉关键耦合关系。

Result: 在MuJoCo模拟的月球重力条件下，反作用飞轮控制器使峰值空中角度偏差减少超过65%，着陆姿态误差控制在3.5度以内；同时减少了每个跳跃周期的执行器饱和，确保足够的控制能力。

Conclusion: 该方法显著减轻了飞行中的姿态偏移，实现了一致的直立着陆，为不规则地外地形上的运动提供了实用且控制效率高的解决方案。

Abstract: Planetary bodies characterized by low gravitational acceleration, such as the Moon and near-Earth asteroids, impose unique locomotion constraints due to diminished contact forces and extended airborne intervals. Among traversal strategies, hopping locomotion offers high energy efficiency but is prone to mid-flight attitude instability caused by asymmetric thrust generation and uneven terrain interactions. This paper presents an underactuated bipedal hopping robot that employs an internal reaction wheel to regulate body posture during the ballistic flight phase. The system is modeled as a gyrostat, enabling analysis of the dynamic coupling between torso rotation and reaction wheel momentum. The locomotion cycle comprises three phases: a leg-driven propulsive jump, mid-air attitude stabilization via an active momentum exchange controller, and a shock-absorbing landing. A reduced-order model is developed to capture the critical coupling between torso rotation and reaction wheel dynamics. The proposed framework is evaluated in MuJoCo-based simulations under lunar gravity conditions (g = 1.625 m/s^2). Results demonstrate that activation of the reaction wheel controller reduces peak mid-air angular deviation by more than 65% and constrains landing attitude error to within 3.5 degrees at touchdown. Additionally, actuator saturation per hop cycle is reduced, ensuring sufficient control authority. Overall, the approach significantly mitigates in-flight attitude excursions and enables consistent upright landings, providing a practical and control-efficient solution for locomotion on irregular extraterrestrial terrains.

</details>


### [130] [Parallel-in-Time Nonlinear Optimal Control via GPU-native Sequential Convex Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10711)
*Yilin Zou,Zhong Zhang,Fanghua Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出完全GPU原生的轨迹优化框架，结合序列凸规划与共识ADMM，通过时间分割实现大规模并行计算，在边缘计算平台上实现4倍吞吐加速和51%能耗降低


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CPU序列求解器依赖全局稀疏线性代数或动态规划串行特性，无法充分利用GPU等大规模并行计算架构，限制了实时轨迹优化的性能

Method: 结合序列凸规划与基于共识的交替方向乘子法，采用时间分割策略将优化时域解耦为独立的节点子问题，完全在GPU上并行执行，避免内存传输和大规模稀疏分解

Result: 在四旋翼敏捷飞行和火星动力下降任务中验证，相比12核CPU基准实现4倍吞吐加速和51%能耗降低，GPU利用率超过96%，规划速率超过100Hz，可扩展至多轨迹优化和鲁棒模型预测控制

Conclusion: 提出的GPU原生轨迹优化框架通过大规模并行计算显著提升性能，为实时自主系统提供可扩展的高效解决方案，支持鲁棒控制和多场景优化

Abstract: Real-time trajectory optimization for nonlinear constrained autonomous systems is critical and typically performed by CPU-based sequential solvers. Specifically, reliance on global sparse linear algebra or the serial nature of dynamic programming algorithms restricts the utilization of massively parallel computing architectures like GPUs. To bridge this gap, we introduce a fully GPU-native trajectory optimization framework that combines sequential convex programming with a consensus-based alternating direction method of multipliers. By applying a temporal splitting strategy, our algorithm decouples the optimization horizon into independent, per-node subproblems that execute massively in parallel. The entire process runs fully on the GPU, eliminating costly memory transfers and large-scale sparse factorizations. This architecture naturally scales to multi-trajectory optimization. We validate the solver on a quadrotor agile flight task and a Mars powered descent problem using an on-board edge computing platform. Benchmarks reveal a sustained 4x throughput speedup and a 51% reduction in energy consumption over a heavily optimized 12-core CPU baseline. Crucially, the framework saturates the hardware, maintaining over 96% active GPU utilization to achieve planning rates exceeding 100 Hz. Furthermore, we demonstrate the solver's extensibility to robust Model Predictive Control by jointly optimizing dynamically coupled scenarios under stochastic disturbances, enabling scalable and safe autonomy.

</details>


### [131] [AR-VLA: True Autoregressive Action Expert for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10126)
*Yutong Hu,Jan-Nico Zaech,Nikolay Nikolov,Yuanqi Yao,Sombit Dey,Giuliano Albanese,Renaud Detry,Luc Van Gool,Danda Paudel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AR-VLA：一种自回归动作专家模型，通过持续因果序列生成动作，具备长期记忆和上下文感知能力，解决快速控制与慢速推理的频率不匹配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型和扩散策略在每次新观测时重置时间上下文，以反应式方式预测动作，缺乏长期记忆和上下文一致性，导致动作轨迹不连续。

Method: 1) 设计自回归动作专家，将动作生成为连续因果序列；2) 引入长期记忆机制保持历史上下文；3) 使用重锚定机制同步异步的视觉-语言-动作模态；4) 支持与重型感知骨干的模块化集成。

Result: 在模拟和真实机器人操作任务中，AR-VLA表现出优越的历史感知能力和更平滑的动作轨迹，在保持或超过最先进反应式VLA任务成功率的同时，能有效替代传统的基于块的动作头。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了一个可扩展、上下文感知的动作生成框架，为训练有效的机器人策略提供了稳健的结构基础，解决了VLA模型中的时空一致性问题。

Abstract: We propose a standalone autoregressive (AR) Action Expert that generates actions as a continuous causal sequence while conditioning on refreshable vision-language prefixes. In contrast to existing Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models and diffusion policies that reset temporal context with each new observation and predict actions reactively, our Action Expert maintains its own history through a long-lived memory and is inherently context-aware. This structure addresses the frequency mismatch between fast control and slow reasoning, enabling efficient independent pretraining of kinematic syntax and modular integration with heavy perception backbones, naturally ensuring spatio-temporally consistent action generation across frames. To synchronize these asynchronous hybrid V-L-A modalities, we utilize a re-anchoring mechanism that mathematically accounts for perception staleness during both training and inference. Experiments on simulated and real-robot manipulation tasks demonstrate that the proposed method can effectively replace traditional chunk-based action heads for both specialist and generalist policies. AR-VLA exhibits superior history awareness and substantially smoother action trajectories while maintaining or exceeding the task success rates of state-of-the-art reactive VLAs. Overall, our work introduces a scalable, context-aware action generation schema that provides a robust structural foundation for training effective robotic policies.

</details>


### [132] [Cross-Hand Latent Representation for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10158)
*Guangqi Jiang,Yutong Liang,Jianglong Ye,Jia-Yang Huang,Changwei Jing,Rocky Duan,Pieter Abbeel,Xiaolong Wang,Xueyan Zou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: XL-VLA提出了一种用于灵巧操作的跨具身视觉-语言-动作框架，通过统一的潜在动作空间实现不同灵巧手之间的数据共享和高效训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作对机器人自主性至关重要，人类依赖多模态感知（视觉、声音、语言引导意图）进行灵巧动作。然而，训练可靠的视觉-语言-动作模型需要大规模演示数据，且随着新灵巧手具身的快速出现，为每个具身收集数据成本高昂且不切实际，因此需要可扩展的跨具身学习方法。

Method: 引入XL-VLA框架，集成了跨不同灵巧手共享的统一潜在动作空间。这个具身不变的潜在空间可直接插入标准VLA架构，实现无缝的跨具身训练和现有数据与新收集数据的高效复用。

Result: 实验结果表明，XL-VLA在原始关节空间中持续优于基线VLA模型，证明了其作为可扩展跨具身灵巧操作解决方案的有效性。

Conclusion: XL-VLA通过统一的潜在动作空间解决了跨具身灵巧操作的扩展性问题，为不同灵巧手之间的数据共享和高效训练提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation is essential for real-world robot autonomy, mirroring the central role of human hand coordination in daily activity. Humans rely on rich multimodal perception--vision, sound, and language-guided intent--to perform dexterous actions, motivating vision-based, language-conditioned manipulation systems for robots. However, training reliable vision-language-action (VLA) models for dexterous manipulation requires large-scale demonstrations across many robotic hands. In addition, as new dexterous embodiments appear rapidly, collecting data for each becomes costly and impractical, creating a need for scalable cross-embodiment learning. We introduce XL-VLA, a vision-language-action framework integrated with a unified latent action space shared across diverse dexterous hands. This embodiment-invariant latent space is directly pluggable into standard VLA architectures, enabling seamless cross-embodiment training and efficient reuse of both existing and newly collected data. Experimental results demonstrate that XL-VLA consistently outperforms baseline VLA models operating in raw joint spaces, establishing it as an effective solution for scalable cross-embodiment dexterous manipulation.

</details>


### [133] [Dance2Hesitate: A Multi-Modal Dataset of Dancer-Taught Hesitancy for Understandable Robot Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10166)
*Srikrishna Bangalore Raghu,Anna Soukhovei,Divya Sai Sindhuja Vankineni,Alexandra Bacula,Alessandro Roncone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出并开源了一个多模态、舞者生成的犹豫运动数据集，用于研究人机协作中机器人犹豫表达对人类协调策略的影响，包含机械臂和人体两种形态在不同犹豫程度下的运动数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人机协作中，机器人的犹豫表达是影响人类协调策略、注意力分配和安全判断的关键因素。然而，设计具有泛化能力的犹豫机器人运动具有挑战性，因为观察者的推断高度依赖于具体形态和上下文环境。

Method: 创建了一个多模态数据集，包含两种具体形态-上下文对：1) 机械臂/人体上肢接近Jenga塔，2) 自由空间中的人形全身运动。数据集包括：(i) 在Franka Emika Panda机械臂上通过动觉示教获得的三级犹豫程度（轻微、显著、极端）的运动轨迹；(ii) 同步RGB-D运动捕捉的舞者表演相同伸手行为的运动数据。提供了可重复基准测试的文档。

Result: 收集了70个独特的全身运动轨迹、84个跨越三个犹豫程度的上肢轨迹，以及66个跨越三个犹豫程度的动觉示教轨迹。数据集已开源，可通过指定网址访问。

Conclusion: 该数据集为解决机器人犹豫运动设计的泛化挑战提供了重要资源，支持在人机协作领域进行可重复的基准测试和研究。

Abstract: In human-robot collaboration, a robot's expression of hesitancy is a critical factor that shapes human coordination strategies, attention allocation, and safety-related judgments. However, designing hesitant robot motion that generalizes is challenging because the observer's inference is highly dependent on embodiment and context. To address these challenges, we introduce and open-source a multi-modal, dancer-generated dataset of hesitant motion where we focus on specific context-embodiment pairs (i.e., manipulator/human upper-limb approaching a Jenga Tower, and anthropomorphic whole body motion in free space). The dataset includes (i) kinesthetic teaching demonstrations on a Franka Emika Panda reaching from a fixed start configuration to a fixed target (a Jenga tower) with three graded hesitancy levels (slight, significant, extreme) and (ii) synchronized RGB-D motion capture of dancers performing the same reaching behavior using their upper limb across three hesitancy levels, plus full human body sequences for extreme hesitancy. We further provide documentation to enable reproducible benchmarking across robot and human modalities. Across all dancers, we obtained 70 unique whole-body trajectories, 84 upper limb trajectories spanning over the three hesitancy levels, and 66 kinesthetic teaching trajectories spanning over the three hesitancy levels. The dataset can be accessed here: https://brsrikrishna.github.io/Dance2Hesitate/.

</details>


### [134] [A gripper for flap separation and opening of sealed bags](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10890)
*Sergi Foix,Jaume Oriol,Carme Torras,Júlia Borràs*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开发了一种新型夹持器设计，用于可靠分离和抓取无菌袋薄片，自动化医院中重复性高的开袋任务


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决医院临床环境中护士需要手动打开大量无菌袋的重复性任务，该任务与肌肉骨骼损伤相关，且现有夹持器难以可靠抓取薄而柔性的层状材料

Method: 设计了一种结合主动齿状滚轮指尖和柔性手指的新型夹持器，利用环境约束来可靠抓取薄柔性片，通过两个夹持器同时抓取两个袋片来承受开袋所需的力量

Result: 实验表明该夹持器能可靠抓取和分离密封袋片及其他医院薄层材料，性能最敏感变量是施加的法向力，系统能承受开袋所需力量

Conclusion: 这是首批展示机器人辅助自动化医院重复性关键任务的成果之一，为解决临床环境中薄柔性材料分离挑战提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: Separating thin, flexible layers that must be individually grasped is a common but challenging manipulation primitive for most off-the-shelf grippers. A prominent example arises in clinical settings: the opening of sterile flat pouches for the preparation of the operating room, where the first step is to separate and grasp the flaps. We present a novel gripper design and opening strategy that enables reliable flap separation and robust seal opening. This capability addresses a high-volume repetitive hospital procedure in which nurses manually open up to 240 bags per shift, a physically demanding task linked to musculoskeletal injuries. Our design combines an active dented-roller fingertip with compliant fingers that exploit environmental constraints to robustly grasp thin flexible flaps. Experiments demonstrate that the proposed gripper reliably grasps and separates sealed bag flaps and other thin-layered materials from the hospital, the most sensitive variable affecting performance being the normal force applied. When two copies of the gripper grasp both flaps, the system withstands the forces needed to open the seals robustly. To our knowledge, this is one of the first demonstrations of robotic assistance to automate this repetitive, low-value, but critical hospital task.

</details>


### [135] [Characterizing Healthy & Post-Stroke Neuromotor Behavior During 6D Upper-Limb Isometric Gaming: Implications for Design of End-Effector Rehabilitation Robot Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10173)
*Ajay Anand,Gabriel Parra,Chad A. Berghoff,Laura A. Hallock*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究利用OpenRobotRehab 1.0数据集分析健康和中风后用户在等距轨迹跟踪任务中的神经运动行为，评估界面设计对行为的影响，分析病理特征在末端执行器力数据中的表现，并开发基于HMM的sEMG分类方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人康复需要设计能促进健康运动实践的游戏和机器人干预，但由于用户神经运动行为、游戏界面和物理机器人之间的复杂相互作用，设计系统元素和区分健康/病理行为具有挑战性。

Method: 利用OpenRobotRehab 1.0开放数据集，分析13名健康用户和2名中风后用户在末端执行器康复机器人上执行等距轨迹跟踪任务时的力输出、肌肉激活和游戏表现。采用界面设计影响评估、6D末端执行器力数据分析病理特征，并开发基于隐马尔可夫模型(HMM)的表面肌电信号(sEMG)神经运动行为分类方法。

Result: 任务规范（包括约束轴和用户对跟踪指令的解释）显著影响用户行为；病理相关特征在等距任务执行期间的6D末端执行器力数据中可检测（健康和中风后用户在力误差和平均力产生方面存在显著差异，p=0.05）；健康神经运动策略具有异质性且难以表征；HMM模型能区分健康和中风后神经运动动态，而基于协同作用的分解方法无法实现这种区分。

Conclusion: 研究结果为设计能够促进不同用户群体健康运动策略的自适应末端执行器康复机器人提供了重要启示，强调了界面设计对用户行为的影响，证明了力数据在病理检测中的有效性，以及HMM方法在神经运动动态分类中的优势。

Abstract: Successful robot-mediated rehabilitation requires designing games and robot interventions that promote healthy motor practice. However, the interplay between a given user's neuromotor behavior, the gaming interface, and the physical robot makes designing system elements -- and even characterizing what behaviors are "healthy" or pathological -- challenging. We leverage our OpenRobotRehab 1.0 open access data set to assess the characteristics of 13 healthy and 2 post-stroke users' force output, muscle activations, and game performance while executing isometric trajectory tracking tasks using an end-effector rehabilitation robot. We present an assessment of how subtle aspects of interface design impact user behavior; an analysis of how pathological neuromotor behaviors are reflected in end-effector force dynamics; and a novel hidden Markov model (HMM)-based neuromotor behavior classification method based on surface electromyography (sEMG) signals during cyclic motions. We demonstrate that task specification (including which axes are constrained and how users interpret tracking instructions) shapes user behavior; that pathology-related features are detectable in 6D end-effector force data during isometric task execution (with significant differences between healthy and post-stroke profiles in force error and average force production at $p=0.05$); and that healthy neuromotor strategies are heterogeneous and inherently difficult to characterize. We also show that our HMM-based models discriminate healthy and post-stroke neuromotor dynamics where synergy-based decompositions reflect no such differentiation. Lastly, we discuss these results' implications for the design of adaptive end-effector rehabilitation robots capable of promoting healthier movement strategies across diverse user populations.

</details>


### [136] [Octopus-inspired Distributed Control for Soft Robotic Arms: A Graph Neural Network-Based Attention Policy with Environmental Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10198)
*Linxin Hou,Qirui Wu,Zhihang Qin,Yongxin Guo,Cecilia Laschi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SoftGM是一种受章鱼启发的分布式控制架构，用于分段软体机械臂，能够在接触丰富的环境中通过在线障碍物发现学习到达目标，无需依赖全局障碍物几何信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决软体机械臂在复杂接触环境中自主导航的挑战，传统方法需要全局环境信息，而SoftGM旨在通过分布式学习和在线障碍物发现实现自适应控制。

Method: 将每个臂段建模为协作智能体，将臂-环境交互表示为图，采用两阶段图注意力消息传递方案，遵循集中训练分散执行范式，使用集中式评论家和分散式执行器。

Result: 在Cosserat-rod模拟器中评估，SoftGM在简单环境中与强CTDE方法相当，在墙洞任务中表现最佳；在观测噪声、单段驱动故障和瞬态干扰的鲁棒性测试中保持成功且控制努力有界。

Conclusion: SoftGM通过选择性接触相关信息路由实现弹性协调，为软体机械臂在复杂接触环境中的分布式控制提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes SoftGM, an octopus-inspired distributed control architecture for segmented soft robotic arms that learn to reach targets in contact-rich environments using online obstacle discovery without relying on global obstacle geometry. SoftGM formulates each arm section as a cooperative agent and represents the arm-environment interaction as a graph. SoftGM uses a two-stage graph attention message passing scheme following a Centralised Training Decentralised Execution (CTDE) paradigm with a centralised critic and decentralised actor. We evaluate SoftGM in a Cosserat-rod simulator (PyElastica) across three tasks that increase the complexity of the environment: obstacle-free, structured obstacles, and a wall-with-hole scenario. Compared with six widely used MARL baselines (IDDPG, IPPO, ISAC, MADDPG, MAPPO, MASAC) under identical information content and training conditions, SoftGM matches strong CTDE methods in simpler settings and achieves the best performance in the wall-with-hole task. Robustness tests with observation noise, single-section actuation failure, and transient disturbances show that SoftGM preserves success while keeping control effort bounded, indicating resilient coordination driven by selective contact-relevant information routing.

</details>


### [137] [Perceptive Hierarchical-Task MPC for Sequential Mobile Manipulation in Unstructured Semi-Static Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10227)
*Xintong Du,Jingxing Qian,Siqi Zhou,Angela P. Schoellig*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出感知分层任务模型预测控制框架，用于动态环境中高效的顺序移动操作


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顺序移动操作面临独特挑战：机器人长时间运行时，任务成功不仅依赖一致的运动生成，还需要对环境变化的感知和适应。现有运动规划器通常假设环境静态且依赖预计算地图，这在长期操作中常因半静态变化（如物体移除、引入或移动）而失效。

Method: 提出感知分层任务模型预测控制框架：1）使用贝叶斯推理框架显式建模物体级变化，维持时间准确的3D环境表示；2）将最新环境表示嵌入词典优化框架，实现顺序任务的高效执行。

Result: 通过仿真和真实机器人实验验证：相比基线方法，该方法能系统处理移动和幻影障碍物，以更高效率和反应性成功完成顺序任务，无需先验地图或外部基础设施。

Conclusion: 提出的感知HTMPC框架能有效处理动态环境中的顺序移动操作，通过显式建模环境变化和集成最新感知信息，实现鲁棒且高效的任务执行。

Abstract: As compared to typical mobile manipulation tasks, sequential mobile manipulation poses a unique challenge -- as the robot operates over extended periods, successful task completion is not solely dependent on consistent motion generation but also on the robot's awareness and adaptivity to changes in the operating environment. While existing motion planners can generate whole-body trajectories to complete sequential tasks, they typically assume that the environment remains static and rely on precomputed maps. This assumption often breaks down during long-term operations, where semi-static changes such as object removal, introduction, or shifts are common. In this work, we propose a novel perceptive hierarchical-task model predictive control (HTMPC) framework for efficient sequential mobile manipulation in unstructured, changing environments. To tackle the challenge, we leverage a Bayesian inference framework to explicitly model object-level changes and thereby maintain a temporally accurate representation of the 3D environment; this up-to-date representation is embedded in a lexicographic optimization framework to enable efficient execution of sequential tasks. We validate our perceptive HTMPC approach through both simulated and real-robot experiments. In contrast to baseline methods, our approach systematically accounts for moved and phantom obstacles, successfully completing sequential tasks with higher efficiency and reactivity, without relying on prior maps or external infrastructure.

</details>


### [138] [Hierarchical Task Model Predictive Control for Sequential Mobile Manipulation Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10232)
*Xintong Du,Siqi Zhou,Angela P. Schoellig*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于非线性词典优化的分层任务模型预测控制框架，用于移动机械臂的顺序任务执行，相比现有方法在轨迹跟踪性能上平均提升42%，执行时间快2.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型的发展，任务规划器能够将人类语言指令转化为任务序列，但需要一种决策算法能够与高层任务规划器无缝对接，高效执行任务序列。现有方法在任务切换、机器人奇异性和参考变化时性能不足。

Method: 基于非线性词典优化思想，提出分层任务模型预测控制框架，通过有效利用机器人冗余度来提升顺序任务执行性能。该方法能够处理任务优先级，在任务空间实现更优的轨迹跟踪。

Result: 相比最先进的任务优先级逆运动学控制方法，在任务变化、机器人奇异性和参考变化时，分层轨迹跟踪性能平均提升42%。相比典型的单任务架构，在任务空间中路径更短，执行一系列递送任务的执行时间快2.3倍。在9自由度移动机械臂上进行了真实世界实验验证。

Conclusion: 提出的分层任务模型预测控制框架能够高效执行顺序任务，通过有效利用机器人冗余度，显著提升了移动机械臂在复杂环境中的反应性和性能，为日常生活中的复杂角色服务提供了更好的控制解决方案。

Abstract: Mobile manipulators are envisioned to serve more complex roles in people's everyday lives. With recent breakthroughs in large language models, task planners have become better at translating human verbal instructions into a sequence of tasks. However, there is still a need for a decision-making algorithm that can seamlessly interface with the high-level task planner to carry out the sequence of tasks efficiently. In this work, building on the idea of nonlinear lexicographic optimization, we propose a novel Hierarchical-Task Model Predictive Control framework that is able to complete sequential tasks with improved performance and reactivity by effectively leveraging the robot's redundancy. Compared to the state-of-the-art task-prioritized inverse kinematic control method, our approach has improved hierarchical trajectory tracking performance by 42% on average when facing task changes, robot singularity and reference variations. Compared to a typical single-task architecture, our proposed hierarchical task control architecture enables the robot to traverse a shorter path in task space and achieves an execution time 2.3 times faster when executing a sequence of delivery tasks. We demonstrated the results with real-world experiments on a 9 degrees of freedom mobile manipulator.

</details>


### [139] [Degeneracy-Resilient Teach and Repeat for Geometrically Challenging Environments Using FMCW Lidar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10248)
*Katya M. Papais,Wenda Zhao,Timothy D. Barfoot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于FMCW激光雷达的退化鲁棒性Teach and Repeat导航系统，通过多普勒测速里程计和退化感知的扫描到地图定位，解决传统ICP方法在几何退化环境中的失效问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Teach and Repeat导航系统依赖ICP估计，但在几何退化环境（如稀疏结构地形）中，ICP容易变得病态，导致定位性能下降和导航不可靠。需要一种能在退化环境中稳定工作的导航解决方案。

Method: 1) 基于FMCW激光雷达的多普勒测速里程计，利用多普勒效应获取每点径向速度，实现几何无关、无需对应关系的运动估计，并包含稳定的位姿不确定性估计；2) 退化感知定位方法，结合每点曲率改进数据关联，统一平移和旋转尺度以实现一致的退化检测。

Result: 在三个不同结构丰富度的环境中进行闭环现场实验，证明所提系统能够可靠完成自主导航，包括在具有挑战性的平坦机场测试场中，而传统基于ICP的系统在此失败。

Conclusion: 提出的FMCW激光雷达T&R导航系统通过多普勒测速里程计和退化感知定位，有效解决了传统ICP方法在几何退化环境中的局限性，为GPS拒止环境下的可靠导航提供了解决方案。

Abstract: Teach and Repeat (T&R) topometric navigation enables robots to autonomously repeat previously traversed paths without relying on GPS, making it well suited for operations in GPS-denied environments such as underground mines and lunar navigation. State-of-the-art T&R systems typically rely on iterative closest point (ICP)-based estimation; however, in geometrically degenerate environments with sparsely structured terrain, ICP often becomes ill-conditioned, resulting in degraded localization and unreliable navigation performance. To address this challenge, we present a degeneracy-resilient Frequency-Modulated Continuous-Wave (FMCW) lidar T&R navigation system consisting of Doppler velocity-based odometry and degeneracy-aware scan-to-map localization. Leveraging FMCW lidar, which provides per-point radial velocity measurements via the Doppler effect, we extend a geometry-independent, correspondence-free motion estimation to include principled pose uncertainty estimation that remains stable in degenerate environments. We further propose a degeneracy-aware localization method that incorporates per-point curvature for improved data association, and unifies translational and rotational scales to enable consistent degeneracy detection. Closed-loop field experiments across three environments with varying structural richness demonstrate that the proposed system reliably completes autonomous navigation, including in a challenging flat airport test field where a conventional ICP-based system fails.

</details>


### [140] [From Prior to Pro: Efficient Skill Mastery via Distribution Contractive RL Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10263)
*Zhanyi Sun,Shuran Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DICE-RL是一个使用强化学习作为"分布收缩"操作符的框架，通过在线反馈放大高成功率行为，将预训练的生成式机器人策略精炼为高性能策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的预训练生成式策略虽然具有广泛的行为覆盖，但性能可能不够理想。需要一种方法能够稳定、高效地将这些行为先验转化为高性能策略，同时保持样本效率和稳定性。

Method: 首先预训练基于扩散或流的策略以获得广泛的行为覆盖，然后使用结合选择性行为正则化和价值引导动作选择的残差离策略RL框架进行微调。该框架将RL作为"分布收缩"操作符，放大高成功率行为。

Result: DICE-RL在模拟和真实机器人上都能可靠地提升性能，具有强大的稳定性和样本效率。能够直接从高维像素输入掌握复杂的长时程操作技能。

Conclusion: DICE-RL提供了一个有效的框架，通过将强化学习作为分布收缩操作符，将预训练的生成式策略转化为高性能策略，在机器人技能学习方面展现出显著优势。

Abstract: We introduce Distribution Contractive Reinforcement Learning (DICE-RL), a framework that uses reinforcement learning (RL) as a "distribution contraction" operator to refine pretrained generative robot policies. DICE-RL turns a pretrained behavior prior into a high-performing "pro" policy by amplifying high-success behaviors from online feedback. We pretrain a diffusion- or flow-based policy for broad behavioral coverage, then finetune it with a stable, sample-efficient residual off-policy RL framework that combines selective behavior regularization with value-guided action selection. Extensive experiments and analyses show that DICE-RL reliably improves performance with strong stability and sample efficiency. It enables mastery of complex long-horizon manipulation skills directly from high-dimensional pixel inputs, both in simulation and on a real robot. Project website: https://zhanyisun.github.io/dice.rl.2026/.

</details>


### [141] [Design of a Robot-Assisted Chemical Dialysis System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10264)
*Diane Jung,Caleb Escobedo,Noah Liska,Maitrey Gramopadhye,Daniel Szafir,Alessandro Roncone,Carson Bruns*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究人员开发了一种用于透析实验的机器人辅助系统，通过用户中心设计方法优化系统，旨在减轻科学家在长时间实验中的工作负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学家需要执行繁琐耗时的实验操作，这些手动程序成为现代实验科学的瓶颈，消耗时间并增加材料科学和医学等领域的工作负担。

Method: 采用用户中心设计方法，通过两次可用性研究收集参与者反馈，修订设计需求，开发最终满足科学家需求的机器人辅助透析系统。

Result: 开发出满足科学家需求的最终系统，该系统有潜力应用于其他实验工作流程，通过整合到化学湿实验室的实际合成程序中可减少科学家在长时间实验中的工作负担。

Conclusion: 该系统为设计更多加速科学发现、解放科学家免于繁琐劳动的机器人辅助系统提供了有效方法，有望在化学湿实验室的实际应用中发挥作用。

Abstract: Scientists perform diverse manual procedures that are tedious and laborious. Such procedures are considered a bottleneck for modern experimental science, as they consume time and increase burdens in fields including material science and medicine. We employ a user-centered approach to designing a robot-assisted system for dialysis, a common multi-day purification method used in polymer and protein synthesis. Through two usability studies, we obtain participant feedback and revise design requirements to develop the final system that satisfies scientists' needs and has the potential for applications in other experimental workflows. We anticipate that integration of this system into real synthesis procedures in a chemical wet lab will decrease workload on scientists during long experimental procedures and provide an effective approach to designing more systems that have the potential to accelerate scientific discovery and liberate scientists from tedious labor.

</details>


### [142] [Update-Free On-Policy Steering via Verifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10282)
*Maria Attarian,Ian Vyse,Claas Voelcker,Jasper Gerigk,Evgenii Opryshko,Anas Almasri,Sumeet Singh,Yilun Du,Igor Gilitschenski*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UF-OPS：一种无需更新的策略引导方法，通过验证器函数预测动作成功概率，在运行时自适应调整策略，提升行为克隆策略的鲁棒性和精确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为克隆（BC）方法虽然流行，但策略往往脆弱且难以处理精确操作任务。现有方法在执行时缺乏自适应调整能力，导致策略在复杂场景中表现不佳。

Method: 提出UF-OPS（Update-Free On-Policy Steering）方法：1）在策略初始评估阶段收集策略执行数据；2）使用这些数据训练验证器函数来预测动作成功概率；3）在执行时使用验证器引导基础策略选择更高成功概率的动作。该方法不改变基础策略参数，保持轻量级和灵活性。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界实验中，该方法对黑盒扩散策略实现了平均49%的成功率提升，覆盖5个真实任务。验证了方法在不修改基础策略参数情况下的有效性。

Conclusion: UF-OPS通过运行时策略引导显著提升了行为克隆策略的鲁棒性和性能，为机器人精确操作任务提供了一种轻量级、灵活的增强方法。

Abstract: In recent years, Behavior Cloning (BC) has become one of the most prevalent methods for enabling robots to mimic human demonstrations. However, despite their successes, BC policies are often brittle and struggle with precise manipulation. To overcome these issues, we propose UF-OPS, an Update-Free On-Policy Steering method that enables the robot to predict the success likelihood of its actions and adapt its strategy at execution time. We accomplish this by training verifier functions using policy rollout data obtained during an initial evaluation of the policy. These verifiers are subsequently used to steer the base policy toward actions with a higher likelihood of success. Our method improves the performance of black-box diffusion policy, without changing the base parameters, making it light-weight and flexible. We present results from both simulation and real-world data and achieve an average 49% improvement in success rate over the base policy across 5 real tasks.

</details>


### [143] [SteadyTray: Learning Object Balancing Tasks in Humanoid Tray Transport via Residual Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10306)
*Anlun Huang,Zhenyu Wu,Soofiyan Atar,Yuheng Zhi,Michael Yip*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ReST-RL分层强化学习架构，将双足机器人运动与负载稳定解耦，通过SteadyTray基准评估，显著提升托盘运输稳定性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决双足机器人在非结构化环境中动态运动时负载不稳定的关键工程瓶颈，传统端到端学习方法难以同时保证运动稳定性和负载稳定性

Method: 采用分层强化学习架构，包含鲁棒的基础运动策略和动态残差模块，残差模块专门用于抵消步态引起的末端执行器扰动，实现运动与负载稳定的解耦

Result: 在仿真中显著优于端到端基线方法，步态平滑度和方向精度提升，变速度跟踪成功率96.9%，外力干扰鲁棒性74.5%，成功在Unitree G1人形机器人硬件上实现零样本仿真到现实的泛化

Conclusion: ReST-RL的分层架构有效解决了双足机器人负载稳定问题，模块化设计实现了可靠的零样本仿真到现实迁移，为动态环境中的稳定运输提供了可行方案

Abstract: Stabilizing unsecured payloads against the inherent oscillations of dynamic bipedal locomotion remains a critical engineering bottleneck for humanoids in unstructured environments. To solve this, we introduce ReST-RL, a hierarchical reinforcement learning architecture that explicitly decouples locomotion from payload stabilization, evaluated via the SteadyTray benchmark. Rather than relying on monolithic end-to-end learning, our framework integrates a robust base locomotion policy with a dynamic residual module engineered to actively cancel gait-induced perturbations at the end-effector. This architectural separation ensures steady tray transport without degrading the underlying bipedal stability. In simulation, the residual design significantly outperforms end-to-end baselines in gait smoothness and orientation accuracy, achieving a 96.9% success rate in variable velocity tracking and 74.5% robustness against external force disturbances. Successfully deployed on the Unitree G1 humanoid hardware, this modular approach demonstrates highly reliable zero-shot sim-to-real generalization across various objects and external force disturbances.

</details>


### [144] [PC-Diffuser: Path-Consistent Capsule CBF Safety Filtering for Diffusion-Based Trajectory Planner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10330)
*Eugene Ku,Yiwei Lyu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PC-Diffuser：一种将可认证的安全屏障函数结构嵌入扩散规划去噪循环的安全增强框架，通过路径一致的安全过滤实现自动驾驶轨迹生成的内生安全保障


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂交通中的自动驾驶需要超越手工规则的数据驱动规划器，但基于扩散的轨迹规划器虽然闭环性能强，却难以认证且在罕见或分布外场景可能发生灾难性故障，需要解决安全认证问题

Method: PC-Diffuser框架：1)使用胶囊距离屏障函数评估碰撞风险，更好反映车辆几何并减少不必要的保守性；2)将去噪路径点转换为基于运动学自行车模型的动态可行运动；3)应用路径一致的安全过滤器消除残余约束违规而不产生几何失真；在每一步去噪中注入安全一致修正并将精炼轨迹反馈回扩散过程

Result: PC-Diffuser实现了迭代的、上下文感知的安全保障而非后处理修复，使安全成为轨迹生成的内在部分而非事后修正，同时保持扩散模型的预期路径几何

Conclusion: 该框架通过将可认证的安全屏障函数结构直接嵌入扩散规划的去噪循环，解决了数据驱动规划器的安全认证挑战，实现了安全与学习性能的统一

Abstract: Autonomous driving in complex traffic requires planners that generalize beyond hand-crafted rules, motivating data-driven approaches that learn behavior from expert demonstrations. Diffusion-based trajectory planners have recently shown strong closed-loop performance by iteratively denoising a full-horizon plan, but they remain difficult to certify and can fail catastrophically in rare or out-of-distribution scenarios. To address this challenge, we present PC-Diffuser, a safety augmentation framework that embeds a certifiable, path-consistent barrier-function structure directly into the denoising loop of diffusion planning. The key idea is to make safety an intrinsic part of trajectory generation rather than a post-hoc fix: we enforce forward invariance along the rollout while preserving the diffusion model's intended path geometry. Specifically, PC-Diffuser (i) evaluates collision risk using a capsule-distance barrier function that better reflects vehicle geometry and reduces unnecessary conservativeness, (ii) converts denoised waypoints into dynamically feasible motion under a kinematic bicycle model, and (iii) applies a path-consistent safety filter that eliminates residual constraint violations without geometric distortion, so the corrected plan remains close to the learned distribution. By injecting these safety-consistent corrections at every denoising step and feeding the refined trajectory back into the diffusion process, PC-Diffuser enables iterative, context-aware safeguarding instead of post-hoc repair...

</details>


### [145] [Adaptive Manipulation Potential and Haptic Estimation for Tool-Mediated Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10352)
*Lin Yang,Anirvan Dutta,Yuan Ji,Yanxin Zhou,Shilin Shan,Lv Chen,Etienne Burdet,Domenico Campolo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于参数化平衡流形(EM)的统一工具操作表示框架，结合触觉估计、在线规划和自适应刚度控制，通过触觉SLAM实现稳健的螺丝松动操作


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决接触丰富的工具操作中视觉遮挡和触觉感知不确定性的挑战，实现人类水平的灵巧操作能力

Method: 1) 提出参数化平衡流形(EM)作为工具交互的统一表示；2) 建立物理-几何对偶性，使用包含可微接触模型的自适应操作势能；3) 将触觉估计重构为流形参数估计问题，采用混合推理策略（触觉SLAM）——离散物体形状通过粒子滤波分类，连续位姿通过解析梯度优化估计；4) 动态更新操作势能参数，重塑诱导的EM以指导在线轨迹重规划和实现不确定性感知的阻抗控制

Result: 在仿真和超过260次真实世界螺丝松动试验中验证系统性能，实验结果显示：在标准场景中实现稳健的识别和操作成功，同时保持准确跟踪；消融研究确认触觉SLAM和不确定性感知刚度调制优于固定阻抗基线，有效防止紧密公差交互中的卡滞现象

Conclusion: 提出的基于平衡流形的框架通过物理-几何对偶性统一了触觉感知和操作控制，实现了闭环的感知-动作系统，在接触丰富的工具操作任务中表现出优越性能

Abstract: Achieving human-level dexterity in contact-rich, tool-mediated manipulation remains a significant challenge due to visual occlusion and the underdetermined nature of haptic sensing. This paper introduces a parameterized Equilibrium Manifold (EM) as a unified representation for tool-mediated interaction, and develops a closed-loop framework that integrates haptic estimation, online planning, and adaptive stiffness control. We establish a physical-geometric duality using an adaptive manipulation potential incorporating a differentiable contact model, which induces the manifold's geometric structure and ensures that complex physical interactions are encapsulated as continuous operations on the EM. Within this framework, we reformulate haptic estimation as a manifold parameter estimation problem. Specifically, a hybrid inference strategy (haptic SLAM) is employed in which discrete object shapes are classified via particle filtering, while the continuous object pose is estimated using analytical gradients for efficient optimization. By continuously updating the parameters of the manipulation potential, the framework dynamically reshapes the induced EM to guide online trajectory replanning and implement uncertainty-aware impedance control, thereby closing the perception-action loop. The system is validated through simulation and over 260 real-world screw-loosening trials. Experimental results demonstrate robust identification and manipulation success in standard scenarios while maintaining accurate tracking. Furthermore, ablation studies confirm that haptic SLAM and uncertainty-aware stiffness modulation outperform fixed impedance baselines, effectively preventing jamming during tight tolerance interactions.

</details>


### [146] [Few-Shot Adaptation to Non-Stationary Environments via Latent Trend Embedding for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10373)
*Yasuyuki Fujii,Emika Kameda,Hiroki Fukada,Yoshiki Mori,Tadashi Matsuo,Nobutaka Shimada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于潜在趋势ID的少样本适应框架，通过估计低维环境状态而非修改模型参数来应对概念漂移，避免灾难性遗忘并降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人系统在真实环境中常面临概念漂移问题，即输入输出关系因潜在环境因素变化而改变。传统适应方法通过更新模型参数会导致灾难性遗忘和高计算成本。

Method: 提出潜在趋势ID框架：1) 通过反向传播估计低维环境状态(趋势ID)，保持模型参数固定；2) 引入时间正则化和状态转移模型，确保潜在空间平滑演化，防止过拟合。

Result: 在定量食物抓取任务实验中，学习到的趋势ID在潜在空间中分布在不同的区域，具有时间一致的轨迹，并且能够在少样本情况下适应未见环境，无需修改模型参数。

Conclusion: 该框架为在多样化和演化环境中运行的机器人应用提供了可扩展且可解释的解决方案，通过估计环境状态而非修改模型权重来实现适应。

Abstract: Robotic systems operating in real-world environments often suffer from concept shift, where the input-output relationship changes due to latent environmental factors that are not directly observable. Conventional adaptation methods update model parameters, which may cause catastrophic forgetting and incur high computational cost. This paper proposes a latent Trend ID-based framework for few-shot adaptation in non-stationary environments. Instead of modifying model weights, a low-dimensional environmental state, referred to as the Trend ID, is estimated via backpropagation while the model parameters remain fixed. To prevent overfitting caused by per-sample latent variables, we introduce temporal regularization and a state transition model that enforces smooth evolution of the latent space. Experiments on a quantitative food grasping task demonstrate that the learned Trend IDs are distributed across distinct regions of the latent space with temporally consistent trajectories, and that few-shot adaptation to unseen environments is achieved without modifying model parameters. The proposed framework provides a scalable and interpretable solution for robotics applications operating across diverse and evolving environments.

</details>


### [147] [ScanDP: Generalizable 3D Scanning with Diffusion Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10390)
*Itsuki Hirako,Ryo Hakoda,Yubin Liu,Matthew Hwang,Yoshihiro Sato,Takeshi Oishi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于扩散策略的数据高效3D扫描框架，通过模仿人类扫描策略，使用占据栅格映射和混合空间表示方法，在未见物体上实现高覆盖率和短路径的扫描


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的3D扫描方法需要大量训练数据且泛化能力有限，难以适应未见物体类别。需要开发数据高效且能泛化到新物体类别的扫描方法

Method: 采用扩散策略模仿人类扫描策略；使用占据栅格映射替代直接点云处理以提高噪声鲁棒性；结合球体空间表示和路径优化确保路径安全性和扫描效率

Result: 在形状和尺度各异的未见物体上评估，相比基线方法获得更高覆盖率和更短路径，对传感器噪声保持鲁棒，并在真实世界执行中验证了实用可行性和稳定操作

Conclusion: 提出的数据高效3D扫描框架通过扩散策略和占据栅格映射，成功解决了传统模仿学习的冗余和不可预测行为问题，实现了对未见物体的有效泛化和实际应用

Abstract: Learning-based 3D Scanning plays a crucial role in enabling efficient and accurate scanning of target objects. However, recent reinforcement learning-based methods often require large-scale training data and still struggle to generalize to unseen object categories.In this work, we propose a data-efficient 3D scanning framework that uses Diffusion Policy to imitate human-like scanning strategies. To enhance robustness and generalization, we adopt the Occupancy Grid Mapping instead of direct point cloud processing, offering improved noise resilience and handling of diverse object geometries. We also introduce a hybrid approach combining a sphere-based space representation with a path optimization procedure that ensures path safety and scanning efficiency. This approach addresses limitations in conventional imitation learning, such as redundant or unpredictable behavior. We evaluate our method on diverse unseen objects in both shape and scale. Ours achieves higher coverage and shorter paths than baselines, while remaining robust to sensor noise. We further confirm practical feasibility and stable operation in real-world execution.

</details>


### [148] [Safe Probabilistic Planning for Human-Robot Interaction using Conformal Risk Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10392)
*Jake Gonzales,Kazuki Mizuta,Karen Leung,Lillian J. Ratliff*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出结合控制屏障函数与保形风险控制的概率安全控制框架，为人机交互提供形式化安全保证，通过动态调整安全边界降低碰撞率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决人机交互中复杂人类行为带来的安全挑战，传统方法难以在动态环境中提供形式化安全保证，需要量化预测误差并建立概率约束满足保证

Method: 结合控制屏障函数与保形风险控制，使用保形风险控制量化CBF安全值的预测误差，建立约束满足概率的形式化保证，引入基于交互上下文动态调整安全边界的算法

Result: 在人机导航场景实验中，相比基线方法显著降低碰撞率和安全违规，同时保持高目标到达成功率和高效控制性能

Conclusion: 提出的概率安全控制框架为人机交互提供了形式化安全保证，能有效处理复杂人类行为，在安全性和任务性能间取得良好平衡

Abstract: In this paper, we present a novel probabilistic safe control framework for human-robot interaction that combines control barrier functions (CBFs) with conformal risk control to provide formal safety guarantees while considering complex human behavior. The approach uses conformal risk control to quantify and control the prediction errors in CBF safety values and establishes formal guarantees on the probability of constraint satisfaction during interaction. We introduce an algorithm that dynamically adjusts the safety margins produced by conformal risk control based on the current interaction context. Through experiments on human-robot navigation scenarios, we demonstrate that our approach significantly reduces collision rates and safety violations as compared to baseline methods while maintaining high success rates in goal-reaching tasks and efficient control. The code, simulations, and other supplementary material can be found on the project website: https://jakeagonzales.github.io/crc-cbf-website/.

</details>


### [149] [Rethinking Gaussian Trajectory Predictors: Calibrated Uncertainty for Safe Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10407)
*Fatemeh Cheraghi Pouria,Mahsa Golchoubian,Katherine Driggs-Campbell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于核密度估计和卡方分布匹配的新型损失函数，用于校准高斯轨迹预测器的不确定性置信水平，提高下游不确定性感知运动规划的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高斯轨迹预测器主要依赖负对数似然损失，容易产生过度自信或不足自信的分布，导致不确定性置信水平不可靠。当预测器与不确定性感知规划器集成时，这种不可靠性可能导致不安全或过于保守的运动规划，影响下游规划器的安全性。

Method: 提出一种新颖的损失函数用于校准预测不确定性：1) 利用核密度估计估计置信水平的经验分布；2) 通过显式匹配估计的经验分布与卡方分布，强制与高斯假设属性一致；3) 为确保准确的均值预测，在最终损失公式中加入了均方误差项。

Result: 在真实世界轨迹数据集上的实验结果表明，该方法显著提高了不同最先进高斯轨迹预测器预测的置信水平的可靠性。通过将使用该损失函数训练的高斯轨迹预测器与不确定性感知模型预测控制集成，在从真实世界数据集提取的场景中实现了改进的规划性能，通过校准的置信水平实现了无碰撞导航。

Conclusion: 提出的损失函数能有效校准高斯轨迹预测器的不确定性置信水平，为规划器提供可靠的概率洞察，在复杂场景中实现更好的碰撞避免和规划性能，证明了为规划器提供校准置信水平的重要性。

Abstract: Accurate trajectory prediction is critical for safe autonomous navigation in crowded environments. While many trajectory predictors output Gaussian distributions to represent the multi-modal distribution over future pedestrian positions, the reliability of their confidence levels often remains unaddressed. This limitation can lead to unsafe or overly conservative motion planning when the predictor is integrated with an uncertainty-aware planner. Existing Gaussian trajectory predictors primarily rely on the Negative Log-Likelihood loss, which is prone to predict over- or under-confident distributions, and may compromise downstream planner safety. This paper introduces a novel loss function for calibrating prediction uncertainty which leverages Kernel Density Estimation to estimate the empirical distribution of confidence levels. The proposed formulation enforces consistency with the properties of a Gaussian assumption by explicitly matching the estimated empirical distribution to the Chi-squared distribution. To ensure accurate mean prediction, a Mean Squared Error term is also incorporated in the final loss formulation. Experimental results on real-world trajectory datasets show that our method significantly improves the reliability of confidence levels predicted by different State-Of-The-Art Gaussian trajectory predictors. We also demonstrate the importance of providing planners with reliable probabilistic insights (i.e. calibrated confidence levels) for collision-free navigation in complex scenarios. For this purpose, we integrate Gaussian trajectory predictors trained with our loss function with an uncertainty-aware Model Predictive Control on scenarios extracted from real-world datasets, achieving improved planning performance through calibrated confidence levels.

</details>


### [150] [AsyncMDE: Real-Time Monocular Depth Estimation via Asynchronous Spatial Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10438)
*Lianjie Ma,Yuquan Li,Bingzheng Jiang,Ziming Zhong,Han Ding,Lijun Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AsyncMDE：一种异步深度感知系统，通过基础模型和轻量级模型的异步协作，在边缘设备上实现实时单目深度估计，显著降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于基础模型的单目深度估计计算成本高，难以部署在边缘平台；现有方法每帧独立推理，浪费了连续机器人操作中相邻视角间的计算冗余。

Method: 系统由基础模型和轻量级模型组成：基础模型在后台生成高质量空间特征；轻量级模型在前台异步运行，通过互补融合将缓存记忆与当前观测结合，输出深度估计并自回归更新记忆，实现跨帧特征重用。

Result: 仅3.83M参数，在RTX 4090上达到237 FPS，恢复基础模型77%的精度差距，参数减少25倍；在室内静态、动态和合成极端运动基准测试中验证，在Jetson AGX Orin上通过TensorRT实现161FPS。

Conclusion: AsyncMDE通过异步架构和特征重用，在保持精度的同时显著降低计算成本，证明了在边缘设备上实时部署的可行性，为机器人感知提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Foundation-model-based monocular depth estimation offers a viable alternative to active sensors for robot perception, yet its computational cost often prohibits deployment on edge platforms. Existing methods perform independent per-frame inference, wasting the substantial computational redundancy between adjacent viewpoints in continuous robot operation. This paper presents AsyncMDE, an asynchronous depth perception system consisting of a foundation model and a lightweight model that amortizes the foundation model's computational cost over time. The foundation model produces high-quality spatial features in the background, while the lightweight model runs asynchronously in the foreground, fusing cached memory with current observations through complementary fusion, outputting depth estimates, and autoregressively updating the memory. This enables cross-frame feature reuse with bounded accuracy degradation. At a mere 3.83M parameters, it operates at 237 FPS on an RTX 4090, recovering 77% of the accuracy gap to the foundation model while achieving a 25X parameter reduction. Validated across indoor static, dynamic, and synthetic extreme-motion benchmarks, AsyncMDE degrades gracefully between refreshes and achieves 161FPS on a Jetson AGX Orin with TensorRT, clearly demonstrating its feasibility for real-time edge deployment.

</details>


### [151] [KnowDiffuser: A Knowledge-Guided Diffusion Planner with LM Reasoning and Prior-Informed Trajectory Initialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10441)
*Fan Ding,Xuewen Luo,Fengze Yang,Bo Yu,HwaHui Tew,Ganesh Krishnasamy,Junn Yong Loo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: KnowDiffuser：一种知识引导的运动规划框架，将语言模型的语义理解与扩散模型的生成能力相结合，用于自动驾驶运动规划


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型具有强大的语义推理能力但无法生成连续物理可行轨迹，扩散模型能生成可靠轨迹但缺乏语义可解释性和场景理解对齐，需要弥合自动驾驶中的语义-物理鸿沟

Method: 使用语言模型从结构化场景表示中推断上下文感知元动作，将其映射到先验轨迹锚定去噪过程，采用两阶段截断去噪机制高效细化轨迹，保持语义对齐和物理可行性

Result: 在nuPlan基准测试中，KnowDiffuser在开环和闭环评估中显著优于现有规划器，建立了鲁棒且可解释的框架

Conclusion: KnowDiffuser有效弥合了自动驾驶系统中的语义-物理鸿沟，将语言模型的语义理解与扩散模型的生成能力紧密结合

Abstract: Recent advancements in Language Models (LMs) have demonstrated strong semantic reasoning capabilities, enabling their application in high-level decision-making for autonomous driving (AD). However, LMs operate over discrete token spaces and lack the ability to generate continuous, physically feasible trajectories required for motion planning. Meanwhile, diffusion models have proven effective at generating reliable and dynamically consistent trajectories, but often lack semantic interpretability and alignment with scene-level understanding. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{KnowDiffuser}, a knowledge-guided motion planning framework that tightly integrates the semantic understanding of language models with the generative power of diffusion models. The framework employs a language model to infer context-aware meta-actions from structured scene representations, which are then mapped to prior trajectories that anchor the subsequent denoising process. A two-stage truncated denoising mechanism refines these trajectories efficiently, preserving both semantic alignment and physical feasibility. Experiments on the nuPlan benchmark demonstrate that KnowDiffuser significantly outperforms existing planners in both open-loop and closed-loop evaluations, establishing a robust and interpretable framework that effectively bridges the semantic-to-physical gap in AD systems.

</details>


### [152] [FAR-Dex: Few-shot Data Augmentation and Adaptive Residual Policy Refinement for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10451)
*Yushan Bai,Fulin Chen,Hongzheng Sun,Yuchuang Tong,En Li,Zhengtao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FAR-Dex是一个用于灵巧操作的分层框架，通过少样本数据增强和自适应残差精炼实现鲁棒的臂-手协调


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多指手与机械臂协作实现类人灵巧操作面临高质量演示稀缺和高维动作空间复杂性的挑战

Method: 提出FAR-Dex分层框架：1) FAR-DexGen利用IsaacLab模拟器从少量演示生成多样化物理约束轨迹；2) FAR-DexRes引入自适应残差模块，结合多步轨迹段和观测特征精炼策略

Result: 实验表明：数据质量提升13.4%，任务成功率提高7%优于SOTA方法；真实世界任务成功率超过80%，具有强位置泛化能力

Conclusion: FAR-Dex通过少样本数据增强和自适应残差精炼，实现了鲁棒精确的臂-手协调，推动了灵巧操作的发展

Abstract: Achieving human-like dexterous manipulation through the collaboration of multi-fingered hands with robotic arms remains a longstanding challenge in robotics, primarily due to the scarcity of high-quality demonstrations and the complexity of high-dimensional action spaces. To address these challenges, we propose FAR-Dex, a hierarchical framework that integrates few-shot data augmentation with adaptive residual refinement to enable robust and precise arm-hand coordination in dexterous tasks. First, FAR-DexGen leverages the IsaacLab simulator to generate diverse and physically constrained trajectories from a few demonstrations, providing a data foundation for policy training. Second, FAR-DexRes introduces an adaptive residual module that refines policies by combining multi-step trajectory segments with observation features, thereby enhancing accuracy and robustness in manipulation scenarios. Experiments in both simulation and real-world demonstrate that FAR-Dex improves data quality by 13.4% and task success rates by 7% over state-of-the-art methods. It further achieves over 80% success in real-world tasks, enabling fine-grained dexterous manipulation with strong positional generalization.

</details>


### [153] [DepthCache: Depth-Guided Training-Free Visual Token Merging for Vision-Language-Action Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10469)
*Yuquan Li,Lianjie Ma,Han Ding,Lijun Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DepthCache：利用深度作为结构先验的免训练视觉令牌压缩框架，通过深度分区和时空差异化合并策略，在保持机器人操作性能的同时显著降低VLA模型推理延迟


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在通用机器人操作中面临高推理延迟瓶颈，主要源于大型语言骨干处理大量视觉令牌。现有方法采用均匀剪枝或合并策略，会损害机器人控制所需的空间推理能力。

Method: 1) 基于深度分区：将观测划分为深度区域，应用空间差异化合并比例，保留近场工作空间同时压缩远场背景；2) 时间冗余利用：在连续帧间分布合并过程，确保表示一致性同时减少每步计算；3) 运动自适应管道：基于末端执行器动力学优化辅助视图压缩；4) 无需模型修改，可泛化到多种VLA架构。

Result: 在LIBERO基准测试中，DepthCache实现最高1.28倍推理加速，三种VLA模型（pi_0.5、OpenVLA、GR00T）平均成功率下降小于1%。相比之下，剪枝和合并基线在相似压缩率下导致4-24%的性能下降。真实世界实验证明DepthCache在物理机械臂上实现更快任务吞吐和更灵敏的闭环控制。

Conclusion: DepthCache通过利用深度作为结构先验进行视觉令牌压缩，有效解决了VLA模型推理延迟问题，在保持机器人操作性能的同时显著提升效率，为延迟敏感场景提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models enable generalist robotic manipulation but suffer from high inference latency. This bottleneck stems from the massive number of visual tokens processed by large language backbones. Existing methods either prune or merge tokens uniformly, degrading the spatial reasoning essential for robotic control. We present DepthCache, a training-free framework that leverages depth as a structural prior for visual token compression. It partitions observations into depth-based regions and applies spatially differentiated merge ratios, preserving the near-field workspace while compressing the distant background. To exploit temporal redundancy, DepthCache distributes the merging process across consecutive frames, ensuring consistent representations while reducing per-step computation. A motion-adaptive pipeline further optimizes auxiliary view compression based on end-effector dynamics. The framework requires no model modification, generalizing across diverse VLA architectures. On the LIBERO benchmark, DepthCache achieves up to 1.28x inference speedup with less than 1% average success rate degradation across three VLA models (pi_0.5, OpenVLA, GR00T), whereas pruning and merging baselines incur 4--24% degradation at comparable compression. Real-world experiments on a physical manipulator demonstrate that DepthCache enables faster task throughput and more responsive closed-loop control in latency-sensitive scenarios.

</details>


### [154] [BinWalker: Development and Field Evaluation of a Quadruped Manipulator Platform for Sustainable Litter Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10529)
*Giulio Turrisi,Angelo Bratta,Giovanni Minelli,Gabriel Fischer Abati,Amir H. Rad,João Carlos Virgolino Soares,Claudio Semini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 四足机器人系统用于在复杂户外环境中自主收集垃圾，结合了腿式移动、机械臂操作和感知能力，旨在解决传统人工清理效率低、成本高的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 垃圾污染是全球性环境问题，特别是在地形复杂、难以进入的区域。目前垃圾收集主要依赖人工，劳动强度大、效率低、成本高。机器人技术有潜力支持并部分自动化环境清理任务，但传统机器人平台难以在复杂地形中工作。

Method: 开发了一种四足机器人系统，结合腿式移动的优势（适应不平坦地形）和由机械臂与车载垃圾容器组成的操作系统。系统集成了感知、移动和操作功能，能够在复杂户外环境中自主检测、抓取和存储垃圾物品。

Result: 在户外场景中的实验评估表明，该方法有效展示了腿式机器人平台在环境清理任务中的可行性。系统能够在传统机器人平台难以到达的环境中工作，具有协助大规模垃圾清除操作的潜力。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了将感知、移动和操作集成到腿式机器人平台上进行环境清理任务的可行性。四足机器人系统为解决复杂地形中的垃圾收集问题提供了有前景的解决方案，代码已开源供进一步研究使用。

Abstract: Litter pollution represents a growing environmental problem affecting natural and urban ecosystems worldwide. Waste discarded in public spaces often accumulates in areas that are difficult to access, such as uneven terrains, coastal environments, parks, and roadside vegetation. Over time, these materials degrade and release harmful substances, including toxic chemicals and microplastics, which can contaminate soil and water and pose serious threats to wildlife and human health. Despite increasing awareness of the problem, litter collection is still largely performed manually by human operators, making large-scale cleanup operations labor-intensive, time-consuming, and costly. Robotic solutions have the potential to support and partially automate environmental cleanup tasks.
